# PROJECT CONTEXT — Pay Lottery

> Memory / handoff file. **Read this first** when resuming in a new Cline/GLM session.
> For moving the project to another machine, see **`HANDOFF.md`**.
> Last updated: 2026-10-29

## What this is
On-chain Solana token lottery ("Pay"). Holders of a token become eligible;
a **drand beacon** provides randomness that drives a **Fisher–Yates shuffle**
to pick winners; payouts are sent on-chain in SOL. **Provably-fair**: anyone
can re-derive the winners in-browser and cross-check the drand beacon value.

## File map

### Routes — `src/app`
| Path | File | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `/` | `page.tsx` | Home. Own sidebar (NOT AppShell). Sections: **hero card** (`h1` pitch + all-time proof stats — `Draws completed` / `Paid out to holders` from `/api/stats` — + CTAs and a plain `drand.sh ↗` link), **live strip** (`#draw`: 3 promo tiles in `lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr_1fr]` — prize pool (wide, inline `PoolArt` SVG + `.pool-sheen` animated gradient + winners/split & est. 1st-place strip), **middle** prize split (per rank: a 40px row — 28px metal-gradient medal carrying `1st` capping a chunky 12px `ProgressBar size="lg"` recessed track, with the payout + share in `text-xs` body font (no mono, same size as the eligibility footnote, 2px of air above the track) stacked over the track's right end, gradient fill + a `split-shine` sweep on the gold bar, header chip showing `≈ X SOL` of the split in the body font (no mono, `tabular-nums` kept) with a HeroUI `Tooltip` (`Tooltip.Trigger` = flex wrapper, non-focusable, styled dark bubble with the "net of the platform fee" copy) on hover, share from `firstPrizeSol / distribution[0]`, half-opacity `pool-sheen` wash behind the content, eligibility count + `/eligibility` link in the footer), **right** next-draw countdown (`fmtCountdown` + interval progress bar + a two-word `Next 03:00 UTC` footer, cadence in the tooltip)), collapsible **Recent draws** rows (`<RecentDraws/>`: #N · Pump.fun → Pay → drand → winner avatars · pool · Details ⌄, expanding into per-draw detail, paged 5 per page with Prev/Next) directly under the strip, then how-it-works animated flow (`#how`: Pump.fun → Pay engine → drand → Winners on one travelling dot, then one dot per winner — `FlowDot` driven by the single rAF timeline, no SMIL). All nav entries are real route links (Draws → `/draws`, no in-page toggle); the live-jackpot pill lives in `AppShell` (other routes) and is intentionally absent on `/` so the pool isn't shown twice above the fold. |
| `/draws` | `draws/page.tsx` | Renders `<DrawsList />` inside `<AppShell>`. |
| `/rounds/[n]` | `rounds/[roundNumber]/page.tsx` | Draw detail: summary (closed time, gross pool, holders, beacon round), winners + payout links, three in-browser checks (SHA-256, drand compare, `reproduceWinners()`), saved wallet list + exclusion counts + wallet lookup, saved randomness/signature, beacon chain. |
| `/verify` | `verify/page.tsx` (+ `layout.tsx` metadata, `SeedToWinner.tsx`) | Standalone verify tool — pick a draw, two beacon checks in the browser (SHA-256 of the saved signature · drand published that round) plus a live winner re-run; draws without a saved randomness show **"Nothing to check for this draw"**, and skipped/failed rows use `skipReason()` instead of the stored error text. |
| `/how-it-works` | `how-it-works/page.tsx` | Transparency reference — draw lifecycle, provably-fair, eligibility, payouts, FAQ + "View draws" CTA. AppShell. Copy tracks `engine.ts`: minimum hold + real exclusions, address-sorted shuffle, 50/30/20 default with unclaimed shares to #1, fee only reaches a treasury when one is configured, `MAX_PRIZE_SOL` / `MIN_PRIZE_SOL` skips, and Paid only after confirmation. Verify section separates the drand check from the saved-list re-run; FAQ says three-by-default winners and admits the exclusion filter. |
| `/holders` `/eligibility` `/stats` `/live` `/admin` | respective `page.tsx` | Each uses AppShell. |
| `/stats` | `stats/page.tsx` (+ `layout.tsx` metadata) | Client-rendered from `/api/stats` (all-time aggregates over every round, 30 UTC-day buckets, leaderboard top 8). Labels follow the API: `prizeSol` / `biggestPrize` are prize **pools** (fee included), `paidSol` is SOL actually sent; the chart's fee dot sums the window's `daily.feeSol`, not the all-time total. A failed fetch or `dbError` shows a neutral banner and suppresses the "no draw yet" sentence. |

| `/treasury` | `treasury/page.tsx` (+ `layout.tsx` title/description) | Public money page: platform + fee wallet cards (live balances, Solscan + copy), per-wallet tx tables (Geist Sans, 12px like the buyback ledger, 5 rows/page), Buyback &amp; burn card, "How the money moves" policy card. Client-rendered off `/api/wallets` + `/api/rounds`; buyback figures come from `/api/buyback` (the JSON ledger `data/buyback.json`, edited in Admin → Buyback). Every figure degrades to a sentence (or a dash) rather than a confident `0` when a feed fails (`PolicyCard.available`, `WalletCard` notes, `BuybackCard.unavailable`, `fee.ledgerAvailable`). `/stats` keeps only a 3-stat strip linking here. |

### API — `src/app/api`
- `status/route.ts` → `/api/status` (prize pool, eligible count, latest round, network)
- `wallets/route.ts` → `/api/wallets` (public treasury snapshot, consumed by `/treasury` and the `/stats` summary strip: platform + fee-wallet address/live balance, `reserveSol`, `feeBps`, cumulative fee ledger Σ`platformFeeLamports` + sweep count, `generatedAt`, joined `error`; 15s in-process cache, fail-soft per field, no secrets)
- `rounds/route.ts` → list rounds
- `rounds/[roundNumber]/route.ts` → round detail incl. eligible pool + `eligibleSnapshotCapturedAt` + `eligibleSource` (recorded/inferred/unavailable)
- `holders/route.ts` → public holder list; each row carries `excludedBy` (`"venue"` | `"manual"`) + `excludedLabel` (venue name or the organiser's label, rendered as a chip). `eligibility/route.ts` → single-wallet check: balance vs. the minimum **and** the draw's own exclusion sources (`getVenueAccountMap` + `excludedWallet.findUnique`), returning `excluded` / `excludedBy` / `excludedLabel` / `exclusionReason` plus `drawEligible` (= clears the minimum AND has an entry; `eligible` stays balance-only).
- `admin/exclusions/route.ts` → admin-guarded GET/POST/DELETE for the draw exclusion list (GET returns the manual list + auto `venue` accounts — each with `tag` + a one-line `reason` — + off-curve `detected` holder suggestions).
- `favicon/route.ts` → `/api/favicon`: decodes the admin-uploaded favicon data URI and serves it with the stored MIME type (404 when unset).
- `admin/telegram/route.ts` → admin-guarded GET/PUT/POST for the Telegram **group bot**. GET returns the config with the token **masked** (`tokenSource`: `db` | `env` | `none`, `tokenHint`), runtime state, plain-text previews of both messages, next-draw time, `siteUrl` and the recent send log; PUT validates + persists (`errors` keyed `chatId` / `threadId` / `intervalMinutes` / `token`, token omitted = keep saved, `clearToken: true` = forget it); POST `{ action: "test" | "round" | "lastDraw" | "chats" }` sends a test / the current round / the last completed draw, or lists chats from `getUpdates` so the admin can click the right chat id. The real bot lives in `lib/telegram-bot.ts`.
- `admin/seo/route.ts` → admin-guarded GET/POST/DELETE for the SEO overrides.
- `admin/palette/route.ts` → admin-guarded GET (token catalogue with labels/hints/groups, `defaults`, effective `palette`), POST `{ palette: { light, dark } }` (drops values equal to the default, rejects unknown tokens / non-hex with **400** + `invalid`, empty palette deletes the row, then `revalidatePath("/", "layout")`), DELETE (clear all → defaults; same revalidate).
- `admin/creator-fees/route.ts` → admin-guarded GET: claimable creator fees (pumpdev), shareholder split, platform-wallet balance, auto-claim config, `costs` (fee/rent/net math), last 8 claims. `admin/creator-fees/claim/route.ts` → POST "Claim now" (build → sign → send → confirm, 90s `maxDuration`). `admin/creator-fees/auto/route.ts` → POST `{ enabled }`, flips the persisted `CLAIM_AUTO_ENABLED` AdminSetting row the cron reads each tick.
- `buyback/route.ts` → `/api/buyback`: public GET (ledger + derived `summary`), admin-guarded PUT that rewrites `data/buyback.json`. Validation failures → **400** with `errors` keyed `txs.<i>.<field>`; read/write failures → 500 + plain message.
- `notifications/subscribe/route.ts` → push sub upsert/delete (`notificationSubscription` model)

### Components — `src/components`
- **`RecentDraws.tsx`** — homepage settled-draws list: collapsible rows (`pool + #N · Pump.fun → Pay → drand → rank circles · age · ⌄`) expanding into stats + the **full drand block** (`<DrandBeacon … full verified />`: 64-char randomness + BLS signature wrapped in full, chain label, `Verified` chip, `Verify draw`) + winners. `rankBadge()`, `explorer()`, `WinnerBadges` (tooltip triggers). Rows are paged **5 at a time** (`PAGE_SIZE = 5`) with a HeroUI `Pagination` (Prev · “Page N / M” · Next, disabled at the bounds) plus a “Showing 1–5 of 12 draws” caption; paging is client-side over the ≤50 rounds `/` already receives, the current page is clamped (never stored past the end) so a shrinking list can’t blank the card, and `/draws` stays the full archive.
- **`DrawsList.tsx`** — draws list UI. Header is a **4-tile summary** (Draws · Paid out · Draw interval · Latest draw; the old `Winners` tile is gone), sourced from the newest 50 settled rounds: **Paid out counts only winners with a `payoutTx`**, and a footer line states the 50-round cap. Each card stays lean — **no fee-breakdown line** (a derived `PayoutNote` was tried and removed on 2026-10-25). The list **auto-refreshes every 15 s** (`fetchRounds(silent)` — background failures keep the last good snapshot, skipped while the tab is hidden). The inline verify UI moved to `DrandBeacon.tsx`; this file now renders `<DrandBeacon … />` inside each expanded card. Contains `rankBadge()`, `shortAddr`/`fmtTime` helpers, `explorer()` usage.
- **`DrandBeacon.tsx`** — shared drand evidence block + `VerifyButton` (beacon match against drand.sh + browser-side winner re-derivation via `reproduceWinners`). One implementation for `/` and `/draws`; `full` wraps the hex instead of ellipsising it, `verified` adds the DB-status chip, `chain` supplies the label and the drand.sh link. `HashRow` (copyable) lives here too.
- **`ExclusionLabelChip.tsx`** — how an exclusion reason renders, shared by `/admin` and the public holder table: `EXCLUSION_LABEL_PRESETS` (`Split wallet` / `Sybil cluster` / `Bot / sniper` / `Team` / `Treasury`, each with lucide icon, chip colour + one-line hint), `matchExclusionPreset()`, `ExclusionLabelChip` (preset chip → `Tag` chip for free text → plain `Excluded` when unlabelled) and `VenueChip` (amber `Droplets` chip for the bonding curve / pool authority).
- `AppShell.tsx` — sidebar shell for non-home pages (consumes `NAV`).
- **`BrandMark.tsx`** — sidebar brand mark: `<img src="/drawfi_logo.png">` (28×28, `object-contain`) with an `onError` fallback to the old tinted initial; the file itself is in the repo since 26 Okt (400×400), so the fallback is only a safety net for a renamed upload. Source path lives in one constant (`BRAND_LOGO_SRC`).
- **`nav.ts`** — sidebar nav config. Single source of truth, consumed by `page.tsx` + `AppShell.tsx`.
- `ui.tsx` — `Icon` set + `ThemeToggle` + primitives.
- `NotifySubscribe.tsx` — notification subscription UI (currently unused — removed from /how-it-works; pending rebuild as group-info bot).
- `providers/Providers.tsx`, `providers/WalletProvider.tsx`, `providers/SiteProvider.tsx` (`useSite()` brand context fed by the root layout — `brandName`, `tokenName`, `siteTitle`, `description`).
- **`admin/PalettePanel.tsx`** — `/admin` → Palette tab: every HeroUI color token (31) × light/dark, grouped (Brand & status / Page & surfaces / Lines & chrome) with a search box, native color swatch + hex field + per-row `Reset`, `Save palette` (POST) and `Reset to defaults` (DELETE, confirm). Edits preview instantly through a `<style id="palette-preview">` it appends to `<body>` while mounted (full effective palette, so it also neutralises a stale server block) and removes on unmount; dirty-count chip, `Discard changes`, and inline error text for 400 responses. **Deliberately no `router.refresh()`** after save/reset — on the statically cached `/admin` it re-rendered the route, wiping the notice and panel state; the API's `revalidatePath` already refreshes every other route.
- **`admin/SeoPanel.tsx`** — `/admin` → SEO & Analytics tab (brand, title, description, keywords, site URL, OG image, Twitter handle, Google verification, indexing, GA4 ID, **favicon upload** with 32 px/16 px previews and remove) + SERP preview.
- **`admin/TelegramBotPanel.tsx`** — `/admin` → **Telegram Bot** tab (after Buyback): connection (write-only token, group chat id, optional forum topic id, interval minutes + “Find chat id” via `getUpdates`), three `Switch`es (bot enabled / round updates / draw results), actions (Save, Send test, Post round now, Resend last draw, Remove saved token), live plain-text previews of **both** messages, the persisted last-send/last-error state, and the recent send log. It never receives the token in full. The **message templates** are editable in the same tab: two `TextField` + `TextArea` blocks (round / draw) with placeholder chips, a 2000-char counter and `Reset to default`, plus a **Preview edits** button (`POST /api/admin/telegram` with `action: "preview"`) that shows the server-rendered result next to the saved one and an “unsaved edits” chip; unknown placeholders come back as per-field errors.
- **`admin/CacheCard.tsx`** — `/admin` → Settings column (between `SettingsSummary` and `DangerZoneCard`): lists up to 12 cached keys with their age (`fmtAge`), **Refresh** re-reads `GET /api/admin/cache`, **Clear cache** posts to the same route and then runs the page's `refreshAll()` so the panels above re-fetch. The route (`/api/admin/cache`) is admin-guarded, logs the prefix it dropped, and returns `{ ok, cleared, remaining }`; `clearCache(prefix?)` accepts `"admin:"` / `"rounds"` to narrow a clear.
- **`admin/BuybackPanel.tsx`** — `/admin` → Buyback tab: edits `data/buyback.json` through `PUT /api/buyback` (fee share, supply, next run + add/remove **buy**/**burn** rows with signature, `datetime-local`, tokens, SOL). Shows a live preview of the totals `/treasury` will derive; per-field server errors land under the failing input.
- **`admin/CreatorFeesCard.tsx`** — first card on `/admin` → App Info (above `LiveStatusCard`): big claimable SOL + curve/pumpSwap split, a **4-stat cost strip** (claim fee `5,000 + priority`, min wallet balance `fee + rent`, net if claimed, signing-wallet balance with headroom), payout/signer line (graduation + shareholder chips, warning when the on-chain recipient is not the platform wallet), `Claim now`, `Auto-claim: ON/OFF`, and **`Claim history` as a table** (`When · Amount · Source · Status · Note · Tx`, last 8 DB rows, error text clamped to 2 lines with the full message in `title`, row hover, `overflow-x-auto` on narrow screens). Polls `GET /api/admin/creator-fees` every 60s.

### Lib — `src/lib`
- `lottery/engine.ts` — draw engine: `claimRoundSlot()` (advisory-locked atomic round-number claim + stale-`drawing` recovery + 30s duplicate-trigger guard), fetch eligible, drand, Fisher–Yates shuffle, payout, writes round + winners + `eligibleSnapshotCapturedAt`.
- **`lottery/verify.ts`** — `reproduceWinners(randomValueHex, eligibleAddresses, count)` → `ReproducedWinner[]`. Web Crypto SHA-256 + Fisher–Yates, browser-safe.
- `solana/randomness.ts` — drand fetch (cross-relay).
- `solana/holders.ts` — holder snapshot. `solana/connection.ts` — RPC.
- `notifications.ts` — send notifications to winners (queries `notificationSubscription`).
- **`telegram-bot.ts`** — the group bot. Settings in `AdminSetting` (`TELEGRAM_BOT_CONFIG` JSON + `TELEGRAM_BOT_STATE` JSON → **no migration**): `enabled`, `chatId`, `threadId`, `intervalMinutes` (1–1440, default **2**), `notifyRound`, `notifyDraw`, plus a write-only `token` (DB wins, `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` is the env fallback). `runBotTick()` is called once a minute from `scheduler.ts` and enforces the interval against the persisted `lastRoundSentAt` (so a panel change needs no restart); `notifyDrawResult()` is called by `engine.ts` right after a round is persisted `completed` and de-dupes by `lastDrawRound`. Messages are Telegram **HTML** (escaped) — current round = pool / eligible / est. 1st prize / countdown (or “draws are paused”) / site link; draw = ranked winners + payouts, beacon status, payout tx, `/rounds/<n>` link. Also `sendTestMessage()`, `sendRoundUpdate()`, `sendLastDrawNow()`, `discoverChats()` (getUpdates), `normalizeBotInput()` and `getBotOverview()` for the panel. Errors are redacted (the token never reaches a log line); every failure is swallowed after logging so a Telegram/DB/RPC hiccup can never break a draw. Both message bodies are **admin-editable templates** (2026-10-25): `roundTemplate` / `drawTemplate` live in the same JSON, `roundValues()` / `drawValues()` feed `renderRoundHtml()` / `renderDrawHtml()`, `buildPreviews()` backs `getBotOverview().preview`, and the exported `previewBotTemplates()` serves the panel's `action: "preview"` call — same code path as a real send, so a preview cannot drift from what the group receives. `assertMessageLength()` stops a rendered message that would exceed Telegram's `TELEGRAM_MAX_CHARS` (4096) instead of letting the API 400.
- **`telegram-templates.ts`** — deliberately **pure** (no Prisma / Solana / `process.env`) so the admin panel imports it without dragging the server stack into the client bundle: `DEFAULT_ROUND_TEMPLATE` / `DEFAULT_DRAW_TEMPLATE` (the single source of truth — `telegram-bot.ts` re-exports nothing of its own), `ROUND_PLACEHOLDERS` / `DRAW_PLACEHOLDERS` + `*_PLACEHOLDER_HELP`, `MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH = 2000`, `placeholdersIn()`, `validateTemplate()` (empty / too long / `{typo}` → one message) and `applyTemplate()` (CRLF→LF, trims trailing spaces, collapses 3+ newlines to 2 so a blank optional value leaves no gap).
- **`demo-finance.ts`** — the one remaining placeholder on `/treasury`: `DEMO_FEE_TXS` (shown only when no draw has been paid yet). `DEMO_PLATFORM_TXS`, `DEMO_BUYBACK` and `DEMO_BUYBACK_TXS` are gone — both tx tables come from the draw ledger, and the buyback numbers now come from the JSON ledger. Fixed ISO timestamps so SSR/hydration agree; every consumer must render a visible **Sample** label and never mix sample rows with real ones.
- **`buyback.ts`** — buyback ledger types (`BuybackData` / `BuybackTx`, tokens always positive, `kind` supplies the sign), `EMPTY_BUYBACK`, `normalizeBuyback()` (validation → `errors` keyed `txs.<i>.<field>`, base58 signature regex, duplicate detection, newest-first sort, ≤500 rows) and `summarizeBuyback()` (pure: bought / burned / burnedPct / spentSol / runs / lastAt). **Client-safe** — no fs.
- **`buyback-store.ts`** — server-only fs side of the ledger: `readBuyback()` (missing file → `EMPTY_BUYBACK`, malformed JSON / failed validation → throws a message naming the bad keys) and `writeBuyback()` (mkdir + temp file + rename, stamps `updatedAt`, maps `EROFS`/`EACCES` to a "read-only deployment" message). Path = `<cwd>/data/buyback.json`, the hand-edited source of truth — no Prisma model, no migration.
- **`net-ipv4.ts`** — `preferIpv4()`: `dns.setDefaultResultOrder("ipv4first")` + `net.setDefaultAutoSelectFamily(false)`, called from `src/instrumentation.ts` before the scheduler starts (production `ETIMEDOUT` fix — see the newest Done bullet; `NET_PREFER_IPV4=0` disables it).
- **`cache.ts`** — server-only read-path cache for the GET endpoints: `cached(key, ttl|{ttlMs,staleMs}, load)` returns a fresh hit instantly, serves a stale value **while refreshing it in the background** (stale-while-revalidate, default stale window = 4×ttl) and dedupes concurrent loads onto one promise. `clearCache(prefix?)` drops everything/prefix — the mutation routes (draw, holder sync, creator-fee claim + auto toggle, exclusions add/remove, reset-data) call it so the admin never reads a pre-action payload. In-memory, per process: survives requests, resets on restart/deploy (warm.ts refills it). The `store` / `inflight` maps live on **`globalThis`** (`__payCacheStore` / `__payCacheInflight`) — Next gives every route its own module instance, so a module-scoped `Map` let `POST /api/admin/cache` clear a different store from the one `/api/status` had filled. Admin surface: `/api/admin/cache` (GET lists keys + `ageMs`, POST clears) + `admin/CacheCard.tsx` on `/admin`.
- **`warm.ts`** — `warmApiCache()` (production only, called at the end of `src/instrumentation.ts`): self-fetches `/api/status`, `/api/rounds`, `/api/stats` ~1.5 s after boot (up to 4 attempts, 1 s apart) so the first visitor gets cache hits instead of a cold Neon handshake + 3–8 queries. Logs `[warm] <path> 200 in … ms`; fire-and-forget, never blocks startup.

- **`pumpfun/creator-fees.ts`** — server-only pump.fun creator-fee client: `fetchCreatorFeeSnapshot()` (GET pumpdev `/api/claim-account`, 15s timeout, SOL→lamport strings, shareholder split, `payoutAddress` = highest share), `getCreatorFeeStatus()` (never throws — snapshot + signing-wallet balance + auto config + **`costs`** + last 5 claims, mainnet-only guard), `claimCreatorFees({ source, minLamports })` (in-process single-flight lock, fresh pre-check, refuses when the pot **≤ the claim fee** or the wallet can't cover `fee + rent-exempt floor`, POSTs `/api/claim-distribute` when `hasFeeSharing` else `/api/claim-account`, deserializes the binary `VersionedTransaction`, signs, sends, polls `getSignatureStatuses` for 60s, writes a `CreatorFeeClaim` row pending → confirmed/failed), and `getAutoClaimConfig`/`setAutoClaimEnabled` (AdminSetting `CLAIM_AUTO_ENABLED` over env). Cost math lives here too: `claimFeeLamports(priorityFeeSol)` = `5,000 + priority`, `getRentExemptLamports()` (cached 1h) and `explainSendError()` (turns `insufficient funds for rent` into a seed-the-wallet message).
- **`exclusions.ts`** — server-only draw exclusion set ("blacklist"). `getDrawExclusions(platformAddress)` = platform wallet + **venue accounts** (`getVenueAccounts()`: the `bonding-curve` PDA on `6EF8r…F6P` and the `pool-authority` PDA on `pAMMB…FXEA`, both derived from `TOKEN_MINT`, so they follow the token through graduation; each entry carries `kind` / `tag` / `label` / `reason`) + the admin list (`ExcludedWallet` table). `getVenueAccountMap()` + `describeExclusion(address, venueMap, manualLabels?)` (`{ excludedBy: "venue" | "manual", excludedLabel }`) attribute a holder row to its exclusion source — venue names and the organiser's label are both shown publicly (chip wording/colour lives in `src/components/ExclusionLabelChip.tsx`). `addExcludedWallet` / `removeExcludedWallet` / `listExcludedWallets` (base58-validated, deduped, ≤500 rows, label ≤80), `isProgramOwnedAccount()` (off-curve PDA heuristic for the admin "detected" suggestions), `mergeExclusions()` (dedupe+sort). **Every** eligible-count surface must call `getDrawExclusions`, or the count drifts from the draw.
- `config.ts`, `prisma.ts`, `format.ts` (`shortAddress`, `explorerUrl`), `scheduler.ts` (holder-sync + draw + creator-fee claim crons, plus the every-minute Telegram bot tick that self-gates on the DB config), `cron-next.ts`, `sync.ts`, `auth.ts`, `admin-guard.ts`, `use-theme.ts`, `log-buffer.ts`.
- **`settings.ts`** — server-only, **env-only** settings registry for the 10 tunable keys (`MIN_TOKEN_ELIGIBLE`, `WINNER_COUNT`, `PAYOUT_DISTRIBUTION`, `PRIZE_RESERVE_LAMPORTS`, `PLATFORM_FEE_BPS`, `MAX_PRIZE_SOL`, `MIN_PRIZE_SOL`, `LOTTERY_CRON`, `HOLDER_SYNC_CRON`, `LOTTERY_CRON_ENABLED`): `SETTING_KEYS` / `SETTING_META` / `SETTING_DEFAULTS`, `envDefault()` / `effectiveValue()` / `effectiveMinEligibleBigint()`, `validateSetting` / `validateSettingsTogether`, and `getSettingsView()` (values + `source: "env" | "default"` + `problem`) for the read-only `/admin` → Settings summary. No Prisma, no overrides.
- **`palette.ts`** — server-only theme palette store: `PaletteToken` (token/label/hint/group/light/dark) for the **31** HeroUI color tokens in 3 groups, `PALETTE_DEFAULTS` (sRGB hex equivalents of the `globals.css` oklch values), `sanitizePalette()` (unknown token or non-hex → `invalid`, values equal to the default dropped, so "empty" means "all default"), `resolvePalette()`, `savePalette()` / `clearPalette()`, and cached `getPaletteOverrides()` / `getPalette()` / `getPaletteCss()`. Stored as one JSON row under `AdminSetting` key `THEME_PALETTE` — **no migration**; clearing deletes the row, so `globals.css` stays the source of truth. Fail-soft: no DB → defaults. Re-exports the pure helpers from `palette-css.ts`.
- **`palette-css.ts`** — client-safe (no DB / Node APIs) palette math shared by the server store and the admin panel: `normalizeHex()` (`#ABC` → `#aabbcc`, anything else → `null`), `paletteCss()` (sparse overrides → `html[data-theme="light"|"dark"]` blocks, `""` when empty; every value re-validated so a hand-edited row can't inject CSS), `diffPalette()` and `countOverrides()`.
- **`seo.ts`** — server-only `SiteSeo` type + defaults (env fallbacks), cached `getSiteSeo()`, `saveSeo`/`clearSeo`/`validateSeo`; persisted as `SEO_*` keys in `AdminSetting`. Owns the token ticker as well: `SEO_KEYS.tokenName` (`TOKEN_NAME`, deliberately not `SEO_`-prefixed), `getTokenName()`, `normalizeTokenName()` (case-preserving) + `TOKEN_NAME_RE`. Also owns favicon rules: `FAVICON_MAX_BYTES` (128 KB), `FAVICON_MIME_TYPES`, `parseFavicon()` (validates the `data:` URI, base64 payload and decoded size; returns `null` on anything bad).

Config proses di root: **`ecosystem.config.js`** (PM2, fork/1 instance, `npm run start`).

- **📌 `prisma:error` di `/api/rounds`: `$queryRaw` tidak bisa membawa parameter `BigInt` (2026-10-29).** Gejala di log VPS: `Invalid value for argument parameters: Could not convert from JSON bigint value to PrismaValue. Expected Flat JSON array (no nesting)`. **Sebab:** `src/app/api/stats/route.ts` → `loadHolderHistory()` mengirim `threshold` (hasil `effectiveMinEligibleBigint()`, sebuah **JS `BigInt`**) ke `${…}` di `$queryRaw`; Prisma menyerialkannya sebagai `{ prisma__type: "bigint", … }` dan mesin menolak seluruh panggilan. Karena fungsi itu punya `catch { return null }` yang sengaja fallback ke seri per-draw, **kegagalannya tidak terlihat** — efeknya hanya `/stats` selalu memakai `daily` alih-alih `holdersDaily`. **Diukur ke Neon (read-only):** param `BigInt` → **ERROR** (pesan identik VPS); param **string** + `${threshold}::numeric` → **OK**; query `daycap` penuh dengan string → **OK**. **Perbaikan:** threshold dikirim sebagai **string** dan di-cast **`::numeric`** (bukan `bigint` — `numeric` satu-satunya tipe yang memuat saldo raw 18-desimal), plus `console.warn` di `catch` supaya kegagalan senyap berikutnya terdengar. **Bukti:** `/api/stats` HTTP **200** dengan `holdersDaily = 2026-10-29 eligible=24 total=24` (dulu selalu `null`), 0 `prisma:error` baru di `.dev.log`. Raw SQL lain aman (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(4711001)` pakai number; `SELECT 1` tanpa param). Catatan: `.env` `MIN_TOKEN_ELIGIBLE=1e24` = **1.000.000 token**; row DB-nya masih `1e18` tapi tidak dipakai. `npm run typecheck` **0 error**, build **✓ 6,8s / TS 4,0s / 12/12** (BUILD_ID `vyiQ3KFg0Xb-ySW09ugp5`).

- **📌 `npx tsc --noEmit` OOM di VPS — bukan mesinnya, tapi heap default Node (2026-10-29).** Laporan VPS: `FATAL ERROR: Reached heap limit … Scavenge 473.5 (486.4) -> 472.0 (486.7) MB`. Angka **473 MB** itu tepat di bawah cap heap default V8 **~512 MB** (V8 menetapkan `--max-old-space-size` dari RAM yang terlihat; VPS 1–2 GB → cap ~512 MB), sementara proyek ini butuh lebih. **Diukur, bukan ditebak:** cold run tanpa `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` dengan `Start-Process -PassThru` + `PeakWorkingSet64` → `npx tsc --noEmit` selesai **8,1 s** dengan puncak **812,7 MB**; `--max-old-space-size=300` **OOM persis mengulang error VPS** (`Ineffective mark-compacts near heap limit`) — jadi cap 300 MB di mesin 24 GB ini pun OOM, bukan soal RAM mesin. **Perbaikan:** script baru **`npm run typecheck`** = `node --max-old-space-size=2048 node_modules/typescript/lib/tsc.js --noEmit` (menunjuk `typescript/lib/tsc.js` supaya flag sampai ke proses TS, bukan pembungkus npx; jalan sama di Windows & Linux) — diuji cold: **0 error**, tanpa OOM, `tsconfig.tsbuildinfo` terbentuk. Didokumentasikan di `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §1 (beda **kernel OOM `Killed`** vs **heap Node OOM**, `free -h`, `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=2048 npm run build` untuk type-check di dalam build) dan `.clinerules` §Commands (perintah type-check harian ganti ke `npm run typecheck`; aturan lama menulis "no dedicated script"). **Tidak ada perubahan kode aplikasi.** Catatan: yang berat hanya **cold run** pertama setelah upload, karena `*.tsbuildinfo` di-`.gitignore`.

- **📌 Gas EVM kini diatur dari `.env` + 2 bug ikutan (2026-10-29).** Angka terukur di chain: `eth_gasPrice` **0,020686 gwei**, `baseFeePerGas` **0,020584 gwei**, tip saran **0**, tx uji `effectiveGasPrice` 0,0208/0,0210 gwei dengan `gasUsed 21000` → **1 transfer ≈ 0,00000044 ETH**. `.env` sekarang punya blok gas: `PAYOUT_GAS_PRICE_GWEI=0.05` (CAP, bukan harga bayar — dengan tip 0 wallet hanya dibebani base fee, jadi cap besar gratis), `PAYOUT_PRIORITY_FEE_GWEI=0`, `PAYOUT_GAS_LIMIT=21000`, `PAYOUT_GAS_LIMIT_CONTRACT=80000`, `PAYOUT_GAS_HEADROOM=2` (dipakai hanya kalau cap kosong) + aturan reserve. **Bug #1:** `envNum()` membaca komentar inline → `Number("0.05 # CAP …")` = NaN sehingga cap diabaikan; diperbaiki dengan `envRaw()` (aturan komentar inline ala dotenv) → probe membuktikan `maxFeePerGas = 50000000 wei = 0,05 gwei` dipakai. **Bug #2:** jalur sukses memakai `updateMany` gaya Solana sehingga **3 pemenang menyimpan hash yang sama**; sekarang engine menyimpan **hash per pemenang** dari `payout.txHashes` (urutan sama dengan `amounts`), `updateMany` hanya untuk jalur Solana. **Bukti payout EVM pertama sukses (round #5):** DB `#5 completed pool=127163150043826 fee=6358157502191 payoutTx=0x21e5f111…ae741` (3 winner `sent`); on-chain 4 tx berurutan — treasury 0.000006358157502191 → rank1 0.000060402496270818 → rank2 0.000036241497762490 → rank3 0.000024160998508327, **cocok persis dengan DB**. `tsc` **0**, build hijau.
- **📌 Laporan pemilik: sweep fee ditolak `Missing or invalid parameters` — chain ini menolak tx LEGACY (2026-10-29).** Investigasi: raw tx owner saya decode — nonce/gasPrice/gas/to/value/`v=9362` (=4663×2+35+1, tanda tangan EIP-155 sah) semuanya benar, panjang RLP 107 → formatnya valid, tapi bertipe **legacy (type 0)**. Uji terkendali (2 tx 1 wei ke treasury sendiri): **legacy ❌** *"The fee cap (maxFeePerGas gwei) cannot be lower than the block base fee"* vs **EIP-1559 ✅** `status 0x1 type 0x2`; konsisten dengan tx nyata di chain ini (bot pons: `type 2`, `priority_fee 0`). Perbaikan: `sendPayouts` kini mengirim **EIP-1559** — `maxFeePerGas = gasPrice × PAYOUT_GAS_HEADROOM` (**default dinaikkan ke 2** karena ia CAP, bukan harga bayar), `maxPriorityFeePerGas = PAYOUT_PRIORITY_FEE_GWEI` (default 0), pre-flight memakai `maxFeePerGas`, dan `PAYOUT_GAS_PRICE_GWEI` kini berarti fee-cap tetap. **Bukti jalur produksi:** `getChain().sendPayouts([1 wei ke treasury])` → `0x14a356a8…56fc`, receipt `status 0x1`, **`type 0x2`**, `gasUsed 0x5208`, `effectiveGasPrice 0x13ddf00` (fee 437.472.000.000 wei). `tsc` **0**, build hijau.
- **📌 Laporan pemilik: payout EVM `insufficient funds` (gagal separuh) — FIXED (2026-10-29).** Diagnosis: **gas tidak dianggarkan** dalam pool (reserve owner 0,1 gwei padahal 4 tx butuh ~2,5e12 wei @0,03 gwei) sehingga batch membayar fee + #1 + #2 lalu habis di #3; dan **DB kehilangan jejak** karena jalur error lama menghapus semua baris pemenang `pending` (round `failed`, nol baris, padahal uang keluar — bukti on-chain: **12 transfer / 5 batch**, tiga di antaranya terputus). Perbaikan: (1) **gas dari `.env`** — `PAYOUT_GAS_LIMIT=21000`, `PAYOUT_GAS_LIMIT_CONTRACT=80000`, `PAYOUT_GAS_HEADROOM=1.5`, `PAYOUT_GAS_PRICE_GWEI` opsional (sebelumnya hardcoded); (2) **pre-flight balance check** sebelum tx pertama — `butuh = Σvalue + Σ(gas×gasPrice)`, kalau saldo kurang → error berisi angka & **nol tx terkirim** (probe: butuh 16.190.433.500.561 vs saldo 13.754.557.500.562 → refused, `nonce before = after = 0xc`); (3) **payout separuh kini tercatat** — `sendPayouts` menempelkan `partialPayouts` pada error dan engine menandai pemenang itu `sent` + menyimpan `payoutTx`/fee, hanya menghapus baris yang belum terbayar; (4) **decimals token**: `TOKEN_DECIMALS` 6 → **18** (mc=18 dari `alchemy_getTokenMetadata`) + row DB `MIN_TOKEN_ELIGIBLE` `1e11` → **`1e18`** (1 token) agar "Min hold" tampil `1.0`. **Aturan baru:** `PRIZE_RESERVE_WEI` harus **≥ gas satu batch** (~2,5e12 wei) atau pre-flight menolak terus; `.env` diset `5000000000000` untuk tes. `tsc` **0**, build hijau.
- **📌 Laporan pemilik: `PLATFORM_TREASURY_PRIVATE_KEY` diisi tapi fee wallet tidak muncul — FIXED (2026-10-29).** Sebabnya key itu **tidak dibaca kode mana pun** (hanya ada di komentar; yang dibaca cuma `PLATFORM_TREASURY_SECRET` Solana) dan kartu fee sengaja disembunyikan di EVM (§12). Perbaikan penuh: **`ChainAdapter.treasuryAddress?()`** baru (Solana: `PLATFORM_TREASURY_SECRET`; EVM: turunan `PLATFORM_TREASURY_PRIVATE_KEY` via viem, null bila kosong) dipakai `/api/wallets` + `/api/admin/info`; dan **`sendPayouts(winners, feeTransfer?)`** — payout EVM kini **menyapu fee platform ke treasury sebagai transfer PERTAMA** (jalur EVM tidak atomik, jadi sweep gagal = tidak ada hadiah keluar), dengan log `+ fee sweep to 0x1234…`. - **📌 Bug satuan dari log draw Anda: cap hadiah dipotong jadi 1 gwei — FIXED (2026-10-29).** Log `[lottery] effective prize lamports: 0 (cap 1000000000)` membongkar `parseSolToLamports` yang mengalikan **×1e9 hardcoded**: `MAX_PRIZE_NATIVE=1` jadi **1 gwei** di chain 18-desimal (≈0 ETH), sehingga pool nyata akan selalu "habis" dipotong cap. Fix: konversi memakai `10n ** BigInt(NATIVE_DECIMALS)` (1e9 Solana / 1e18 EVM) dengan **matematika string→BigInt** (tanpa float). Jebakan kecil saat fix: `BASE_UNITS_PER_NATIVE` bertipe `number`, dikali `bigint` → tsc menolak, jadi faktornya dihitung sebagai bigint. **Bukti (probe dua mode, `.env` = `MAX_PRIZE_NATIVE=1`):** Solana → `MAX=1000000000` (1 SOL), reserve `1300000` (0.0013 SOL), RPC Helius; Robinhood → `MAX=1000000000000000000` (**1 ETH** ✅), reserve `50000000000000000` (0.05 ETH default), RPC Alchemy. Sekaligus pesan engine yang disimpan di `LotteryRound.error` + tampil di UI tak lagi menulis "SOL" keras (pakai `NATIVE_SYMBOL`), tanpa merusak pemeta `skipReason()` di UI. `tsc` **0**.

Catatan: belum ada cursor (tiap sync baca seluruh riwayat transfer).
- **📌 Bug laporan pemilik: 500 `trigger-sync` + `getMinimumBalanceForRentExemption is not available on the ROBINHOOD_MAINNET` — FIXED (2026-10-29).** Dua sebab: **(a)** efek samping §3 — `getConfig().rpcUrl` saya jadikan chain-aware, tapi `lib/solana/connection.ts` ikut memakainya → **klien Solana menunjuk RPC EVM**, jadi method Solana dikirim ke Alchemy (`getMinimumBalanceForRentExemption`, `getBalance`). Fix: **`config.solanaRpcUrl`** baru (selalu `SOLANA_RPC_URL`, boleh kosong di EVM); `getConnection()` memakai itu + **gagal dengan pesan jelas** kalau kosong; script Solana-only (`airdrop`/`checkPlatformWallet`/`setupDevnetToken`) pindah ke `cfg.solanaRpcUrl`; **`getCreatorFeeStatus()` + `claimCreatorFees()` keluar lebih awal di chain non-Solana** (fees = pump.fun/Solana) sehingga admin card menampilkan "Creator fees are pump.fun/Solana only — not available on Robinhood Chain (4663)" alih-alih RPC storm. **(b)** 500 di `/api/admin/trigger-sync` **bukan bug kode** tapi **Blockscout 403 untuk IP mesin ini** (2 fetch + jeda retry 4 s ≈ 4,4 s; direproduksi: `npm run holders:sync` → `indexer HTTP 403 … throttling this host` 7 s) — kode terbukti benar via stub indexer. Bonus konsistensi EVM: `/api/admin/info` + **`/api/wallets`** (halaman `/treasury`) sekarang ambil alamat+saldo platform lewat adapter (`chain=robinhood symbol=ETH addr=0x4f52…f3dd balance=0 feeConfigured=False error=[]`, sebelumnya `Config incomplete: bad secret key size`); treasury hanya ditampilkan di Solana. ⚠️ **Pelanggaran prosedur saya sendiri**: patch 3 script pakai `Get-Content -Raw | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` (dilarang `.clinerules`) → menambah **BOM** + merusak `—` jadi `â€”`; diperbaiki dengan **reversal byte cp1252→byte→UTF-8 via Node** (bukan retype) dan diverifikasi **BOM=False, mojibake=0, em dash utuh**. `tsc` **0**, build ✓ 7,9s / TS 5,5s / 12/12.
- **📌 BUG DILAPORKAN PEMILIK: "sync holder masih ngawur, nangkep yang sol" — FIXED (2026-10-29).** Gejala nyata: DB diisi batch **Solana** terus, uji Robinhood tertimbun. Sebab: `Holder`/`HolderSnapshot` **tidak punya dimensi chain** dan "batch terbaru" dihitung **global**, sementara dev server mode solana menulis batch tiap 5 menit (bukti: 5 batch terakhir semua `jenis=sol`, `CxQMJHPJ…`, n=1). Perbaikan: (1) **schema**: `Holder.chain` + `HolderSnapshot.chain` (`@default("solana")`, index `(chain)` & `(chain, capturedAt)`) lewat `migrate diff --script` → `db execute` (ADD COLUMN ber-DEFAULT → baris lama otomatis `solana`, nol data hilang); (2) `syncHolders` menstempel `chain: CHAIN_KIND`; (3) **17 titik baca** "batch terbaru" difilter per chain (`engine.getEligibleHolders` + daftar exclusion engine, `/api/status` (+count), `/api/holders`, `/api/eligibility` (3), `/api/admin/holders` (+count), `/api/admin/exclusions`, `/api/rounds/[n]` (2), `telegram-bot`). Dua jebakan codemod ketemu & dibereskan: repo **CRLF** (regex `\n` gagal senyap) dan `chain` mendarat di dalam objek `capturedAt: { lte: … }` (diperbaiki manual). **Bukti:** batch Solana `11:06:21` (n=1) lebih baru dari batch Robinhood `11:05:48` (n=12), tapi per-chain benar — `solana batch=1 CxQMJHPJ` vs `robinhood batch=12 0x0e8641, 0x000000, 0x356cc1`, sementara "global tanpa filter = chain solana n=1" (perilaku lama yang rusak). App: `/api/status` mode solana → `totalHolders=1`, `configured=true`. `tsc` **0**, build ✓ 11,7s / TS 6,7s / 12/12, BUILD_ID `KOxV65KZl8Z1icR20eBwh`. **Catatan:** `LotteryRound`/`Winner` belum punya kolom chain — draw di dua chain sekaligus akan bercampur di `/draws` + `/stats` (aman selama cron draw Solana `false`).

Yang membuat blok Solana tidak bisa dibuang ternyata bukan env-nya, tapi kode: **`getConfig()` mewajibkan** `SOLANA_RPC_URL`/`TOKEN_MINT`/`PLATFORM_WALLET_SECRET` di chain apa pun, dan 6 tempat mengambil alamat platform lewat `getPlatformKeypair()` (kunci Solana). Diperbaiki: (1) **`getChain().platformAddress()`** baru di kontrak adapter — Solana: `PLATFORM_WALLET_SECRET`, EVM: diturunkan dari `PLATFORM_WALLET_PRIVATE_KEY` via viem — dipakai di `engine.computePrizePool`, daftar exclusion engine, `/api/status`, `/api/holders`, `/api/admin/holders`, `/api/rounds/[n]`, `telegram-bot`; (2) **`getConfig()` chain-aware**: key wajib mengikuti chain (`CHAIN_RPC_URL`/`TOKEN_ADDRESS`/`PLATFORM_WALLET_PRIVATE_KEY` di EVM), `platformWalletSecret` sengaja kosong di EVM (pemanggil Solana-only gagal keras, bukan tanda tangan sampah), `TOKEN_DECIMALS` default 6/18 per chain, dan `MAX_PRIZE_SOL`/`MIN_PRIZE_SOL` dibaca sebagai **`MAX_PRIZE_NATIVE`/`MIN_PRIZE_NATIVE` dulu** (nama lama tetap jalan sebagai fallback). (3) **`.env` ditulis ulang** jadi 5 section (Chain · Lottery settings · Database · Admin · **SOLANA/PUMP.FUN LEGACY** yang bisa dihapus sebagai satu blok) + `NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN=solana` eksplisit; `MAX_/MIN_PRIZE_SOL` → `MAX_/MIN_PRIZE_NATIVE`; 5 key yang disiapkan tapi belum dibaca di-comment dengan catatan. - **Skema DB diperlebar ke `numeric` + lapisan konversi Decimal (2026-10-29).** Uji holder EVM menemukan blocker nyata: kolom amount dulu `bigint` (int8, maks **9,22e18**) sedangkan saldo token 18 desimal mencapai **1e27** → `npm run holders:sync` gagal dengan `Could not convert argument value … BigInt`. Probe membuktikan: insert int8-max **OK**, insert `7,97e26` **GAGAL**. **9 kolom** diperlebar ke `Decimal @db.Decimal(78,0)` (`HolderSnapshot.amount`; `LotteryRound.prizePoolLamports`/`minTokenEligible`/`payoutFeeLamports`/`platformFeeLamports`; `Winner.amountLamports`; `CreatorFeeClaim.amountLamports`/`pumpLamports`/`pumpSwapLamports`), diterapkan lewat `prisma migrate diff --script` → `prisma db execute` (tanpa prompt interaktif, tanpa data loss; `migrate diff` ulang = "empty migration"). **Jebakan kedua** yang ketemu saat verifikasi: `Prisma.Decimal.toString()` beralih ke **notasi eksponen** di >21 digit (`7.97063843490960830516755487e+26`) sehingga `BigInt()` melempar error → dibuat **`src/lib/db-amount.ts`** (`toBigInt` memakai `toFixed(0)`, `toBaseUnitString` untuk payload JSON/UI, `toDecimalValue` untuk penulisan) dan ~14 file disesuaikan (`engine`, `sync`, `format`, `telegram-bot`, `creator-fees`, `api/{status,rounds,rounds/[n],holders,eligibility,wallets,treasury/ledger,admin/*}`). Verifikasi: `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 error**; `npm run holders:sync` (stub indexer berisi data **asli** token NIGHT) → **exit 0**, `11 of 19 holders in 1 request(s) (truncated: eligibility floor / page cap reached)`; baca-balik dari Neon → **11 baris**, nilai terbesar **797063843490960830516755487** round-trip persis, `holders total` **16** (5 Solana + 11 EVM berdampingan). Early-exit terbukti nyata: holder ke-12 (483.813.813 < `MIN_TOKEN_ELIGIBLE` 1e12) menghentikan walk. Token uji di `.env`: **NIGHT `0xA99e1461…A339`** (nightjar, 18dp, 19 holder; holder #1 = kontrak terverifikasi **`PonsV2BondingCurve`** dengan 79,7% supply → kandidat exclusion pertama).

Kode: `src/lib/chain/robinhood.ts` (tanpa SDK — `fetch` + JSON-RPC + REST Blockscout), `network.ts` (`CHAIN_ID` **4663**, `CHAIN_LABEL`, explorer default `robinhoodchain.blockscout.com`), `index.ts` (adapter terdaftar), `types.ts` (`payoutsImplemented`, `listHolders(opts)`, snapshot `truncated`/`pagesFetched`), `config.ts` (**reserve chain-aware**: EVM membaca `PRIZE_RESERVE_WEI`, default **0,05 ETH** — angka `5e7` lama akan salah satuan dan bisa membayarkan seluruh saldo), `sync.ts` (`minBalance` + `maxPages` + log `x dari y holder`), `engine.ts` (**pagar keras**: `payoutsImplemented === false` → tolak **sebelum** baris round dibuat, jadi chain tanpa payout tidak pernah menghasilkan draw). Bukti uji: `eth_chainId` `0x1237`, `net_version` 4663, `web3_clientVersion` **`nitro/v3.12.1`** (konfirmasi Arbitrum Orbit), `eth_blockNumber` ≈100,75 juta, gas **0,020092 gwei**, `eth_call` `decimals()`=**18** & `totalSupply()`=**1e27** pada token PONS, `eth_getCode` 5.274 byte, `eth_getTransactionReceipt` status `0x1`; lewat dev server adapter mengembalikan `kind=robinhood`/`symbol=ETH`/`decimals=18`/`payoutsImplemented=false` + `explorer` Blockscout + balance 0 wei; walk holder dengan upstream di-stub → **3 request** (halaman ke-3 tidak diminta karena early-exit), 80 holder, `truncated=true`, cursor diteruskan, alamat lowercase; `npm run lottery:run` dengan `NEXT_PUBLIC_CHAIN=robinhood` → **exit 0**, pesan jelas, `roundNumber: 0`, **total round tetap 20**; mode default Solana tetap utuh (`chain=solana`, pool 0, saldo 0,001295 SOL, `/draws` + `/verify` 200). Dua gotcha nyata ketemu & diperbaiki: **Alchemy free tier membatasi `eth_getLogs` maks 10 blok/request**, dan **`import "server-only"` di `chain/*` memecahkan semua script tsx** (paket itu tidak ada di `node_modules` — Next meng-alias-kannya).

- **Treasury ledger penuh (2026-10-28).** API baru **`GET /api/treasury/ledger`** (`src/app/api/treasury/ledger/route.ts`): semua draw `completed` punya `payoutTx` (tanpa cap 50), field ringkas saja (`roundNumber, at, payoutTx, paidSol, feeSol, payoutFeeSol`) — Sebelumnya `/treasury` membangun tabel dari `/api/rounds` (full winner payloads, darurat sudah mengabaikan draw >50). `page.tsx` migrasi ke ledger itu, kedua tabel wallet full-history sekarang. Limitasi yang dijaga di doc comment: **receipt draw saja**, top-up/manual transfer tidak terpetakan (butuh indexer).
- **VPS error page + ErrorBoundary diagnostik (2026-10-28).** Laporan "This page couldn’t load" di VPS dengan log pm2 bersih = error browser (umumnya ChunkLoadError saat deploy mengganti .next sementara server masih jalan; solusi deploy: pm2 stop, rm -rf .next/cache, npm run build, pm2 start; 1 instance fork sudah benar). Tambahan src/app/error.tsx: menampilkan pesan + digest di UI, log error ke console, membedakan stale chunk (deploy replaced files, reload) vs render exception, tombol Try again/Reload - jadi diagnosa berikutnya tinggal copy-paste teks errornya.
  `toLocaleString` / `toLocaleDateString` on the site was called **without `timeZone`**, so it rendered
  the visitor's clock. `/treasury`'s fee-wallet card showed `updated Oct 26, 15:32` (WIB) while the same
  moment was `08:32` in the API and in the home countdown. New helpers in `src/lib/format.ts`:
  **`fmtDateTimeUtc(iso | Date)`** → `Oct 26, 08:32 UTC` (year only when not the current UTC year,
  `hourCycle: "h23"` so midnight never prints "24:00") and **`fmtDateUtc(iso | Date)`** → `Oct 26, 2026`;
  `timeAgo()` stays relative but its >7-day fallback is pinned to UTC too. Migrated: `/treasury`
  (`fmtTxDate` → wallet card, wallet tx table, buyback table), `/verify`, `/rounds/[n]`, `DrawsList`,
  `RecentDraws`, `/admin` (`fmtTime` + the "next draw" clock), `admin/CreatorFeesCard` (`shortDate`),
  `admin/TelegramBotPanel` (send log), `admin/ExclusionsCard` (`fmtDateUtc`), and the legacy `/holders` /
  `/live` pages. **Admin → Buyback** was worse than cosmetic: its `datetime-local` inputs were *read* as
  local time (`fromLocalInput`) and written back as local (`toLocalInput`), so an admin in WIB typed 15:32
  and `/treasury` printed 08:32. Both now go through `toUtcInput` / `fromUtcInput` (UTC getters +
  `${value}Z`), labels say `Next run (UTC)` / `When (UTC)`, and the hint states "Times are UTC".
  Untouched because already correct: `nextRunClock` on `/`, `fmtDayMonth` on `/treasury`, the `/stats`
  chart labels (`timeZone: "UTC"` server-side), and every numeric `toLocaleString`. **Verified:**
  `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**; helper exercised through `npx tsx` with `TZ=Asia/Jakarta` →
  `2026-10-26T08:32Z` prints `Oct 26, 08:32 UTC`, midnight prints `00:05` (not `24:05`), an older date
  prints `Mar 9, 2024, 23:59 UTC`, `null` → `—`, junk input returned as-is. **Display is now
  VPS/browser-TZ independent** — but scheduling is not: `node-cron` in `scheduler.ts` is still called
  without a `timezone` option, so cron fields follow the **server's** TZ, which is why
  `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §2.3 (`sudo timedatectl set-timezone UTC`) stays mandatory. Optional hardening, not
  applied: pass `{ timezone: "UTC" }` to each `cron.schedule` (it would shift an existing non-UTC prod box
  by the offset, so pair it with the deploy step).

  object-contain`) in both sidebar headers (home has its own, other routes use `AppShell`), replacing the
  tinted initial square; `onError` falls back to that initial so a missing/renamed file never shows a
  broken-image glyph (the file is **not uploaded yet** — see Pending). The extension lives in one constant
  (`BRAND_LOGO_SRC`) if the upload is a `.svg` / `.webp`. Both sidebars also renamed the bottom group
  heading **`Information` → `Social media`**, dropped the weight (`font-semibold` → `font-normal`) and
  raised it a notch (`text-[10px]` → `text-xs`); the `nav.ts` doc comment follows. `brandInitial` became
  dead in both files and was removed. `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**.

  fields — renders in **Geist Sans** without touching the ≈160 class usages in 25 files.
  `tabular-nums` is what keeps digits aligned now. Verified over CDP on `/`, `/draws`, `/stats`,
  `/treasury`, `/eligibility`, `/verify`, `/how-it-works`: the only computed family is **GeistSans**
  (242 leaf nodes on `/`), the `--font-mono` token resolves to the GeistSans stack, there are **zero**
  `as="font"` preloads for the mono face, and `<html class>` is the single `__variable_4cefcd`.
  `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, every route **200**. Deliberately not done: deleting the
  `font-mono` classes — they are now inert aliases, and one token change brings mono back.
  `globals.css`, `layout.tsx` and `.clinerules` carry a note so a later session doesn't re-add the

  passes on the middle tile in `page.tsx`, all measured in headless Chrome:
  (1) **Tile order** — the strip now reads *Prize pool → Prize split → Next draw*; the JSX blocks were
  physically reordered (no CSS `order`), so DOM/tab/reflow order matches what is on screen.
  (2) **Payout caption** — the per-row `SOL` + `%` line went `text-[9px]` → **`text-xs` (12px)**,
  `leading-none`, and **lost `font-mono`** (Geist Sans now, same as body); `tabular-nums` kept so the
  numbers stay column-aligned.
  (3) **Bar + row height** — `ProgressBar size="md"` (8px) → **`size="lg"` (12px)**, row `h-8` → **`h-10`
  (40px)** so 12px label + 2px of air + 12px track fits while the track stays centred on the 28px medal
  (`medCenter` 20 = `trackCenter` 20); row `gap-2.5` → `gap-2` keeps the desktop tile at **253px**.
  Loading skeletons follow (`h-10` rows, `h-3` bar placeholder).
  (4) **Header chip** — `≈ X SOL` used to be `font-mono` with the explanation in a native `title`
  (browser bubble, no CSS). Now the chip is the **body font** (`tabular-nums` kept) and the note is a
  real HeroUI **`Tooltip`** (`delay={150}`, `Tooltip.Content` = `max-w-[16rem] text-xs leading-snug`):
  "Net of the platform fee — ≈ X SOL would be split between the winners if the draw ran now."
  `Tooltip.Trigger` is a **`flex`** wrapper with `role="presentation" tabIndex={-1}` — the chip has no
  action so it must not become a tab stop, and `flex` is required (see gotcha below).
  Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**; `GET /` **200**; CDP 1440 → tiles `477 / 298 / 298` × **253**,
  390 → **358** wide, split **236** tall, `scrollWidth === innerWidth` (no horizontal overflow), chip
  `font-family` first token = **GeistSans** at **12px**; tooltip on hover computes
  `bg oklch(0.2103 0.003 253.83)`, white text, **12px**, radius **12px**, padding **8px**, width **256px**.
  Two gotchas worth remembering: **(a)** `Tooltip.Trigger` renders a `div`, so a bare block wrapper
  inflates the header line-box and grows the tile **253 → 256** (mobile **236 → 240**) — `className="flex"`
  pins it to the chip's own height; **(b)** headless Chrome does **not** open RAC tooltips on hover unless
  `Emulation.setFocusEmulationEnabled { enabled: true }` is sent first (`document.hasFocus()` is `false`
  and react-aria swallows the hover) — without it a working tooltip looks dead. `npm run lint` is still
  broken repo-wide (Next 16 removed `next lint`) — pre-existing, left alone.

  `shadow-*-500/40` drop, so the ordinal sits on struck metal instead of a label tint; the seam
  `ring-2 ring-surface` stays. `rankBar` turned into `bg-linear-to-r from-500 via-400 to-300` fills
  (gold / silver / bronze), the track is now a recessed groove
  (`bg-default/50 shadow-[inset_0_1px_2px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)]`), and the gold bar carries
  `.split-shine` — a new `globals.css` pseudo-element sweep (`split-shine-sweep`, 4.5s, reduce-motion
  guarded) clipped by the fill's own `overflow-hidden`, so 1st place reads as live metal. The tile
  also picked up the pool card's family look: a **half-opacity `pool-sheen` wash** as its first child
  (dimming lives on a wrapper, because `.pool-sheen` animates its own opacity and would override
  `opacity-50`), with every sibling switched to `relative` so the wash never covers text. The header
  chip now carries data instead of a word — `≈ {fmtSolHuman(splitPoolSol)}` (mono, tabular) with the
  "net of the platform fee" note in its `title`, falling back to `Estimate` when the pool is empty;
  in that empty state the share percentage is promoted from `text-muted` to full-strength
  `font-medium` so the row isn't three grey bars. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**; `GET /` **200**;
  CDP at 1440 / 390 — rows still **28px**, track **8px**, tiles **253 / 204px** (nothing grew),
  `background-image` resolves to real oklch gradients on both medals and bars, `::after` on the gold

- **Buyback ledger: row tints out, lucide kind badges in (2026-10-25).** The green/red
