  because there the strings really are hashes.
- **PM2 deployment path + `ecosystem.config.js` (2026-10-25).** New repo-root
  **`ecosystem.config.js`** so the VPS can run the app under PM2 instead of systemd: `exec_mode:
  "fork"` with **1** instance (the scheduler lives inside the Next process — cluster or `-i max`
  would double rounds, syncs and payouts), `cwd: __dirname`, `script: "npm"` + `args: "run start"`
  (literally `npm start` → `next start`), `kill_timeout: 10000` (SIGINT → SIGKILL window so Prisma
  can close), `time` + `merge_logs`, and only `NODE_ENV` / `PORT` / `HOSTNAME` in `env` — secrets
  stay in `.env`, which `next start` loads itself; the legacy `NODE_OPTIONS` DNS flag sits commented
  out because current builds force IPv4 in `src/lib/net-ipv4.ts`. `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §8b grew from a
  five-line alternative into **8b.1–8b.6**: install + `pm2 start ecosystem.config.js` +
  `pm2 startup systemd -u pay --hp /home/pay` + `pm2 save`; verification (`pm2 list` **online** with
  `restarts` 0, `Ready in …ms`, `[cron] holders synced`, `curl` 200, `systemctl status pm2-pay`);
  daily ops (`pm2 restart pay` after `.env`, `pm2 restart ecosystem.config.js --update-env` after
  editing the file, `pm2 monit`, `pm2 flush`, logs in `~/.pm2/logs/pay-*.log` — no journald); the
  PM2 deploy flow (`pm2 stop` **before** `next build`, then `restart ecosystem.config.js
  --update-env`, plus rollback); `pm2-logrotate` (10M / retain 14 / compress); the expensive
  pitfalls (two supervisors fighting over port 3000, `sudo pm2` creating a root daemon that owns
  `data/buyback.json`, a forgotten `pm2 save` resurrecting a stale list, cluster mode, no
  `max_memory_restart` — a restart mid-payout aborts the send — and `pm2 startup` after a Node/nvm
  upgrade); and the path back to systemd (`pm2 delete` → `pm2 save` → `pm2 unstartup`). §0 now lists
  systemd **or** PM2 and names "two supervisors at once" as the thing not to do; §11 gained the PM2
  variant of the update flow; §13.2 gained `EADDRINUSE`/`errored`, restart-loop and `pm2: command
  not found` rows plus a `pm2 logs` line; §14's checklist now says "exactly one supervisor" instead
  of "systemd enabled". **Verified:** `node -e "require('./ecosystem.config.js')"` → one app `pay`,
  `fork`, 1 instance, `npm run start`, env production/3000/127.0.0.1, `kill_timeout` 10000;
  `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; no source file touched, so no rebuild needed. Not run against a
  real PM2 daemon here (none on this machine) — the unit name `pm2-pay` and the `~/.pm2` log paths
  follow PM2 5.x/6.x behaviour.
- **Cache card + editable Telegram templates + paged Recent draws (2026-10-25).** Three independent changes. **(1) Admin → Settings → Cache:** new `admin/CacheCard.tsx` sits between `SettingsSummary` and `DangerZoneCard`, lists up to 12 cached keys with their age, and offers **Refresh** (`GET /api/admin/cache`) + **Clear cache** (`POST` → `{ ok, cleared, remaining }`, then the page's `refreshAll()`). The route is admin-guarded, accepts an optional `prefix` (`"admin:"` / `"rounds"`) and logs what it dropped. The bug it exposed is real: `lib/cache.ts` kept `store` / `inflight` module-local, but Next gives every route its own module instance, so the clear could empty a different `Map` from the one `/api/status` filled — both now live on `globalThis` (`__payCacheStore` / `__payCacheInflight`). **(2) Telegram message templates:** new pure, client-safe `lib/telegram-templates.ts` (`DEFAULT_ROUND/DRAW_TEMPLATE`, `ROUND/DRAW_PLACEHOLDERS` + help, `MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH = 2000`, `placeholdersIn()`, `validateTemplate()`, `applyTemplate()`); `telegram-bot.ts` now renders through it (`roundValues()` / `drawValues()` / `renderRoundHtml()` / `renderDrawHtml()`), keeps escaping every interpolated value, and guards Telegram's 4096-char ceiling before sending. `roundTemplate` / `drawTemplate` round-trip through the panel (`normalizeBotInput` rejects unknown placeholders as per-field errors) and a new `action: "preview"` API path renders a draft through the **same** code as a real send. Unknown placeholders are a save **and** preview error — a `{pool}` typo would otherwise post a literal to the group; empty optional values (`firstPrizeLine`, `playableNote`, `payoutTxLine`, `link`) resolve to `""` and the blank line collapses, so optional rows leave no gap. **(3) Recent draws paging:** `RecentDraws.tsx` pages **5** rows (was an unpaged 3) with HeroUI `Pagination` (Prev · “Page N / M” · Next, disabled at the bounds) + a “Showing 1–5 of 12 draws” caption, and `page.tsx` hands over every settled round in the 50-round window instead of `.slice(0, 3)` — client-side only, `/draws` stays the deeper archive. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0**, `eslint` on the 10 touched files → **0**; live dev probes (`:3000`) — `/api/admin/cache` **401** without the cookie, **200** with 5 entries, POST `{ cleared: 5, remaining: 0 }`; `/api/admin/telegram` 401/200 with both templates and previews (192/204 chars); `{nope}` preview → **400** naming the bad placeholder, a valid custom draft → **200**; `previewBotTemplates()` against the real DB renders the current round + last draw; `GET /` and `/admin` → **200**. No Prisma change, no rebuild needed.
- **Draw cadence cheat-sheet (2026-10-25).** §5.4 gained a "Draw tiap 10 menit → `LOTTERY_CRON=*/10 * * * *`" row plus the `0 */10 * * *` = tiap **10 jam** trap, a "restart to re-register" note, and the tight-schedule caveats (in-process `drawing` guard skips overlapping ticks; an empty prize wallet still writes one `skipped` row per tick → 144/day, and `/api/rounds` returns only **`take: 50`** so real draws can scroll off `/draws`). Verified locally: `nextCronOccurrence('*/10 …')` from `10:07Z` → `10:10, 10:20, 10:30, 10:40`, and `node-cron.validate('*/10 * * * *') === true`. Reminder: the **Telegram** round cadence is not env — it is Admin → Telegram Bot `intervalMinutes` (**1–1440**, default **2**), minute-resolution tick that no-ops when idle.
- **Deploy guide: two DB paths + Telegram bot step (2026-10-25).** `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §6.1 splits **jalur A** (existing Neon DB — no DB command at all) from **jalur B** (new empty Neon project): create project → copy **pooled** (`-pooler`, for `.env`) **and** **direct** strings → `sudo -u pay env DATABASE_URL='…direct…' npx prisma db push` (DDL through PgBouncer hangs; reset prompt means wrong DB → answer No) → verify the **8** tables with a read-only `pg_tables` query → `holders:sync` so `eligible` is not 0. It states there is **no seed** (`AdminSetting` rows — palette/SEO/telegram config — appear on the first `/admin` Save) and that `db push` must never touch the populated DB. Every DB snippet now loads `.env` explicitly with `@next/env` (`require('@next/env').loadEnvConfig(process.cwd())`), because a bare `node -e` is not guaranteed to; the query was validated locally (returns `AdminSetting, CreatorFeeClaim, ExcludedWallet, Holder, HolderSnapshot, LotteryRound, NotificationSubscription, Winner`, `rounds: 19 | holders: 5`) — the first draft used `` $queryRaw`…` `` inside a double-quoted `node -e`, which bash would have treated as **command substitution**, so it was rewritten to `$queryRawUnsafe('… $1 …', 'public')`. Also added: `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` to §5.2 (key list **29 → 30**, noting DB wins and no rebuild needed), an optional **Admin → Telegram Bot** verification step in §10 (BotFather → add to group → **Find chat id** → Save → **Send test** / **Post round now** / **Resend last draw**, plus `401` for `/api/admin/telegram` in the curl block and expectation table), **8** new rows in the §13.2 symptom table (pooler-hang, `P1001`, empty-DB 0 holders, `Unauthorized`, `chat not found`, `not enough rights`, paused panel, no post after deploy), checklist items for the DB path + the bot, and a §11 note that `AdminSetting`-backed features (palette/SEO/Buyback/Telegram) need no migration while `scheduler.ts` changes need build **+** restart. `HANDOFF.md` got the same new-DB ordering and `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`. Both files: fence parity even, zero mojibake.
- **Telegram group bot + new Admin → Telegram Bot tab (2026-10-25).** A bot now posts to one group: (1) a **current-round** message every **N minutes** (default **2**) with the pool, eligible-holder count, estimated 1st prize, countdown to the next draw (or “⏸ Automatic draws are paused” when the cron is off) and the site link; (2) a **draw result** the moment a round is persisted `completed` — ranked winners with payouts, drand beacon status, payout tx and a `/rounds/<n>` link. New **`src/lib/telegram-bot.ts`**, **`/api/admin/telegram`** (admin-guarded GET/PUT/POST) and **`src/components/admin/TelegramBotPanel.tsx`** behind a new **Telegram Bot** tab; `scheduler.ts` adds a **`* * * * *`** tick that calls `runBotTick()` (registered even when the draw cron is off — the tick decides from the DB config), and `engine.ts` calls `notifyDrawResult()` right after `notifyWinners()`. Config is **two `AdminSetting` JSON rows** (`TELEGRAM_BOT_CONFIG` / `TELEGRAM_BOT_STATE`) → **no migration, no `db:push`**; the interval is enforced against the persisted `lastRoundSentAt` so a panel change applies on the next tick without a restart, and a draw is announced once per round (`lastDrawRound`) with a 30-minute / 5-minute-spaced recovery attempt from the tick if the engine’s send never landed. The **token is write-only**: saved in the DB from the panel (it wins) or read from `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` in `.env` (fallback), GET only returns `tokenSource` + a masked `••••1234` hint, and every error/log line is redacted. Panel also offers **Send test**, **Post round now**, **Resend last draw** and **Find chat id** (`getUpdates`, so no hunting for `-100…`), plus plain-text previews of both messages and the in-memory send log. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `eslint` **0** on all six touched files; live admin API probe → invalid interval `0` / bad chat id / bad token each **400** with the field error, valid save **200**, `Send test` with a fake token reached Telegram and came back **400 `Telegram: Unauthorized`** (real outbound path, redacted), `chats` returned the same clean error, and the failure was recorded in `state.lastError` + history; `runBotTick()` with the bot **disabled** is a no-op (238 ms, DB only) and with it **enabled** it posted once, stored `lastRoundSentAt`, then a second tick **did not** re-send (interval gate). No-op paths checked outside a request with `tsx --env-file=.env` (probe deleted afterwards; both test AdminSetting rows removed, so the panel opens clean: *Paused*, no chat id). Headless Chrome (CDP, real session): `/admin` → **Telegram Bot** renders at **1280** and **390 px** with 3 switches + 7 inputs, correct previews (`🎲 DrawFi lottery · round #20 …` / `🏆 … round #17 drawn`), `docScrollW === innerWidth` at both widths (the tab strip scrolls, as before), zero console errors; UI save round-trip → interval `0` blocked with “Must be a whole number between 1 and 1440 minutes.”, `2` saved with “Settings saved.”. **Restart `npm run dev` once** to arm the new scheduler tick (`instrumentation.ts` only registers crons at boot); the panel’s manual actions work immediately. Messages omit links until a site URL exists (SEO & Analytics → Site URL) and the panel says so.
- **Hero proof stats + `drand.sh` link (2026-10-25).** The home hero card (`src/app/page.tsx`) shows **three** all-time counters **above the CTAs**, in narrative order: **Draws completed** (`totals.draws`) → **Winners paid** (`totals.paidCount`) → **Paid out to holders** (`totals.paidSol` + a small muted `SOL` unit), in a `<dl className="flex flex-wrap items-stretch gap-y-4">` — labels `text-muted` (11 px below `sm`, `text-xs` from `sm`), values `font-mono text-xl font-semibold leading-none tracking-tight tabular-nums`, all three animated with the existing `CountUp`. The hairline (`border-t border-(--border) pt-4`) and the 24 px `text-2xl` values were removed on user feedback — the row now reads as plain whitespace under the description and the numbers sit at **20 px**. **Source is `/api/stats`**, not `/api/rounds`: the rounds endpoint is capped at the newest 50 rows, so summing there would silently under-count once history grows; `/api/stats` aggregates every round server-side and is cached 30 s. It is a one-shot fetch (`cache: "no-store"`) separate from the 5 s status poll, `statsLoaded` is set in `finally`, and a failed fetch settles on `—` (skeletons render until then). Paid total uses 4-dp `fmtSolNum` (same precision as the pool tile) because 2 dp collapsed the current 0.0063 SOL into a misleading `<0.01`. **Winners paid** counts winner rows with `status: "sent"` — one per payout actually broadcast. `uniqueWinners` was deliberately not used for it: on the current devnet snapshot it would read **2** while 10 payouts went out (the same two wallets keep winning), which looks broken next to "Draws completed 5". A fourth cell, **Largest pool** (`totals.biggestPrize`), existed briefly and was **removed on user request** — at 0.0018 SOL it read like noise next to the payout total and pushed the ordering off (`draws → winners → paid out` is the story); the field is gone from the hero's `StatsTotals` slice too — `biggestPrize` still lives in `/api/stats` and in the page's `Stats` type because the stats grid further down the home page ("Largest pool") consumes it. The three cells are separated by **1 px `border-l border-(--border)` hairlines** on cells 2 and 3 at **every** width — the row must never break into 2 + 1 on mobile (explicit user requirement: "tetep sejajar dong"), so the cells were made narrow enough to hold one line at 360 px by dropping the label size below `sm`: `text-[11px] leading-4 tracking-tight text-muted sm:text-xs sm:tracking-normal`, with `pl-2.5 pr-2.5 sm:pl-5 sm:pr-5` in place of the old `gap-x-10` (12 px labels at 96 + 71 + 103 px are what used to force the third cell onto its own line). Measured at 360 (294 px available): cells **94 / 83 / 106 px**, right edge **316**, `dl` h=41, 11 px slack; at 390 the same widths leave 41 px free; from 640 px up labels return to `text-xs` and the cells measure **116 / 112 / 124 px** with rules at x=165/533 and x=421/533. `dl` scrollWidth === clientWidth at those widths (no clipping) and every `dd` stays one line (`h` 20–21). 320 px (iPhone SE 1st gen) still wraps — accepted, it is below any current device class. The container is `items-stretch` so the rules run the full cell height. A plain-text **`drand.sh`** link follows the "How draws work" button, built on the shared `ExternalLink` (muted, `decoration-(--border)`, `arrow-up-right`, `target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"`, `href="https://drand.sh"`), at **`text-xs`** so it trails the 14 px buttons without reading as a third CTA. A `Randomness from drand.sh` caption on its own line (`sm:basis-full`) was tried first and reverted — the user wanted the bare label, just smaller. The CTA row itself is `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3` — no `flex-col` on mobile and **no centring at any width**, so the two buttons sit **side by side** at 360 px flush left with the description (button padding drops to `px-4` below `sm`, back to `px-5` above it, measured 131 + 145 px + 10 px gap = 286 px inside 294 px), and `drand.sh` wraps to a second line that is also left-aligned at the card's text edge (x=33 at 360/390; it was briefly centred and reverted on user request). Verified via CDP at 360/390/768/1440: `docScrollW === vw` at 360 and 390, `dl` h=**41** with **all three cells on one row** at 360/390/768 (identical `y`, no `border-top` anywhere), rules computed as `1px solid oklch(0.28 0.0015 253.83)` on cells 2 and 3 only, `dt` fontSize **11 px** below `sm` / **12 px** from `sm`, stats value size `20px`, live values read **5** draws / **10** winners paid / **0.0063 SOL** paid out. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0, `eslint src/app/page.tsx` → 0.
- **Mobile tables pan instead of clipping (2026-10-25).** Root cause: HeroUI's `.table-root` ships `overflow: clip`, so a `<table>` wider than its card was **cut off** — the `overflow-x-auto` wrappers on `/treasury` and `/eligibility` never had anything to scroll (`rootW` 326–356 vs `tableW` 342–428 at 360–390 px; the TX/STATUS columns were simply gone). Fix: every wide table now nests `<Table.ScrollContainer tabIndex={0} className="overscroll-x-contain focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-accent">` around `<TableContent>` and carries a `min-w-*` so columns stay legible: `HoldersBrowser.tsx` (`min-w-[30rem]`), `/stats` leaderboard (`min-w-[32rem]`), `/rounds/[n]` winners (`min-w-[34rem]`), `/treasury` `TxTable` (`min-w-[21rem]`) and buyback ledger (`min-w-[23rem]`); the dead `overflow-x-auto` wrappers were dropped. `tabIndex={0}` keeps the pan keyboard-reachable (measured `outline: 2px solid oklch(0.6204 0.195 253.83)` = accent, offset −2 px). Verified via CDP (`Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride`) at 360/390/768/1440: `docScrollW === vw` on all four routes, zero bleed/overlap between row cells, and at 1440 no scroll at all (`scrollCw === scrollSw === 1118`). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0, `eslint` on the four touched files → 0. Left alone on purpose: `/holders` + `/live` (308 redirects in `next.config`) and `/admin` (desktop-only).
- **`/rounds/[n]` copy reconciled with the details endpoint and the engine (2026-10-25).** Read `page.tsx`, `layout.tsx`, `api/rounds/[roundNumber]/route.ts`, `engine.ts`, `solana/randomness.ts` and `exclusions.ts` together. **Overclaims removed:** the old subtitle promised "three checks your browser can run … nothing was edited afterwards", but check 3 re-runs the Fisher–Yates shuffle on the **wallet list saved by the server**, so the checks card now says *"The checks cannot show that the list holds every holder."* and check 3's hint repeats it (*"It shows the winners follow from that list, not that the list holds every holder."*). Check 1's comment/title no longer claims a zero-trust edit test: it only verifies `randomness = SHA-256(signature)`, and the hint says so. **Skip/fail copy branches on the real `engine.ts` strings:** `No eligible holders…` → *"No wallet was eligible for this draw, so there was nobody to pick."*, `below minimum` → *"The prize pool didn’t reach the minimum needed for a draw."*, `balance at/below reserve` → *"The balance sat at or below the reserve, so this draw had no prize pool and picked no winners."*, `payout tx`/`failed on-chain` → payout-failed or *"This draw did not finish, so it paid no one."*, unknown skip → *"This draw stopped before it picked any winners."* Raw `round.error` only on `title`; the duplicate standalone *"This draw picked no winners."* line is suppressed when the note already says it. **Vocabulary:** *Finished* → **Closed** (`completedAt`), *Randomness round* → **Beacon round**, *Eligible wallets* → **Eligible holders**, *Wallets excluded* → **Addresses on the exclusion list** (counts **all** `excludedAddresses`, previously only non-platform entries; worded as list size so it is not read as "holders removed"), *Signature type* → **Signature scheme** with `bls-unchained-g1-rfc9380` rendered as **BLS unchained G1 (RFC 9380)** instead of the raw enum.
  **Eligibility card:** heading *The wallet list this draw used* → **for this draw** (skipped rounds never shuffled it); the caption now says the list is every holder at/above the minimum minus the project wallet, the Pump.fun bonding curve, the PumpSwap pool authority and the exclusion list, plus *"A draw only shuffles this list, so an excluded wallet cannot be picked."* **Placeholders:** prose `—` and `≠` are gone; missing values read **Not recorded** (beacon round), **Not saved** (randomness/signature) and **None** (payout tx). The exact-values card is hidden when both fields are null (it used to show two "Not saved" boxes under a "Copy them" caption with no copy button); its caption is now *"These are the raw values behind the checks above, so you can reproduce them elsewhere."* New empty state **"Nothing to check for this draw"** replaces the silent gap when no randomness exists. A note under the summary explains `prizePoolSol` is gross (the amount available to pay winners) while winner amounts are net of the platform fee and display rounding. **Links/UI:** winner addresses and payout signatures use muted `hover:text-foreground hover:underline` instead of `text-accent` (accent is blue in this theme), the two drand links use the shared `ExternalLink` from `@/components/ui`, and check chips that end idle **with a detail** read **Not run** instead of a permanent "Waiting". `layout.tsx` descriptions are status-neutral (*"What happened in draw #N: the result, the values saved with it, and the checks anyone can re-run."*). **Also confirmed:** `DrawsList.tsx` (~line 450) *does* link `payoutTx` to the explorer (icon-only anchor, `aria-label="Open the payout transaction on Solscan"`), closing that open question. **Second pass (same day, rendered-page audit):** every visible string was then checked against `/rounds/12` and `/rounds/14`. The subtitle now switches to *"What stopped this draw, and what was saved with it."* when no randomness was saved (it no longer promises winners on a skipped draw); *Finished* became **Closed at** (summary + skeleton), *Holders in the pool* became **Holders in the list** (pool already means prize pool), `3s` became **3 seconds**, `2023-08-23` became **Aug 23, 2023**, the wallet lookup asks *"Was this wallet eligible for draw #N?"* with **Yes, eligible** / **No, not eligible**, check details read *"Re-derived 2 winners…"* and *"N ranks differ"* (no `rank(s)`), and the drand link reads *"Open beacon round N on drand.sh"*. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, `npx eslint` on both files → **0 problems**; CDP `/rounds/12` → 0 em dashes, 0 mojibake, 0 forbidden strings, 3× `✓ Match`; `/rounds/14` → skip subtitle, reserve sentence, *"Nothing to check for this draw"*, no dead values card. **Still open:** `HowItWorksFlow.tsx` on `/` (flagged, untouched) and the `/draws` payout-link `aria-label` being identical on every row.
- **`/how-it-works` now matches the code path, including the draws that never reach drand (2026-10-25).** Read the page against `engine.ts`, `solana/randomness.ts`, `config.ts` and `exclusions.ts`. Factual fixes: the header promised "hold {token} and you're in every draw" → now names the **minimum** and says **"on the list for every draw"**; eligibility dropped "there is nothing to buy" (you must hold the token) for **"No ticket to buy and nothing to sign"** and adds **"Holding more does not improve the odds: one wallet is one entry"** (`selectWinners` shuffles addresses, not balances). Step 1 now lists the real exclusions (`getDrawExclusions`: project wallet, bonding curve, pool authority, blocked addresses), step 2 says it asks a second relay and refuses **the number** if they disagree, step 3 says the number **seeds a deterministic shuffle** of the address-sorted list (not "hashed into a stream"), step 4 adds the default 50/30/20 and the `min(winnerCount, eligible)` case (unclaimed shares roll into rank 1), and step 5 says Paid appears only **once the transfer confirms** (`status: "sent"` is written after `settle()` checks `meta.err`). Payouts: the reserve sentence now admits the optional `MAX_PRIZE_SOL` cap and the `MIN_PRIZE_SOL` skip; the fee paragraph says the fee reaches a treasury **only when one is configured** (`buildFeeTransfer` returns undefined otherwise, so it stays on the platform wallet). Provably fair: "neither one trusts our server" → "the first asks only drand; the second re-runs the shuffle on the wallet list saved with the draw" (check 2 consumes `/api/rounds/[n]`); card ② no longer claims nobody could have picked the winners, only that they follow from number + list. FAQ: "the team cannot choose" → what the code actually allows (exclusions run before the shuffle and the exact list is snapshotted); "skipped if the relays disagree" → **abandoned** (`requestRandomness` throws, the round is marked failed); winners answer gives **three by default / 50-30-20** plus the fewer-eligible case; network answer drops the false "shown at the bottom of the sidebar" (no such UI); CTA mentions skipped draws. `layout.tsx` description rewritten (152 chars with the live ticker). Every prose em dash dropped (0 in `innerText` **and** `textContent`). **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `eslint` on the 2 files **0 problems**, headless CDP on `http://localhost:3000/how-it-works` — 0 em dashes, none of `DATABASE_URL` / `postgres` / `PrismaClient` / `PLATFORM_WALLET` / `prizePoolLamports`, all 8 FAQ `<details>` expand, `Signature scheme` + `Three by default` + `newest holder snapshot` present.

- **`/verify` now claims only what its checks prove, and never prints a raw error (2026-10-25).** Read `/verify` + `SeedToWinner.tsx` against `/api/rounds/[n]` first. Copy: the intro promised "every draw starts with a random number", but `engine.ts` returns **before** drand for skipped draws; it now names the **three** checks (the saved number is the SHA-256 of its signature, drand published that same number, the saved wallet list reproduces the winners) and says skipped draws have nothing to check. "The beacon behind every draw" → **"The drand beacon"**, `Randomness round` → **"Beacon round"**, `Signature type` → **"Signature scheme"**, "Four steps, number to winners" → **"Four steps from number to winners"**, and the two-check card is now **conditional on `randomnessValue`** (otherwise "Nothing to check for this draw"). Leaks: `title={fetchError}` / `title={data.dbError}` removed from both danger banners, `(e as Error).message` removed from both `SeedToWinner` catches **and** from the two in-page check catches (fixed sentences), and skip rows no longer print the stored driver text — `skipReason()` maps it to one of four sentences (no eligible wallet / below minimum / empty pool / generic), with `statusLabel()` title-casing unknown enums plus a new `pending` label. Semantics: the exclusion line lists the project wallet **plus** the token's liquidity accounts **and** blocked addresses; the wallet-list caption admits `eligibleSource === "inferred"` / `platformExclusionReproduced === false` (the pool may include the project wallet); match/mismatch verdicts say "closest snapshot saved before this draw" instead of "the same wallet list" when the pool was inferred; check 2 also compares the returned `round` and names which of round / randomness / signature disagreed. Every prose em dash dropped. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `eslint` on the 3 touched files **0 problems**, headless CDP — completed **draw #12**: "Two checks on the saved number", both `✓ Match`, match note "from the saved number and the saved wallet list", **0** `—` in prose; skipped **draw #13**: "Nothing to check for this draw", NOTE "The prize pool was empty, so this draw picked no winners.", no raw reason, no `Waiting` chips; forced `/api/rounds` failure **and** a `dbError: "unavailable"` payload each render the neutral banner + "No draws to check yet" (no crash, no `NaN`, no error body).

- **`/stats` labels now say what the aggregates actually are, and a failed feed no longer reads as an empty history (2026-10-25).** Read `/api/stats` before touching copy: `totals.prizeSol` / `biggestPrize` come from `prizePoolLamports` (the pool **including** the platform fee), while `paidSol` is Σ`amountLamports` of winners with `status: "sent"`. So the chart tab **"Prize paid" → "Prize pool"**, the tile **"Biggest prize" → "Biggest pool"** (caption "average … per draw" — `avgPrize = prizeSol / draws` is pool per draw, not a winner's prize), and the chart subtitle is **"0.0054 SOL pooled across 4 draws"** (was "across"), with **"No draw finished in this window"** replacing `0.0000 SOL across 0 draws`. The prize legend's fee dot printed **all-time** `totals.platformFeesSol` beside bars that only cover 30 days → now `view.windowFee` (Σ`daily.feeSol`), label without "total", and one explanation replaces two overlapping ones: "Each bar is one day's prize pool: what went to winners plus any platform fee. No bar means no draw finished that day." Hero copy: "Every draw since launch" → **"Every completed draw"** (`totals.draws` counts `status: "completed"` only) and "wallets **qualified**" → **"wallets were eligible"** (eligibility is not draw entry once venue/manual exclusions apply); the eligible subtitle is "Eligible wallets from holder snapshots, latest N" / "Eligible wallets at draw time, no holder snapshots yet". The duplicate eligible legend sentence is gone; it now states what `eligibleValues` actually does: "Days without a snapshot carry the last count forward." **Failure state:** `/api/stats` now returns `dbError: "unavailable"` instead of `(e as Error).message` (Prisma messages can carry host, table and column names) and both `title={error}` / `title={data.dbError}` tooltips are gone; "No draw has finished yet. These numbers appear once the first one does." renders only when `!error && !data?.dbError`, because it used to sit directly under the error banner and claim an empty history during a failed fetch. Banners are "Couldn't load these numbers. Refresh the page to try again." / "These numbers are temporarily unavailable. Refresh in a moment." Also corrected `/treasury`'s platform caption **"pays winner airdrops and network fees" → "pays winner prizes and network fees"**: payouts are one batch of `SystemProgram.transfer` instructions (`distributePayouts`), and `requestAirdrop` exists only in the devnet faucet script `src/scripts/airdrop.ts`. Verified live via headless-Chrome DOM: **"Prize pool" ×1**, **"Biggest pool" ×1** (0.0018 SOL, "average 0.0014 SOL per draw"), "0.0054 SOL pooled across 4 draws", "Platform fee 0.0003 SOL", "8 of 8 payouts sent"; `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**, `npx eslint` on the three touched files **0**. Heading **"The lottery in numbers" → "Draw statistics"** to match the sibling pattern (`Draws` → "Draw history", `Holders` → "Token holders", `How it works` → "How the lottery works"); the tab title stays `Stats`, like the other nav labels, and the metadata description was already accurate.

- **`/treasury` no longer invents numbers or says "not configured" when a feed fails (2026-10-25).** Trigger: `PolicyCard`/`WalletCard` built their copy from `?? 0.05` / `?? 0` defaults, so a failed `/api/wallets` rendered a confident **"not configured"** plus a **0.05 SOL** reserve and **0%** fee; `BuybackCard` printed **0** runs / **0** tokens / **0%** supply on a failed `/api/buyback`. Fixed with explicit availability flags instead of new wording: `PolicyCard` gained **`available`** (one neutral sentence when `/api/wallets` fails, and the `sharePct = "—"` fallback is gone, so a missing share reads "Buyback share unavailable right now. Fees are collected from …"), `BuybackCard` gained **`unavailable`** (dashes, "ledger unavailable", "No runs yet", "supply unavailable" / "supply not recorded"), and `WalletCard` notes branch on whether wallets loaded at all. Leaks removed: `PLATFORM_TREASURY_SECRET` and the "edit `data/buyback.json` / Admin → Buyback" instructions are no longer public copy; the fee card caption is a 4-way ternary that uses `fee.ledgerAvailable` to separate "no fees yet" from "the fee ledger couldn't be read". `DEMO_FEE_TXS` can no longer sit in a half-loaded table: sample rows render only when `!roundsError && !feeTxs.live && feeTxs.draws === 0`. The rounds banner no longer falsely promises "the fee table below shows sample rows", the wallets banner is "Some figures couldn't be refreshed just now, so they may be out of date.", and the fee address link now uses `ExternalLink` (`tone="foreground"`) like the other pages. Em dashes removed from user-facing prose (tooltip, aria-label, empty notes) and the `layout.tsx` description no longer claims "every fee sweep on chain". A final copy pass removed the remaining config jargon ("No platform wallet is configured" → **"The platform wallet isn't available yet."**, fee note → **"Fees stay on the platform wallet. No separate fee wallet is in use."**), fixed three comma splices (platform empty note is now **"No payout transaction yet. One appears when a draw settles."**, fee caption "…at payout time, and buybacks are paid from it"), made the buyback units honest while the ledger is down (**"Bought — tokens unavailable"**, "Burned — supply unavailable", "SOL spent — not available" instead of a dash followed by "DrawFi tokens"), and the policy footer now drops the "payouts and fee sweeps are public Solana transactions" claim whenever `/api/wallets` failed. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, `npx eslint` on both files → **0 problems**; `Select-String` for `—` returns **18 hits, none in prose** (13 code comments plus the 5 `"—"` missing-value placeholders, which match the app-wide convention used by `/draws`, `/stats`, `/`, `/rounds`); `GET /treasury` against the running dev server → **200**, no runtime-error overlay, and the new copy matches live `/api/wallets` (`configured: true`, 4 sweeps, `ledgerAvailable: true`) + `/api/buyback` (3 runs, 3,752,333 bought, 3,000,000 burned, 3% of supply); a CDP run with **all six `/api/*` requests failed** renders the three neutral banners, `—` balances, "Wallet details couldn't be loaded just now.", "The buyback ledger couldn't be loaded. Refresh the page to try again." and the sentence-only policy card ("Reserve and fee figures couldn't be loaded. Refresh the page to try again."), with **0 hits** for `PLATFORM_TREASURY_SECRET`, `data/buyback.json`, `Admin`, `not configured`, `0.05`, `0%`, `Sample`, `undefined`, `NaN`.
- **`/eligibility` copy now matches what the API actually returns (2026-10-25).** Read `/api/eligibility` end-to-end first: `found` only means *present in the latest holder snapshot*, so the old `"This wallet hasn’t held {token} yet … check again after your first buy."` was wrong for a wallet that has held and sold, or was synced before the last buy. That banner is now **"This wallet isn’t in the holder list yet. If you just bought {token}, check again in a few minutes."** (chip `Not in the holder list` → **`Not listed`**). The info card's `Snapshot` row was ambiguous (it showed `lastSyncAt`, i.e. the list's age, not the wallet's balance read) → **`List updated`**, plus a separate **`Balance read`** row that appears only when `capturedAt` is missing or differs from `lastSyncAt`, and prints **`not in the latest snapshot`** instead of a blank when `capturedAt` is `null`. Server errors follow the `/draws` rule: `dbError` no longer paints Prisma text into the page — neutral sentence, raw string on `title`. `HoldersBrowser.tsx` (rendered **only** by `/eligibility`) got the same treatment: its fetch/`dbError` banner shows a generic sentence + `title`, the warning `Alert` is retitled **"Some wallets aren’t entered in the draw"** and now names liquidity accounts first (bonding curve / pool authority — excluded **by design**, which the old "Suspicious activity means no draw entry" framing got wrong) before sybils/bots/split wallets, and the table chip **`Below min` → `Below minimum`** to match the banner and result card vocabulary. Provenance checked against real data: `GET /api/holders` + `GET /api/eligibility` for a small wallet, a `manual` exclusion and a `venue` exclusion → `found` / `eligible` / `excluded` / `excludedBy` / `shortfallHuman` line up with the four banner states, and every found wallet had `capturedAt === lastSyncAt` (so the new `Balance read` row stays hidden in the normal case). Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, `eslint` on both files → **0 problems**, headless dump of `/eligibility` shows the new subtitle + hint + alert text, `Below minimum` ×2, and **zero** hits for the stale `Suspicious activity` / `Below min`. Copy then tightened after the user's review: **no em dashes in the prose, commas instead** (also applied to `DrawsList`'s two skip-reason strings and the `/draws` intro), the not-listed banner cut to two short sentences, and the holder-table alert cut from ~50 to ~30 words (title kept). Re-checked: `tsc`/`eslint` 0, rendered `/eligibility` and `/draws` DOM contain **0 em dashes**.

- **`/draws` honesty pass — the numbers now say what they mean (2026-10-25).** An audit of `/draws` produced 30 findings; the fact-and-copy half is now fixed (navigation was deliberately skipped). `DrawsList.tsx`: **"Paid out" only counts money that moved** (`w.payoutTx ? w.amountSol : 0` — the old sum added every winner's amount even when the payout failed); both it and the "Draws" count are captioned for what they actually cover (`settled rounds` / `SOL to winners`) with a footer under the stats card reading **"Showing up to 50 settled draws."**, because `/api/rounds` caps the list at 50 (`route.ts:32`) while the old caption said `total in SOL`. `CountUp` now formats with `fmtSolNum` so the caption owns the unit (`fmtSolHuman` already appends `" SOL"`; the tile used to read "0.0015 SOL … total in SOL"). "Draw interval" is captioned **median gap between rounds**, not "…between draws": `medianDrawIntervalMs()` diffs `startedAt` across every round including skipped ones (10 of 11 on mainnet), so it measures attempt cadence. A derived fee line (**`PayoutNote`**: `prizePoolSol − Σ winner amounts`) was built and then **removed at the user's request the same day** — the draw cards stay clean. The gross-vs-net gap (`prizePoolSol` is inflow, winner amounts are net of `PLATFORM_FEE_BPS`) is still unexplained publicly and belongs on `/how-it-works` step 4, which today mentions only rounding dust. Card copy: "Eligible wallets" → **"Eligible holders"** (matches `/`), the winners list heading → **"Winners and payouts"** (it duplicated the "Winners" stat directly above), and the no-beacon line now matches `RecentDraws`: **"No drand beacon was recorded for this draw."** instead of claiming the round "stopped before the draw step". Errors: raw `{r.error}` (JSON-RPC text like `InsufficientFundsForRent`) no longer paints the card — neutral sentence, raw string kept on `title` (same pattern as `failureReason`) — and the fetch/`dbError` branches show generic sentences instead of `err.message` / Prisma text. `page.tsx`: H4 "Draws" → **"Draw history"**, and the subtitle is now **one 16-word line in the homepage's voice**: **"Each round, eligible holders are drawn at random using drand — public randomness no one here controls."** — it mirrors home's "Each round, N holders are drawn at random and paid in SOL." An earlier draft quoted `/verify` ("a public beacon run by independent organisations") and appended the merged-share rule; **both sentences were cut on 2026-10-25 as fine print**. The merged-share fact still matters (`selectWinners` folds unfilled ranks into rank 1, `engine.ts:764-768`: round #10 paid rank 1 ~70% of the pool) but belongs on `/how-it-works` step 4 "The prize is split by rank", next to the rounding-dust line — not in the `/draws` intro. **Auto-refresh:** `fetchRounds(silent)` + a 15 s `setInterval` (same window as the API cache, skipped while `document.hidden`) keeps "Latest draw" honest and picks up settled rounds without flashing skeletons; a failed background refresh keeps the last good snapshot and a failed first load self-heals on the next tick. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0**, `npx eslint src/components/DrawsList.tsx src/app/draws/page.tsx` → **0**; SSR/headless probes of `GET /draws` → "Draw history" 1×, "public randomness no one here controls" 1×, retired "unfilled shares" / "rank 1." / "Winners received" 0×; client strings compile into `.next/dev/static/chunks/app/draws/page.js`, while `"total in SOL"`, `"in this list"`, `"No randomness recorded"` and `"stopped before the draw step"` survive only inside stale `webpack/…hot-update.js` artifacts. **Still open:** nav wording (`Verify` → "Verify a draw", `Eligibility` → "Check eligibility") and the orphan `/live` + `/holders` pages (`/holders` 308s to `/eligibility`; `/live` is unlinked and still uses dead `text-default-400` / `border-default-200` classes); `rankBadge()` is still duplicated in 4 files; consecutive "Draw skipped" cards are not collapsed; the payout `aria-label` is identical on every row.

- **Token ticker is now one admin setting instead of a hardcoded `"PAY"` (2026-10-25).** The panel that already owned brand/SEO now owns the ticker too. `src/lib/seo.ts`: `SEO_KEYS.tokenName = "TOKEN_NAME"` (deliberately **not** `SEO_`-prefixed — it is a product setting read by pages/APIs, not metadata, but it lives in the same key/value store so the name is edited in ONE place), a `tokenName` field on `SiteSeo`, `normalizeTokenName()` (trims + drops a `$`, **keeps the case you typed**: `$drawfi` → `drawfi`) + `TOKEN_NAME_RE` (`^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,15}$`, a hand-edited row that fails it falls back to the default), and **`getTokenName()`** — the single server accessor, wrapped in React `cache` and reusing `getSiteSeo()`'s query, so it adds no DB round-trip inside a request that already read SEO. Order: DB `TOKEN_NAME` → env `TOKEN_NAME` → `"PAY"`. **Client surface:** `SiteProvider`'s `SiteBrand` carries `tokenName` (local `"PAY"` fallback — importing `SEO_DEFAULTS` would pull Prisma into the client bundle), the root layout passes `seo.tokenName` down, and `useSite()` replaces the hardcoded ticker in `src/app/page.tsx` (hero “Hold {token}”, min-balance chip + prose), `eligibility/page.tsx`, `how-it-works/page.tsx` and `treasury/page.tsx`. **Metadata:** `eligibility/layout.tsx`, `holders/layout.tsx` and `how-it-works/layout.tsx` export async `generateMetadata()` that reads `getTokenName()` for their descriptions, and `/api/eligibility` returns `tokenName` from the same helper instead of a literal. Admin field is a plain “Token name” `TextField` in **Brand & headings** (no auto-uppercase — `DRAWFI`, `DrawFi` and `drawfi` are stored and displayed verbatim, placeholder = default); `/api/admin/seo` already round-trips every `SEO_KEYS` entry, so no new endpoint. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; build validated on a clean copy of the tree (own `.next`, `node_modules` junction) → `next build --webpack` compiled in **5.0 s**, 12/12 static pages, `BUILD_ID Z8CJtxncH6SnvOGzaw-FW`; the in-place `.next` still holds the old `8ailukWaP0e7pAHKvSDQx` because `:3000` is owned by a dev-server process this shell cannot stop — run `npm run build` with dev stopped before deploying. Smoke test on the running dev server → `/`, `/draws`, `/eligibility`, `/how-it-works`, `/treasury`, `/stats`, `/verify`, `/admin` **200**, `/holders` **308** → `/eligibility`. **Live DB proof:** `TOKEN_NAME=DRAWFI` (brand “DrawFi”, saved from the panel) renders as `<h1>Hold … DRAWFI … Win the draw.</h1>`, `/eligibility` says “minimum DRAWFI”, and `/api/eligibility` returns `tokenName:"DRAWFI"` — `PAY` is gone from those surfaces. **Sweep closed:** the last hardcoded ticker (`BuybackPanel` supply hint) now uses `useSite().tokenName`; the admin login subtitle uses `siteTitle`; `HANDOFF.md` + `DEPLOY-VPS.md` document optional `TOKEN_NAME` (DB wins). Remaining `Pay`/`PAY`: the “Pay engine” brand step (`HowItWorksFlow`/`RecentDraws`), comments, `SEO_DEFAULTS`/`SiteProvider` fallbacks, the `pay.fun` example URL and the word “payout”. **Case is free-form (no forced uppercase):** `DrawFi` round-tripped through `POST /api/admin/seo` and rendered verbatim in the hero (`Hold DrawFi`, `DRAWFI` count on `/` → 0), in `/api/eligibility` (`tokenName:"DrawFi"`) and in the eligibility result card (`data.tokenName` is no longer `.toUpperCase()`); `$drawfi` saved as `drawfi`; `a b` → **400** with the field error and no write; the setting was restored to `DRAWFI` afterwards. `git` still unavailable, so no diff.

- **Drawer mobile sekarang beranimasi, bukan pop (2026-10-25).** `src/components/AppShell.tsx` dan salinan sidebar di `src/app/page.tsx`: (1) overlay selalu ter-mount → `opacity-0 ↔ opacity-100` 300 ms `ease-out` + `backdrop-blur-[2px]` saat buka, `pointer-events-none` saat tutup, jadi fade-nya kelihatan dua arah; (2) panel slide `-translate-x-full ↔ translate-x-0` 300 ms `cubic-bezier(.22,.61,.36,1)` + `shadow-2xl shadow-black/40` yang dimatikan `lg:shadow-none`; (3) link nav masuk bertahap lewat `@keyframes drawer-item-in` baru di `globals.css` (delay `70 ms + 22 ms/baris`, cap 190 ms, hanya <1024 px, otomatis mati di `prefers-reduced-motion`); (4) tombol close X ditambahkan di header drawer AppShell (home sudah punya), plus Escape menutup, body scroll lock, tap backdrop menutup, dan resize ke ≥1024 px menutup drawer supaya body tidak tetap terkunci. **Gotcha yang bikin animasi awal tidak jalan:** Tailwind v4 meng-compile `translate-x-*` ke properti **`translate`**, bukan `transform` — jadi transisi wajib `transition-[width,translate]` (+ `will-change-[width,translate]`); `transition-[…,transform]` tidak menganimasikan apa pun. **Verified (CDP, headless Chrome):** 375×812 → `aside.left` **−240 → −109 → −40 → −11 → 0** px saat buka dan **0 → −131 → −240** saat tutup, backdrop **0 → 0.54 → 1 → 0**, `animation-name: drawer-item-in` aktif di link, `body.style.overflow` `hidden` hanya saat buka; 320×568 tanpa overflow horizontal, tombol close 32×32 terlihat, `nav` `overflow-y:auto`; desktop 1280 px → panel di `0`, `translate: 0px`, shadow transparan, backdrop `display:none`; `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` → `transition-property: none`. `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**, `npm run build` clean, 7 rute **200**.
- **VPS: `fetch failed (ETIMEDOUT)` hilang, kartu Creator Fees normal lagi (2026-10-24).** Fix IPv4-first diterapkan di `root@DrawFi:/var/www/pay` dan user mengonfirmasi kartu Admin → Creator Fees kembali berfungsi ("sekarang bisa kok"). Diagnosis happy-eyeballs tertutup: bukan firewall/MTU/proxy. Untuk build baru tidak ada flag yang dibutuhkan — hook instrumentation yang mengaturnya dan mencetak `[net] ipv4-first aktif, family auto-selection dimatikan` saat start; `NODE_OPTIONS=--dns-result-order=ipv4first --no-network-family-autoselection` tetap didokumentasikan di `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §8a/§8c hanya sebagai setara untuk build lama. Kalau mau memastikan mekanismenya: `journalctl -u pay -n 40 | grep '\[net\]'` (ada baris → hook bekerja; tidak ada tapi kartu normal → ada yang masih memakai flag).
- **`fetch failed (ETIMEDOUT)` root cause found & fixed in code: Node happy-eyeballs, not the firewall (2026-10-24).** A 4-variant probe on the VPS settled it: `lookup` returns A `80.78.25.150` then an AAAA the host cannot route; `autoSelectFamily=true`, `attemptTO=250`; **A default → `ETIMEDOUT` in 304 ms**, **B `ipv4first` → still `ETIMEDOUT` in 252 ms** (aggregate errors `ETIMEDOUT@80.78.25.150` + `ENETUNREACH@2a0a:…`), **C `ipv4first` + `setDefaultAutoSelectFamily(false)` → HTTP 200 in 899 ms**, **D `ipv6first` → 200 in 970 ms** (only because IPv6 fails instantly). Reading: the IPv4 handshake to pumpdev takes ~0.9 s, the 250 ms attempt budget aborts it, the IPv6 attempt then dies with `ENETUNREACH`, and Node reports the aborted-but-healthy IPv4 attempt as `ETIMEDOUT` — consistent with `curl -4` needing 1.68 s. `--dns-result-order=ipv4first` alone is therefore **not** a fix. New **`src/lib/net-ipv4.ts`** (`preferIpv4()` — dynamic `node:dns`/`node:net` imports, `NET_PREFER_IPV4=0` escape hatch) is awaited from **`src/instrumentation.ts`** before `startScheduler()`, so every server `fetch` (creator fees, drand, Helius, Prisma/Neon) runs IPv4-only from the first request; it logs `[net] ipv4-first aktif, family auto-selection dimatikan`. Verified: `npx tsx` probe of the module → `autoSel true→false`, order `verbatim→ipv4first`, `fetch https://pumpdev.io` **200 in 836 ms**; `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**, `eslint` **0** on both files. `DEPLOY-VPS.md` updated accordingly (§5.2 `NET_PREFER_IPV4`, §8a/§8b/§8c flags now both, §13.3 probe + real output + "C works ⇒ solved" reading, §14 checklist).
- **`npm run net:test` — diagnosa lapisan jaringan untuk `fetch failed` (2026-10-24).** `src/scripts/netTest.ts` memeriksa, per host: `dns.resolve4/6` → TCP connect IPv4 **dan** IPv6 (timeout lokal 8 s, supaya tidak menggantung) → handshake TLS dengan SNI asli (verifikasi sertifikat aktif) → bila gagal, handshake pembanding `SNI=example.com` (membedakan penyaringan **nama domain** dari port) → request HTTPS sebenarnya. Target = host `PUMPFUN_API_BASE`, host `SOLANA_RPC_URL` (hanya hostname, API key tidak pernah dicetak), dan host kontrol `NET_TEST_CONTROL` (default `github.com`); diakhiri verdict yang memisahkan DNS gagal / egress diblok / SNI filtering / TLS-jam-server / IPv6 blackhole. Tanpa dependency baru (`node:net`, `node:tls`, `node:dns`). Dipakai di `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §13.3 (termasuk panduan khusus `ETIMEDOUT`) dan §15. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `eslint` 0, dijalankan lokal → `pumpdev.io` A=80.78.25.150/AAAA ada, `tcp4 194ms`, `tls TLSv1.3 cert pumpdev.io`, `HTTP 400`.
- **`fetch failed` tidak lagi menjadi pesan buntu (2026-10-24).** `src/lib/pumpfun/creator-fees.ts` sekarang punya `describeFetchError()` yang membuka `cause` undici: kartu Admin → Creator Fees menampilkan mis. `pumpdev unreachable: fetch failed (ENOTFOUND — getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND pumpdev.io)` atau `(CERT_NOT_YET_VALID …)` alih-alih `fetch failed` saja — perbedaan antara DNS, egress 443 yang diblok, dan jam server yang meleset jadi terlihat tanpa akses SSH. Dipakai di GET snapshot **dan** POST claim (yang sebelumnya meneruskan `e.message` mentah ke `explainSendError`), plus `apiBase()` kini `.trim()` supaya spasi tak sengaja di `PUMPFUN_API_BASE` tidak menghasilkan URL invalid. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `eslint` 0, dan uji langsung dengan host palsu → `fetch failed (ENOTFOUND — getaddrinfo ENOTFOUND pumpdev.invalid)`. Prosedur diagnosis ada di `DEPLOY-VPS.md` §13.3.
- **`DEPLOY-VPS.md`: panduan install ke VPS Ubuntu (2026-10-24).** Satu dokumen 15 bagian dari VPS kosong → domain HTTPS: prasyarat (Node ≥ 20.9 / 22 LTS, 2 vCPU + 2 GB RAM), `apt` + user `pay` + **TZ UTC wajib** (node-cron pakai TZ server, UI menjanjikan UTC), swap untuk VPS 1 GB, `ufw`, NodeSource, transfer kode (git/rsync), tabel 28 env key (wajib vs default, nilai nyata dari `.env` dev), `npm ci --include=dev` → `prisma generate` → `tsc --noEmit` → `next build --webpack`, unit **systemd** (satu proses = satu scheduler, `HOSTNAME=127.0.0.1`, `EnvironmentFile=-…`) + alternatif PM2 fork-mode, nginx proxy + certbot, verifikasi (`/api/status` 200, `/api/admin/palette` **401**), alur update/rollback, backup `data/buyback.json` + Neon, tabel troubleshooting, checklist, dan lampiran skrip manual. Tiga jebakan didokumentasikan eksplisit: (a) **di `NODE_ENV=production` cron draw SELALU aktif** — `LOTTERY_CRON_ENABLED` tidak mematikan apa pun, jadi kontrolnya lewat `LOTTERY_CRON`; (b) cookie admin `secure` → login `/admin` butuh HTTPS; (c) `NEXT_PUBLIC_SOLANA_RPC_URL` di-inline saat build, bukan runtime.
- **Admin → Palette: every theme color is now editable at runtime (2026-10-24).** New `/admin` tab backed by **`src/lib/palette.ts`** (+ client-safe **`src/lib/palette-css.ts`**), **`src/app/api/admin/palette/route.ts`** and **`src/components/admin/PalettePanel.tsx`**. 31 HeroUI semantic tokens in 3 groups, each with a light + dark hex value defaulting to the sRGB equivalent of the `globals.css` oklch values, so re-saving a default writes nothing. Overrides persist as **one JSON row** in the existing key/value `AdminSetting` table (`THEME_PALETTE`) — **no Prisma migration** — and only non-default values are stored; the root layout injects them as `<style id="theme-palette">` (only when non-empty) using `html[data-theme="light"|"dark"]` so they outrank `globals.css` regardless of document order. Every value is re-validated at render time, so a hand-edited DB row can't inject CSS. Panel UX: grouped rows with swatch + hex field + per-row `Reset`, search, dirty-count chip, `Discard changes`, `Save palette`, `Reset to defaults` (confirm → DELETE, row gone → defaults), inline 400 errors, and an instant preview via `<style id="palette-preview">` that emits the **full effective palette** (draft over defaults) so a per-row reset still wins over a stale server block on statically cached pages. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, eslint on the 6 touched files → **0 problems**, `npm run build` → **BUILD_EXIT_0**; lib-level script → 22/22 (hex normalisation incl. CSS-injection rejection, default-dropping, sparse persistence, invalid POST writing nothing, clear idempotent, DB left clean); headless-Chrome/CDP pass → **22/22** (login, `/admin` hydrates, Palette tab present, 62 swatches, live repaint of `--muted` before save, `#theme-palette` untouched before save, save → `GET /` HTML carries `--muted:#123456`, API reports it, per-row reset restores `#9fa0a2`, reset-all → `/` HTML has no palette tag, fresh `/admin` shows "All defaults"). Unauthenticated API calls → **401**. One deviation from the plan: the panel does **not** call `router.refresh()` after save/reset — on the statically cached `/admin` that re-rendered the whole route, clearing the success notice and unmounting the panel; the API's `revalidatePath("/", "layout")` already makes every other route fresh on its next request.
- **`/admin` → Settings is now a read-only `.env` summary — the DB override store is gone (2026-10-24).** `src/lib/settings.ts` no longer touches `AdminSetting`: `getOverrideSync` / `ensureOverridesLoaded` / `refreshOverrides` / `applyOverrides` / `clearOverrides` are deleted, `effectiveValue()` is env → built-in default, and `getConfig()` in `src/lib/config.ts` reads `process.env` directly (new `envOr()` helper keeps an empty-string env on the default, so the summary and the engine can't disagree). `getSettingsView()` now returns `source: "env" | "default"` per row plus a `problem` string from `validateSetting` / `validateSettingsTogether` (bad cron, shares not summing to 1, winner-count ≠ share count), and `GET /api/admin/settings` is the only method left — `POST` / `DELETE` return **405**. The Settings tab renders a read-only **Project Settings** card: label + `env`/`default` chip, env var name, value in a mono chip, distribution percentage chips and a "restart after editing `.env`" footnote; the **Danger zone** delete card is unchanged and the old Save / Reset / per-key Reset controls are gone. `ensureOverridesLoaded()` was also removed from `lib/scheduler.ts`, `api/admin/info` and `api/wallets`. Config that only existed in the DB was carried into `.env` (`MIN_TOKEN_ELIGIBLE=100000000000`, `PRIZE_RESERVE_LAMPORTS=1300000`, `PLATFORM_FEE_BPS=500`, `MAX_PRIZE_SOL=1`, `MIN_PRIZE_SOL=0`), preserving the last effective values. Out of scope, still on `AdminSetting`: SEO (`SEO_*`, incl. the favicon) and the creator-fee auto-claim toggle (`CLAIM_AUTO_ENABLED`) — different tabs. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, `eslint` on the 7 touched files → **0 problems**, `GET /api/admin/settings` → **200** with all 10 rows `source: "env"` and the values above, `POST` / `DELETE` → **405**.

- **App Info shows SOL at 6 decimals; "delete draws" now clears creator-fee claim history (2026-10-24).** (1) **6 dp on App Info only.** New `SOL_ADMIN_DIGITS = 6` in `src/lib/format.ts` (documented next to `SOL_BALANCE_DIGITS`; public pages stay on `SOL_DIGITS = 4`, so their columns keep the stable 4-decimal width). - **Admin Creator Fees card rebuilt around the real cost model, with claim history as a table (2026-10-24).** The old card stacked pots, chips, fee text and up to 5 claim rows in wrapped flex lines — once a row carried a `SendTransactionError` the block turned into an unreadable wall. New layout in `admin/CreatorFeesCard.tsx`: headline pot (`0.0011` + `curve … · pumpSwap …`) → **4-stat strip** (`Claim fee` = `5,000` base + priority, `Min wallet balance` = fee + rent-exempt, `Net if claimed` green/red, `Signing wallet` with `covers fee + rent` / `needs ≥ …`) → payout + `signer shortAddr` line → actions → **`Claim history` table** (`When` with `timeAgo` sub-line, `Amount`, `Source`, `Status` chip, `Note`, `Tx` Solscan link). `shortNote()` strips the `Simulation failed. Message: … Catch the \`SendTransactionError\`…` boilerplate so the cell shows the actual cause (full string in `title`, 2-line clamp); confirmed rows show `→ recipient`. Empty state is a one-liner, table wrapper is `overflow-x-auto` (420px viewport: card 357px, table 697px, page body has no horizontal overflow), grid is 1/2/4 columns at base/`sm`/`xl`. `getRecentClaims(5)` → **`getRecentClaims(8)`** so the table has a little history. Claim/auto messages now carry a `view tx` link when the response includes a signature. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `npx eslint` on the file **0 problems**; headless Chrome (CDP) on `/admin` with the real API → 1 table, headers `WHEN/AMOUNT/SOURCE/STATUS/NOTE/TX`, 5 rows on the dev run (`0.0007 failed "Transaction not confirmed within 60s"`, `0.0002 confirmed → 3LfBsn…`, two rent failures, one pumpdev 400) — the production build (`next start` on **:3100**, built from this code) renders the same single table with **6 rows** and the header line `6 recent attempts · 2 confirmed (0.0016 SOL)`; 4 stat cells at 242px, table 990px in a 1024px card, `overflow-x: auto` with no scroll; at **420px** the grid is one 325px column, the 697px table scrolls inside its 357px card, `document.body` has no horizontal overflow and the console is clean apart from a missing `favicon.ico`; stubbed payloads (CDP `Fetch.fulfillRequest`) → healthy `Claim now` **enabled** net `+0.000244`, pot `0.0002` **disabled** + `"Claimable 0.0002 SOL is below the 0.000505 SOL claim fee — claiming now would lose SOL."`, wallet `0.0001` **disabled** + `"Platform wallet needs at least 0.001500 SOL (0.000505 SOL fee + rent-exempt reserve) to pay the claim."`, pot `0` **disabled** + `"Nothing to claim yet …"`.
- **Creator-fee claims: the "insufficient funds for rent" simulation failure can no longer happen (2026-10-24).** Trigger: two manual claims (pot **0.0002 SOL** and **0.0001 SOL**) failed with `Transaction results in an account (0) with insufficient funds for rent. Logs: []`. Those logs are empty because the runtime rejects the tx **before execution**: a tx may not leave the fee payer below the **rent-exempt floor** (`0.00065024 SOL`, 0-byte account), and the only guard was `CLAIM_MIN_WALLET_SOL=0.0001` while the real fee is **0.000505 SOL** (`5,000` base + `0.0005` priority — confirmed by `meta.fee` on the successful claim `5vEiadB2…`). Nothing was lost: the failed txs never landed and burned no SOL. Fix in `src/lib/pumpfun/creator-fees.ts`: `claimFeeLamports()` (base + priority), `getRentExemptLamports()` (`getMinimumBalanceForRentExemption(0)`, cached 1h, 1M-lamport fallback), guard = `max(CLAIM_MIN_WALLET_SOL, fee + rent)`; also refuse a pot **≤ the fee** (claiming 0.0002 for 0.000505 is a loss) and translate the raw `SendTransactionError` via `explainSendError()`. `CreatorFeeStatus.costs` (`feeLamports`, `rentExemptLamports`, `requiredBalanceLamports`, `netLamports`) now drives `CreatorFeesCard` — it prints the claim fee + real minimum balance and disables `Claim now` with the reason ("pot below fee" / "seed the wallet"). Auto-claim ticks that hit either floor are silent skips (unchanged scheduler). **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` 0, `npm run build` OK, `GET /admin` 200; real-config status → fee **505,000**, rent **650,240**, required **1,155,240** lamports (wallet **0.002034513 SOL**), pot **748,917** → net **+243,917**; guard proven twice against a local pumpdev mock: (a) pot 0.001 SOL < fee → refused, (b) `CLAIM_PRIORITY_FEE_SOL=0.01` with a 0.02 SOL pot → "needs 0.010655 SOL … holds 0.002034513", and the mock never received the POST (no tx built). `.env`: `CLAIM_MIN_WALLET_SOL` raised from `0.0001` to **`0.0015`** (fee 0.000505 + rent 0.00065024 + buffer); `CLAIM_MIN_SOL=0.0005` is still below the fee floor, so raise it to ≥ **0.002** if auto-claim should ever fire.
- **Draw exclusion list ("blacklist") — venue accounts can no longer be counted or drawn (2026-10-24).** Trigger: the largest "holder" `CxQM…iubp` = **996.8M PAY** is not a person — it is the token's **pump.fun bonding curve** (derived: PDA `["bonding-curve", mint]` on `6EF8rrecthR5Dkzon8Nwu78hRvfCKubJ14M5uBEwF6P`, verified offline against `TOKEN_MINT`), and it actually **won rank 1 in round #1**. Semantics: exclusions are removed from the candidate pool **before** the drand-seeded shuffle, so an excluded address gets no index and the beacon *cannot* pick it — it is never "skipped by randomness".
  **(1) Schema.** `ExcludedWallet { address @id, label?, createdAt, updatedAt }` + `LotteryRound.excludedAddresses String[]` (the exact list used, snapshotted per round). Applied with `prisma db push`; client regenerated.
  **(2) One source of truth.** New `src/lib/exclusions.ts` — `getDrawExclusions(platformAddress)` = platform wallet + **venue accounts** (`getVenueAccounts()` derives the curve + PumpSwap `pool-authority` PDAs from `TOKEN_MINT`, so graduation is covered with no admin action) + the manual list. The engine (`getEligibleHolders(cfg.minTokenEligible, excludedAddresses)`), `/api/status` (new **`excludedCount`**), `/api/holders` and `/api/admin/holders` (per-row `excluded`, `excludedTotal`) all use it, so every reported count equals the draw pool.
  **(3) History stays verifiable.** The engine records the list on **completed and skipped** rounds; `GET /api/rounds/[n]` rebuilds the pool from that snapshot and returns `excludedAddresses` + `exclusionSource: "recorded" | "platform-only"`. Rounds drawn before the column existed (empty array) fall back to platform-only — the CURRENT blacklist is **never** applied retroactively, so round #1 still reproduces its 2 winners (curve included) rank-for-rank. `/rounds/[n]` gained a **Wallets excluded** field and copy explaining that the seed only reorders the pre-filtered list; `verify.ts`'s header documents the same.
  **(4) Admin UI.** New **Exclusions** tab (`src/components/admin/ExclusionsCard.tsx`, API `src/app/api/admin/exclusions/route.ts`): add address + optional label (base58-validated, deduped, ≤500, label ≤80), remove, an **auto** block listing the derived venue PDAs, and **Detected program-owned accounts** — current holders that are off the ed25519 curve (`isProgramOwnedAccount`, `PublicKey.isOnCurve` static) with a one-click Exclude. Holders table + `/holders` + `HoldersBrowser` now chip `excluded` (warning) instead of calling those rows "below min".
  **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `npm run build` → compiled + TypeScript + 12 static pages OK (`BUILD_ID` written); live dev server: eligible **4 → 3** and `excludedCount 1` with **0** manual rows (the curve is excluded automatically), `/api/holders` row `excluded=true`, `/api/rounds/1` unchanged (`platform-only`, 2 eligible, 2 winners); HTTP admin round-trip (login → GET → POST → GET → DELETE) returned counts to 4/0; in-process handler tests covered **401** unauth, invalid address **400**, duplicate **400**, unknown delete **404**, label trim, and the candidate pool shrinking by exactly one when a holder is excluded.

- **Holder counts now mean "live snapshot", eligibility now matches the draw pool, and a holder shortage is a recorded skip (2026-10-24).** Three fixes after the admin tile disagreed with the holders tab (tile **4** vs. **2** live / **2** eligible, while the draw could only pick **1**).
  **(1) Root cause — two different holder sources.** `Holder` is cumulative history (`sync.ts` `createMany({ skipDuplicates: true })`; a wallet that sells out and closes its token account is **never deleted**), while `HolderSnapshot` holds one row per wallet per sync batch. `/api/status` reported `totalHolders = prisma.holder.count()` (all-time → 4) and `eligibleCount =` snapshot rows `>= threshold` **without** excluding the platform wallet (→ 2). Live reality at the time: first batch (18:20Z) had **4** wallets — `j7Zb…DEZ` 3.56M PAY and `3jUt…RrYm` 342.7k PAY sold out and vanished, `CxQM…iubp` grew to 998.2M, `3LfB…E47v` = **platform wallet** 1.788M. Latest batch (18:35Z) → **2** holders: one real holder + the platform wallet.
  **(2) API fixes.** `GET /api/status` now computes `totalHolders` / `eligibleCount` from the latest snapshot batch (per-wallet sums) and excludes the platform wallet (`getPlatformKeypair()` in a try/catch) exactly like `getEligibleHolders()`; the cumulative count is exposed separately as **`knownHolders`**. `GET /api/admin/holders` and `GET /api/holders` now return `platformAddress`, per-row `platform: boolean` (admin) and `knownTotal` (admin), and compute `eligible` as `amount >= threshold && address !== platformAddress`.
  **(3) UI.** `/admin` "Holders" tile shows the live count with a `knownHolders` "known all-time" caption; the Token Holders tab header reads `N current · M in draw` plus `known all-time`; rows for the platform wallet render a warning `platform` chip instead of a misleading `eligible`. `/live` replaces `Total holders` with `Holders (current)` + `Known all-time`. `/holders` shows a warning `Platform` chip.
  **(4) Engine shortage semantics.** `eligible.length === 0` → the round is now recorded **`skipped`** with `error: "No eligible holders in the latest snapshot - round skipped"` (was `completed` + 0 winners with no reason), still no drand request and no payout. `0 < eligible < winnerCount` was already safe (`selectWinners` → `min(winnerCount, n)`) and `computeWinnerAmounts` rolls every unassigned share into rank 1, so 100% of the distributable pool is paid: verified numerically (pool 1,000,000 lamports, fee 500 bps → distributable 950,000) → 3 eligible = 475,000/285,000/190,000, 2 eligible = 665,000/285,000, 1 eligible = 950,000. A `warn` log now states the shortfall so the round history explains the smaller winner list.
  **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; live dev server → `/api/status` `totalHolders=2 eligibleCount=1 knownHolders=4`, `/api/holders` `total=2 eligibleTotal=1` with `CxQM…` eligible and `3LfB…` flagged platform; admin login **200**, `/api/admin/holders` `total=2 eligibleTotal=1 knownTotal=4 platformAddress=3LfB…`, `/admin` **200** and its page chunk compiles (**200**). `npm run lint` remains broken project-wide (Next 16 dropped `next lint`).


- **Creator-fee claim system (2026-10-24).** New admin card at the top of `/admin` → App Info (above `LiveStatusCard`) that shows the pump.fun creator fees claimable through pumpdev, with a manual **Claim now** and a persisted **Auto-claim: ON/OFF** toggle + a 10-minute cron. Pieces: **Prisma `CreatorFeeClaim`** (`amountLamports` + curve/pumpSwap snapshot, `recipient`, `source` manual|auto, `status` pending|confirmed|failed, `signature` unique, `error`; indexes on `createdAt`/`status`) — table already applied to the Neon DB with `prisma db push` (additive only); **`src/lib/pumpfun/creator-fees.ts`** (see Lib); **`GET /api/admin/creator-fees`**, **`POST .../claim`**, **`POST .../auto`**; **`src/components/admin/CreatorFeesCard.tsx`**; **`scheduler.ts`** registers the claim cron whenever config is ready and `network === "mainnet"` (devnet logs `claim="n/a (devnet)"`), but each tick re-reads `CLAIM_AUTO_ENABLED` from `AdminSetting`, so the card's toggle applies on the next tick without a restart — a boot-time registration restart **is** needed once for the new cron itself. Env (appended to `.env`): `PUMPFUN_API_BASE=https://pumpdev.io`, `CLAIM_CRON=*/10 * * * *`, `CLAIM_AUTO_ENABLED=false`, `CLAIM_MIN_SOL=0.05` (auto threshold; manual ignores it), `CLAIM_MIN_WALLET_SOL=0.01` (fee headroom), `CLAIM_PRIORITY_FEE_SOL=0.0005` (pumpdev default, SOL). **Live state:** claimable **0.501984577 SOL** (all curve, not graduated), `hasFeeSharing=true` with **100% → `HfYNP…pY6j`**, so claimed fees currently do **not** land on the platform wallet (`CsRj…NHkE`, **0.000995 SOL**) — card shows the amber "pays → …" warning, and the claim button is refused server-side until the wallet holds **≥ 0.01 SOL** for network + priority + pumpdev commission. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `npx eslint` on all new/changed files **0 problems**; live e2e against the dev server → anon GET **401**, login **200**, GET **200** (claimable/recipient/balance/auto parsed correctly), POST claim **400** `"Platform wallet holds 0.0010 SOL — at least 0.0100 SOL is needed to pay the claim fee. Seed the platform wallet first."` with **no tx sent**, auto ON **200** → auto OFF **200** (DB row reverted to `false`), and the `/admin` client chunk compiles + contains `CreatorFeesCard`.

- **Home draw panel: the drand `Verified` chip now carries an icon (2026-10-24).** `DrandBeacon.tsx` rendered `Verified` / `Not verified` as a text-only `Chip`, while every other status chip in the app has a glyph (`DrawsList`'s `StatusChip`, `/verify`'s `RoundStatusChip`). It now matches that pattern: `<CircleCheck size={14} aria-hidden />` when verified, `<Clock size={14} aria-hidden />` when not (warning colour = beacon pending/failed), with the label moved into `<Chip.Label>` (HeroUI v3 slot API) and both icons imported from lucide-react.
