- **SOL is 4 decimals everywhere; the two treasury wallet cards keep 6 (2026-10-22).** One rule, one helper: `src/lib/format.ts` now exports `SOL_DIGITS = 4` / `SOL_BALANCE_DIGITS = 6`, a new `fmtSolNum(sol, digits?)` (number → `"0.0013"`, no unit), and `fmtSolHuman` / `formatSol` gained an optional `digits` param that **defaults to 4**. Both previously used `maximumFractionDigits: 4`, which *trimmed* trailing zeros — that is why the same figure appeared as `0.001` on one page and `0.0013` on another, and why the payout table's `toFixed(4)` was the only fixed-width one. Every SOL **amount** (prize pool, est. 1st prize, payouts, platform fee, totals, buyback spend, the illustrative 0.5/0.3/0.2 split on `/how-it-works`) now renders exactly 4 decimals; only `WalletCard` in `src/app/treasury/page.tsx` (platform + fee headline balance, their Prize pool / Reserve / Lifetime fees facts, via local `fmtSolPrecise(sol, SOL_BALANCE_DIGITS)`) keeps 6, because a rent floor of 0.00065024 or a swept fee of 0.00007475 SOL vanishes at 4. **Dust guard:** a real but sub-precision value (the 5,000-lamport network fee = 0.000005 SOL) prints as **`<0.0001 SOL`** instead of a misleading `0.0000`; non-finite input prints `—` rather than `NaN SOL`. **Bugs fixed while sweeping:** `/admin` printed the unit twice (`${fmtSolHuman(x)} SOL` → `0.0012 SOL SOL`) in five places (last-draw prize pool + platform fee, winner payout cell, Total paid tile, Max/Min prize tiles, wallet "reserve" hint) and its per-round Payout fee / Platform fee tiles formatted raw lamports at 6 dp — all now go through `fmtSolHuman` / `formatSol(bigint)`; `/rounds/[n]` payout cell `w.amountSol.toFixed(4) + " SOL"` → `fmtSolHuman` (gain: thousands grouping, no hand-built unit); home's hero pool number was an inline `toLocaleString({maximumFractionDigits: 4})` → `fmtSolNum` (keeps its separate `SOL` label); admin Buyback preview paragraph 9 dp → `fmtSolNum`. **Verified:** `npx tsx` probe over the helper — `0.0013→"0.0013 SOL"`, `0.001→"0.0010 SOL"` (the reported 3-dp case), `1234.5→"1,234.5000 SOL"`, `0.000005→"<0.0001 SOL"`, `1.9649224 @6→"1.964922 SOL"`, `formatSol(5_000n)→"<0.0001 SOL"`, `NaN→"—"`; `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors; `npx eslint` on all seven touched files → 0 errors (1 pre-existing `<img>` warning in `HowItWorksFlow.tsx`); `npm run build` clean; UTF-8 re-read of all seven files → 0 replacement chars. **Not changed on purpose:** prose that names a round number ("about 0.05 SOL reserve", "1 SOL creator fee" on `/how-it-works`), PAY token amounts (`fmtTokenHuman`, `HoldersBrowser` threshold), and server-side log/email text (`engine.ts` `.toFixed(4)/(6)`, `notifications.ts`) — those are operator/email strings, not UI cells.
- **Treasury wallet cards — address is plain text, only the corner arrow opens Solscan (2026-10-22).** The platform and fee wallet strips used to be a single `<a>` around the whole 44-char base58 address, so *any* click (including an attempt to select or copy it) navigated away. `WalletCard` in `src/app/treasury/page.tsx` now renders `<div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md bg-default px-2.5 py-1">` with the address as a plain `<span className="min-w-0 flex-1 select-all break-all font-mono text-xs leading-5 text-foreground">` (click = select whole address, copy by hand, no hover colour) and a separate **20×20 `<a>` holding only `<Icon name="arrow-up-right" />`** pushed to the right edge by `flex-1` + `shrink-0 -mr-1.5`; `title` = full address + "open on the explorer", `aria-label` = `Open <label> address on the explorer`, `hover:text-accent`, `focus-visible` ring. The `PolicyCard` "Fee destination" row keeps its inline `shortAddress` link — it sits mid-sentence, so there is no corner to park an arrow in. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/treasury/page.tsx` → 0 problems; the dev-compiled chunk (`.next/dev/static/chunks/app/treasury/page.js`) contains `select-all` + `address on the explorer` and shows the `<a>` wrapping only the icon. **Gotcha:** the server on **:3000 is a production `next start`** (`.next/BUILD_ID` present), so source edits do not appear there until `npm run build` + restart — check UI work on a dev server on another port instead.


- **Payout expiry retried safely; tiny prizes now fail fast with a readable error (2026-10-22).** Rounds **#33** (18:00 cron) and **#34** (18:01:50 manual) died with `Signature 3HrKy… has expired: block height exceeded`. **It is not an amount/min-prize problem:** both signatures return `null` from `getTransaction` and from `getSignatureStatuses(searchTransactionHistory: true)`, i.e. the tx never landed and **no SOL left the platform wallet**, and the exact payout tx simulates clean (`simulation err: null`, 362 bytes, 149 blocks of blockhash validity left). `MIN_PRIZE_SOL` is currently **0** (`/api/status` → `prize.minPrizeSol: 0, playable: true`) and the below-minimum path marks a round **skipped** with `Prize pool X SOL below minimum Y SOL — round skipped`, never `failed` — so 0.000765 SOL is not "too little" for the round to run. Fixes in `distributePayouts` (`src/lib/lottery/engine.ts`): (1) **ordering** — the payer balance is read *before* `getLatestBlockhash`, so no RPC round-trip sits between fetching the blockhash and broadcasting (the old code signed, then did a `getBalance` round-trip, then sent); `maxRetries` 3 → **5**. (2) **Expiry is retried, once, and only when provably safe** — `isBlockhashExpiry()` catches `TransactionExpiredBlockheightExceededError` (plus a message check), then `fetchLandedTx()` (`getTransaction` with 3×400 ms retries) settles a tx that actually landed (its `meta.err` still throws; its fee is derived from the balance delta), `getSignatureStatuses` distinguishes "landed but unreadable — check an explorer" from "absent", and only an absent signature triggers a second attempt with a **fresh** blockhash (`PAYOUT_SEND_ATTEMPTS = 2`). An expired blockhash can never land, so the retry cannot double-pay. (3) **New `assertPrizesCanCreateWallets()` pre-check** — a prize below the RPC's `getMinimumBalanceForRentExemption(0)` (**0.00065024 SOL on mainnet today**, *not* the often-quoted 0.00089088) can't create a brand-new winner wallet, so those amounts are checked against one batched `getMultipleAccountsInfo`; if a winner wallet doesn't exist the round fails in ~200 ms with `payout would fail on-chain: <amount> SOL → <addr> is below the … rent-exempt minimum … nothing was sent` instead of landing a 5,000-lamport `InsufficientFundsForRent` tx (rounds **#3** / **#31**). Fee maths unchanged (`balanceBefore − balanceAfter − transferred`, so priority fees are included). `DrawsList.failureReason` now maps expiry → "The payout transaction expired before it could confirm — no SOL left the wallet…", the rent case → "A prize was too small to create a winner's wallet…", and "not retrievable" → an unconfirmed notice. **Practical floor for the 50/30/20 split:** the 20 % share must reach 0.00065024 SOL → pool ≥ 0.0032512 → set **MIN_PRIZE_SOL ≈ 0.0035** (0.005 is a rounder number). `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**.


- **`/treasury` header cards trimmed, "How the money moves" condensed (2026-10-22).** (1) **No chips on the two wallet cards**: the `Live` / `Treasury` / `Unavailable` / `Not configured` soft chips are gone — the balance (`—` when unknown) and the caption already carry that meaning, and a green "Treasury" chip next to a fee wallet that isn't even configured on this deployment was actively misleading. `WalletCard` lost the `chipLabel`/`chipColor` props; the header is now just `<h2>` (matching `BuybackCard`). `Chip` stays imported — the `Sample` chip in `TxCard` still uses it. (2) **Copy button removed**: `CopyButton` (icon-only, clipboard + check SVG, `setTimeout` reset) deleted along with its `Button` import and the wrapper `<div>` it lived in. The whole address strip is now a single anchor → Solscan, `title`/`aria-label` "open on the explorer", so the external glyph *is* the affordance (one click target instead of address-link + copy). (3) **`PolicyCard` condensed 5 rows → 3** and the sweep count dropped entirely (it only repeated the fee table): *Prize pool* (reserve), *Platform fee* (rate), *Fee destination* (buyback % + fee wallet link, or the no-key fallback). Footer copy shortened to "Every payout and fee sweep is a public Solana transaction. Verify a draw replays any round; how it works covers the fee maths." Now every card on the page shares one rhythm: `text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight` title, `text-xs text-muted` caption, mono numbers. **Gotcha:** splicing the old `CopyButton` out with PowerShell `Get-Content`/`Set-Content` double-encoded every non-ASCII char (— ’ ·) into mojibake, because PS 5.1 reads UTF-8 files as ANSI; re-pair fixed it with `ReadAllText(UTF8)` → `GetBytes(1252)` → `UTF8.GetString` → `WriteAllText(UTF8Encoding($false))`. Use the editor tool for non-ASCII files. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/treasury/page.tsx` → 0 problems, `GET /treasury` → **200**; chunk has `How the money moves` / `no separate key configured` / `open on the explorer` and **no** `Copy address`, `Sweeps recorded`, or `Not configured`; mojibake count back to **0**.
- **`/treasury` tables — no raw hashes, buyback spends in the fee ledger, friendlier fee copy (2026-10-22).** (1) **No signatures**: every `TX` cell now renders `TxLink` (new local component) = the literal `tx` + `arrow-up-right`, with `title`/`aria-label` "View transaction on the explorer" and the signature still in the href — an 88-char base58 string was noise that wrapped at 12px. Applies to the platform table, the fee table and the buyback table; `shortAddress()` is now only used for real addresses. (2) **Buyback shown in the fee table**: `feeTxs` appends one **negative** row per `kind: "buy"` leg out of `data/buyback.json` (label *Buyback purchase*), because that SOL leaves the fee wallet — without it the table implied 100% of fees stay put. Burn legs move tokens only (no SOL), so they stay in the Buyback & burn card; sample rows still only appear when nothing at all is recorded, never mixed with real ones. Card caption is now "platform fees swept here at payout time · buybacks spend out of it". (3) **Copy**: fee inflow labels are **"Earned from draw #N"** (was "Platform fee · draw #N") in both the live rows and `DEMO_FEE_TXS`; fee table `aria` = "Fee wallet activity — fees earned and buyback spends", empty note = "No fee sweep or buyback spend recorded yet." **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint` on both files → 0 problems, `GET /treasury` → **200**; treasury chunk contains `Earned from draw #42`, `Buyback purchase`, `View transaction on the explorer`, and contains **no** `Platform fee · draw` nor `shortAddress(t.signature`.
- **`/treasury` buyback follow-up — no "Last run" tile, colour-coded rows (2026-10-22).**
  *(Row tints superseded 2026-10-25 — the ledger is untinted again and the kind is shown by an
  icon chip; the rest of this entry still stands.)* (1) The 4th headline tile was dropped at the user's request: the strip is now `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3` (Bought / Burned / SOL spent) and the next-run date moved up into the card header (`1 completed run · next 12 Nov`) so nothing was lost — it just isn't a tile any more. `BuybackSummary.lastAt` is still computed by `summarizeBuyback()` but no longer rendered anywhere. (2) Ledger rows are tinted by kind: buy = soft green, burn = soft red, via `.tx-row--buy` / `.tx-row--burn` (className on `TableRow`) in `globals.css`. Deliberately **not** black — a black row reads as "disabled" and disappears in dark mode. Both are `color-mix(in oklab, var(--success|--danger) N%, transparent)` inside `@supports (color: color-mix(in lab, red, red))`: **without that guard Lightning CSS rewrites every `color-mix()` as `background: var(--success)` + an @supports override, i.e. a *solid* green row on browsers that can't mix** — with it, the only fallback is no tint (today's look). Hover steps to 18% / 16%, which also restores pointer feedback because an unlayered rule beats HeroUI's layered `.table__row` hover. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/treasury/page.tsx` → 0 problems, `GET /treasury` → **200**; served `layout.css` carries the `@supports` block verbatim; the treasury chunk contains `tx-row--buy`; `"Last run"` no longer appears in any dev chunk.
- **Buyback ledger moved to JSON + `/treasury` type back to 12px (2026-10-22).** User decision: the buys and burns are executed by hand, so a DB table + input form was overkill — the ledger is now a plain editable JSON file. **Data:** `data/buyback.json` = `{ feeSharePct, supply, nextRunAt, updatedAt, txs[] }`; a row is `{ signature, at, kind: "buy"|"burn", sol?, tokens }` with tokens **always positive** (`kind` supplies the sign, so nobody types a `-`). **Derived, never stored:** `summarizeBuyback()` computes bought / burned / burnedPct / SOL spent / runs (one per buy leg) / last run from the rows, so the headline strip can't disagree with the table. **Code:** `src/lib/buyback.ts` (types + validation + maths, client-safe), `src/lib/buyback-store.ts` (server-only: missing file → empty ledger, malformed JSON or failed validation → throws naming the bad keys, atomic temp-file + rename write, `EROFS`/`EACCES` → read-only message), **`/api/buyback`** (public GET `{ buyback, summary }`; admin PUT → 401 without the session cookie, 400 `{ errors }` keyed `txs.0.sol` etc., 500 + message otherwise). **Admin:** new **Buyback** tab (`admin/BuybackPanel.tsx`, sits between Token Holders and System Log) — fee share / supply / next run + add-buy / add-burn rows (`datetime-local` ↔ ISO in the browser's timezone), Remove per row, live "treasury will show …" preview, Unsaved chip, Reload. **`/treasury`:** fetches `/api/buyback` next to wallets/rounds (`buybackError` banner when the file is broken), `BuybackCard` takes `data` instead of `DEMO_BUYBACK` and drops the **Sample** chip (it is the operator's own ledger now), renders "No buyback recorded yet — add the buy and burn rows in Admin → Buyback." on an empty file, and `PolicyCard`'s "Buyback share" reads `feeSharePct` (Sample chip removed). `DEMO_BUYBACK` / `DEMO_BUYBACK_TXS` / `DemoBuybackTx` deleted from `demo-finance.ts` — only `DEMO_FEE_TXS` remains. **Type size:** `.table-compact .table__cell` is back to `font-size: var(--text-xs)` / `line-height: 1.45` by request — these are read-only analytics in half-width cards, so 12px is wanted even though `/eligibility` keeps 14px; both sub-line dates went back to `text-[10px]`. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors; live round-trip — login → `PUT` buy+burn → **200**, file rewritten with `updatedAt`, rows newest-first, `summary { bought 2046667, burned 1771111, burnedPct 1.77, spentSol 1.386, runs 1 }`; invalid body → **400** with `feeSharePct` / `txs.0.signature` / `txs.0.at` / `txs.0.tokens` / `txs.0.sol` errors and the file untouched; unauthenticated `PUT` → **401**; `/treasury` + `/admin` → **200**; served `layout.css` has `font-size: var(--text-xs)`; treasury chunk carries `/api/buyback` + the empty-state copy and **no** `DEMO_BUYBACK`. **Caveat:** serverless filesystems are read-only/ephemeral — the panel returns the read-only message there, so edit the file in the repo or run on a writable host. `/stats` and `/rounds/[n]` still use the `external` icon glyph.
- **`/treasury` table outbound icons aligned with `/eligibility` (2026-10-22; type-size half superseded above).** Two mismatches the page had drifted into. (1) **Cell type size:** `.table-compact` forced `font-size: var(--text-xs)` (**12px**), so treasury's mono values rendered a step smaller than the `/eligibility` / `/holders` table, whose cells keep HeroUI's default `.table__cell` **14px**. The rule now only owns geometry (12px inline / 8px block padding, 1.25rem outer so cells line up with the card's `px-5` header) and leaves `font-size: var(--text-sm)` / `line-height: 1.5` alone; both `TableContent`s dropped the inert `text-xs` in favour of `className="font-mono"` (HeroUI's layered `.table__cell` always beats an inherited size — the same reason `HoldersBrowser` never got 12px). The activity sub-line date went `text-[10px]` → `text-[11px]` so it sits one step under the 14px value instead of two. (2) **Icon glyph:** all four treasury outbound links used `Icon name="external"` (box with the arrow poking out) while the eligibility/holders table uses `arrow-up-right` (plain diagonal arrow), which is what the page was being compared against — `TxCard`'s tx cell, the buyback tx cell, the wallet address strip and `PolicyCard`'s fee destination now all use `arrow-up-right` at `h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted` (the strip's trailing icon was `h-3 w-3`, now 3.5). **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/treasury/page.tsx` → 0 problems; `GET /treasury` → **200**; the page chunk carries `arrow-up-right` and **zero** `"external"`; dev `layout.css` now serves `.table-compact .table__cell { … font-size: var(--text-sm); line-height: 1.5 }`. **Note:** `/stats` and `/rounds/[n]` still use the `external` glyph — those are the remaining outliers if one glyph is wanted app-wide.
- **`/treasury` decluttered: wallet-table footers + buyback chrome gone (2026-10-22).** (1) **`TxCard` lost its `note` prop** and the `px-5 py-3 text-xs` footer paragraph — the two long sentences ("One row per completed draw: the atomic payout tx…" and the three fee variants) restated what the rows already show, so each card is now just rows + pager. Sample disclosure survives as a tiny right-aligned `Sample` chip strip (`px-5 pt-3 pb-1`) driven by a new optional **`sample?: boolean`**, passed only by the fee table when it falls back to `DEMO_FEE_TXS` (`!feeTxs.live && feeTxs.draws === 0`), so `demo-finance.ts`'s "every consumer must label sample rows" rule still holds without a paragraph. (2) **`BuybackCard` rebuilt** from five stacked bands (title + blurb, four `BuybackTile`s, burn-share progress bar, placeholder paragraph) down to **header + headline figures + combined buy / burn table**: title + `Sample` chip on the left with `9 completed runs` on the right, then a hairline stat strip (`grid grid-cols-2 gap-px border-y bg-(--border) sm:grid-cols-4`, the `/stats` tile pattern) built from a new page-local **`BuybackStat`** (`dt` label / `dd` `text-xl sm:text-2xl` number + muted caption) — **Bought 18,420,000** (`PAY tokens`), **Burned 15,940,000** (`1.27% of supply`), **SOL spent 12.482 SOL** (`in 9 runs`), **Last run Oct 20** (`next Oct 27`, new `fmtDayMonth` helper pinned to `timeZone: "UTC"` so SSR and hydration agree) — because the first pass's single inline sentence (`9 runs · … · burned`) made the totals too small to read, then a 4-column table **`ACTIVITY | SPENT | TOKENS | TX`** — `Buyback` / `Burn` + `fmtTxDate`, `−1.386 SOL` (or `—` on burns), signed token amount in `text-success` / `text-foreground`, Solscan tx link. `BuybackTile` deleted; `feeSharePct` dropped from the summary because `PolicyCard` already states the 70% share. New sample rows **`DEMO_BUYBACK_TXS`** (+ exported `DemoBuybackTx` type) in `src/lib/demo-finance.ts`: two runs, burn row first (newest first), amounts matching `DEMO_BUYBACK`'s per-run averages; `BuybackCard` now takes `net` so links are cluster-correct. (3) **Outbound links aligned** with the rest of the project: tx links went `gap-1 text-muted` / `shortAddress(…,4,4)` / `h-3 w-3` → `gap-1.5 font-mono text-foreground hover:text-accent` / `6/6` / `h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted` (the `/rounds`, `/stats`, `/holders` pattern), and the wallet-address strip keeps the full inline-code address but now carries the same `external` icon (`h-3 w-3 shrink-0 text-muted`) so it reads as an outbound link beside the copy button. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/treasury/page.tsx src/lib/demo-finance.ts` → 0 problems; `GET /treasury` → **200**; served chunk `/_next/static/chunks/app/treasury/page.js` contains `Sample buyback and burn transactions` + `SPENT` and no longer contains `One row per completed draw` / `Fees in, supply out` / `Placeholder figures` / `sample:` tri-state leftovers.

Added a `landmark` case to `Icon` (`src/components/ui.tsx`) using **`Landmark` from lucide-react** (already a dependency; same `size={18} strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden` contract as the other lucide-backed icons), so the entry reads as the bank/treasury. Both sidebars — `AppShell` and the homepage's own `<nav>` — map `NAV`, so the single edit covers them; nothing else referenced `icon: "wallet"`. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/components/nav.ts src/components/ui.tsx` → 0 problems; rendered `/stats` HTML lists the nav anchors at offsets **eligibility (4157) → treasury (4750) → stats (5437) → verify (6000)** and carries `<svg class="lucide-landmark shrink-0">`; `/` and `/treasury` render the same glyph. **Follow-up:** `How it works` swapped its hand-drawn info circle for **`circle-question-mark`** (`CircleQuestionMark` from lucide, same 18px/1.8 contract; the old `how` glyph stays in the icon set but nothing references it), since the label is a help entry. `Stats` moved to **`chart-line`** (`ChartLine`, plain line chart instead of `ChartSpline`); the old `chart` case was renamed rather than duplicated, so no dead icon branch remains. **Also removed the Treasury strip from `/stats`:** the `TreasuryStrip` section (heading, "View treasury" link, Platform balance / Lifetime fees / Fee rate cells), its skeleton, the `WalletOverview` type, the `/api/wallets` fetch with `wallets`/`walletError` state, and the now-unused `fmtSolPrecise` helper are gone (~142 lines), so the page is purely draw metrics and no longer calls `/api/wallets`. Re-verified: tsc 0 / eslint 0, `Treasury|api/wallets|fmtSolPrecise` in `src/app/stats/page.tsx` → **0 hits**, `/stats` renders `lucide-chart-line` and returns 200 (as do `/`, `/treasury`, `/draws`, `/how-it-works`).
- **`/treasury` tx tables: real payouts + 5-per-page pagination (2026-10-22).** The platform table was the last sample block on the page; it now comes straight from the draw ledger. **`platformTxs` memo** (`src/app/treasury/page.tsx`) keeps `status === "completed" && payoutTx` rows, one per draw, labelled `Prize payouts · draw #N`, amount = **−(Σ `winners[].amountSol`)** with a `prizePoolSol − fee` fallback for rows recorded before winner snapshots — those are provably the same figure (engine: `computeWinnerAmounts` deducts the fee, then `distributePayouts` sends one atomic tx from the platform wallet: winners + optional fee sweep). Newest first, dropped if the amount is 0. The card's note now explains the atomic payout tx, `sample={false}`, and its empty state reads "No payout yet — one row appears per completed draw." **`DEMO_PLATFORM_TXS` deleted** from `src/lib/demo-finance.ts` (header rewritten: only `DEMO_FEE_TXS` + `DEMO_BUYBACK` remain sample), so nothing fake can leak into the table. **Fee table honesty fix:** the old filter was `r.payoutTx && r.platformFeeLamports`, and since `"0"` is a truthy string every zero-fee draw produced a 0-amount row; it now requires `Number(platformFeeLamports) > 0`. Consequence: with paid draws but a 0% fee rate the table stays a *real* empty table ("These draws carried no platform fee — the fee rate is 0%") and only falls back to `DEMO_FEE_TXS` when **no** draw has been paid yet. **Pagination (both tables, `PAGE_SIZE = 5`):** `TxCard` owns `page` state, clamps with `Math.min(page, totalPages - 1)` (no reset effect — the parent passes memoized arrays, so an effect would fight their identity), slices `rows.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE)`, and renders a footer only when `totalPages > 1`: `1–5 of 12` on the left, `Prev` / `2/3` / `Next` on the right using the existing pager convention (plain `<button>`, `rounded-md border border-(--border) px-2 py-1 text-[11px]`, `hover:bg-default/40 disabled:opacity-40`, `Icon name="arrow-left"` + `rotate-180` for next — there is no chevron icon in `Icon`). Chip is now tri-state: **Sample** (warning) / **From draw ledger** (success) / **Awaiting draws** (default, empty+real), and `emptyNote` is a prop. **Update 2026-10-22:** that header row is gone — the user asked to drop the repeated “Platform transactions / From draw ledger” (and the fee twin) labels, so `TxCard` renders only table + pager + note. `title` and `sample` were removed from the component and both call sites (and with them the tri-state chip), `emptyNote` now pads `px-5 py-4` as the sole empty state, and the wrapper gained `overflow-hidden` so the flattened gray `th` corners clip to the card's `rounded-lg` now that nothing sits above them. The `note` line under each table still explains the rows, and `aria` still labels the `<TableContent>`. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint` on `treasury/page.tsx` + `demo-finance.ts` → 0 problems, `GET /treasury` → **200** with the new strings in `app/treasury/page.js`; live `/api/rounds` returns 30 rows (27 skipped, **2 completed**, 1 failed) and both completed rows satisfy Σwinners = pool − fee (`0.001495 − 0.00007475 = 0.00142025`, `0.00119 − 0.0000595 = 0.0011305`), so both tables show 2 real rows today; pager arithmetic simulated over 12 rows → `1–5` / `6–10` / `11–12` with Prev/Next correctly disabled at the ends.
- **Treasury split to `/treasury` (2026-10-22).** The ~350-line treasury block was lifted out of `src/app/stats/page.tsx` into a dedicated page so the draw metrics stay above the fold and "is the fee really used for buyback?" has a shareable URL. **New `src/app/treasury/page.tsx`** (client) owns `FinanceSection`'s former children verbatim — `WalletCard` (label + status chip, `text-xl sm:text-2xl … tracking-tight` precise balance, full address in a `rounded-md bg-default … font-mono text-xs break-all` strip with Solscan link + `CopyButton`, 2 `dl` facts), `TxCard` (`.table-bordered .table-compact`, `ACTIVITY / AMOUNT / TX`, inflow amount `text-success`, tx cell `shortAddress(sig,4,4)` + `Icon name="external"`), `BuybackCard` + `BuybackTile` (+ burned-share bar), `fmtSolPrecise` (≤9 dp), `fmtTxDate`, `CopyButton`, `TreasurySkeleton` — plus two new pieces: an `updated {generatedAt}` stamp in the header, and **`PolicyCard` "How the money moves"** (prize pool = balance − reserve, `feeBps` → `%` of each pool deducted before the split, fee destination wallet or "stays on the platform wallet" when no treasury key, `sweeps` count, buyback share with its own **Sample** chip, links to `/verify` + `/how-it-works`). Data: `/api/wallets` **and** `/api/rounds` (the latter only to rebuild fee rows — `payoutTx` *is* the fee signature because the sweep rides in the payout tx), each fetch fail-soft with its own banner; `feeTxs` memo is unchanged logic. Fix: copy button forced to `h-6 w-6` (utilities layer beats HeroUI's `components` layer), icon `h-3.5 w-3.5`, strip `items-center` + `py-1`, address link `leading-5`, variant `ghost` → `secondary` (ghost's hover bg is the same `--default` as the strip, so it gave no feedback). Strip is now **32 px** on desktop.
- **Admin → SEO & Analytics panel (2026-10-22).** New tab in `/admin` backed by **`src/components/admin/SeoPanel.tsx`**, storing overrides in the existing key/value `AdminSetting` table under `SEO_*` keys — **no Prisma migration**. Server module **`src/lib/seo.ts`**: `SiteSeo` (brandName, siteTitle, description, keywords, siteUrl, ogImage, twitterHandle, gaMeasurementId, allowIndexing, googleSiteVerification), `SEO_DEFAULTS` (env fallbacks `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` / `NEXT_PUBLIC_GA_ID`), `getSiteSeo()` wrapped in React `cache` and fail-soft (DB error → defaults), `normalizeSeoInput` / `validateSeo` / `saveSeo` (transactional upserts, all-or-nothing) / `clearSeo`. Guarded API **`/api/admin/seo`**: GET → `{ seo, defaults }`, POST → 400 + per-field `errors` or `{ applied }` + `revalidatePath("/", "layout")`, DELETE → reset. Root **`layout.tsx`** now exports `revalidate = 300` + `generateMetadata()`: title template `%s · {brand}`, default title, description, keywords, authors/creator/publisher, canonical + `og:url` only when `siteUrl` is set, OG/Twitter cards (image only if provided), robots index/noindex, google-site-verification. GA4 injected once with `next/script` (`afterInteractive`) plus inline `gtag('config')`, **only when the ID matches `^G-[A-Z0-9]{4,20}$`**. Brand is shared with client components via new **`src/components/providers/SiteProvider.tsx`** (`useSite()`), used for the sidebar wordmark/initial + breadcrumb in `AppShell.tsx` and the homepage's own sidebar. Per-route titles shortened to bare names so the template appends the brand (`How it works · Pay`); added layouts for `draws`, `eligibility`, `holders` and `admin` (admin = `noindex, nofollow`). **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint` on all touched files → 0 problems; live round-trip against the dev server: login → POST test values → home `<title>Pay Test Lottery</title>`, `robots noindex,nofollow`, canonical/`og:url` `https://example.com`, gtag `G-TEST12345` present, wordmark "Pay Test", `/how-it-works` → `How it works · Pay Test`; invalid `UA-12345` → **400** with `gaMeasurementId` error and nothing written; DELETE → defaults restored, GA tag gone, wordmark reverted; unauthenticated GET → **401**; `/draws` → `Draws · Pay`, `/stats` → `Stats · Pay`, `/verify` → `Verify · Pay`, `/admin` → `Admin · Pay` + noindex. (`/live` and `/holders` still 308-redirect, so their layouts/titles are unreachable — pre-existing.) Live DB afterwards holds one user-saved override set (brand `PAY`, title `Hold PAY. Win the draw.`) — saved from the panel itself; use **Reset to defaults** to clear it.
- **Favicon upload in the SEO panel (2026-10-22).** `SiteSeo` gained a `favicon` field (`SEO_FAVICON`), stored as a **base64 `data:` URI** in the same `AdminSetting` row set — still no migration, no filesystem, no object storage. `parseFavicon()` in `src/lib/seo.ts` is the single validation point (MIME allow-list PNG/ICO/SVG/WebP, base64 padding, decoded size ≤ **128 KB**) and is used by both `validateSeo` (400 + `errors.favicon`) and `generateMetadata` (invalid → silently treated as "no icon", so a corrupt row can never break rendering). New route **`src/app/api/favicon/route.ts`** decodes the URI and serves it as a Blob with the stored `Content-Type`, `Cache-Control: public, max-age=300, stale-while-revalidate=86400`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, and **404 when unset** so browsers fall back to the default tab icon; `layout.tsx` emits `icons.icon` / `icons.shortcut` pointing at `/api/favicon` only when parseable. Panel UI: new **Favicon** section before Analytics — 32 px preview + 16 px "tab size" preview (brand-initial fallback), Upload/Remove, client-side type/size checks with the input reset so the same file can be re-picked, and a hint listing allowed types, the size cap and the current KB. **Verified live:** login → POST 1×1 PNG → GET `/api/favicon` **200 `image/png`, 70 bytes** and home HTML contains `<link rel="shortcut icon" href="/api/favicon" type="image/png">` + `<link rel="icon" …>`; `data:text/plain;base64,…` → **400** `{"errors":{"favicon":"Upload a PNG, ICO, SVG or WebP under 128 KB."}}` with nothing written; clearing the field → home HTML has **0** `/api/favicon` refs and the route returns **404**; other SEO values untouched (brand `PAY`, title unchanged). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, eslint clean.

New **`src/components/RecentDraws.tsx`** (client, ~370 lines) renders the home's settled draws as `<ul>` rows; the old 5-column `<table>` in `src/app/page.tsx` and its now-dead helpers (`rankBadgeClass`, `shortAddr`, `explorer`, `fmtTime`, `winnerCols`) were deleted, and the section subtitle became *"Each draw, from the Pump.fun fee to the wallets it paid. Open one to see the receipts."* **Collapsed row:** `#N` + `timeAgo` | `/flow/pumpfun.png → /flow/pay.png → /flow/drand.png` 20 px tiles (same `ART` assets as the how-it-works flow, `hidden md:flex`) → 3 overlapping winner avatars (`slice(0,3)`; `+N` chip if a legacy round paid more) | prize pool (`fmtSolHuman`) + **Details / Hide** + `ChevronRight` rotating 90°. Multiple rows may be open (no accordion lock). - **`/rounds/[n]` copy rewritten (2026-10-22, page 7 of the UI-copy pass).** `src/app/rounds/[roundNumber]/page.tsx` + its `layout.tsx` metadata. **Vocabulary:** `AppShell title="Round"` → **`"Draw"`**, header `Round #{n}` → **`Draw #{n}`**, subtitle (*"Canonical, shareable record… three independent client-side checks that the beacon was valid…"*) → *"Who won, the random number that picked them, and three checks your browser can run to confirm nothing was edited afterwards."*, *"No winners for this round."* → **"This draw picked no winners."**, *"Was this wallet eligible for round #{n}?"* → **"Was this wallet in draw #{n}?"**. **Machine strings hidden:** round chip printed `{round.status}` raw → local `STATUS_LABEL` map (+ `statusLabel()`, same shape as `/verify`), which also fixes the winner table (`sent`/`pending` → **Paid / Pending**); the eligibility chip printed `eligibleSource` raw (`recorded|inferred|unavailable`) → new `SOURCE_LABEL` map (**Saved with this draw / Closest snapshot / Not available**). **Operator log lines gone:** `Error: {round.error}` (e.g. `Prize pool 0 SOL (balance at/below reserve) — round skipped`) → new `roundNote()` prints *"The prize pool was empty, so this draw picked no winners."* / *"The payout transaction failed on-chain, so this draw didn’t pay out."* with the raw string only on `title`; `fetchError` banner now reads *"Couldn’t load this draw. The link may be wrong, or the server didn’t answer…"* (`border-danger/30 bg-danger/10`, raw in `title`). **Plain words:** `Summary` → **Draw summary**; fields `Completed / Eligible holders / Beacon round` → **Finished / Eligible wallets / Randomness round**; `Independent verification` → **Three checks, all in your browser** (+ one-line explainer); check titles `SHA-256(signature) = randomness` → **The stored number belongs to its signature**, `drand.sh publishes the same value` → **drand published the same number**, `Winners re-derived from eligible pool + randomness` → **A fresh shuffle produces the same winners**; chip labels `✓ Pass / ✗ Fail / Idle` → **✓ Match / ✗ Mismatch / Waiting** (matches `/verify`); `Open beacon on drand.sh` → **See that round on drand.sh**; `Eligibility snapshot used` → **The wallet list this draw used**; `Eligible (in pool) / Total holders (batch) / Snapshot captured` → **Wallets in the pool / Wallets in the snapshot / Snapshot taken**; eligibility chips → **Yes — in this draw / No — not in this draw**; `Proof material` + `randomnessValue`/`randomnessSignature` → **The exact values we stored** + `Random number`/`drand signature`; `drand chain` → **The beacon behind every draw** with `Chain/Scheme/Period/Genesis` → `Beacon chain / Signature type / New number every / Live since` and `chainHash/publicKey` → `Chain ID / Public key`. `prizePoolSol.toFixed(4)` → `fmtSolHuman()` (no more `0.0000 SOL`). **Metadata:** route `layout.tsx` title `Round #N — Pay Token Lottery` → **`Draw #N — PAY Token Lottery`** and the jargon description → *"Who won draw #N, the random number that picked them, and the checks anyone can re-run."*. **Tokens:** all 9 `border-default-200` → `border-(--border)`, `text-default-400` → `text-muted`, input `focus:border-default-400` → `focus:border-accent`, `bg-default-50 text-default-500` Mono block → `bg-default text-foreground`; error banner `danger-200/danger-50` → `danger/30` + `danger/10`. Shared `SkeletonStat` (`src/components/ui.tsx`) label token `text-default-400` → `text-muted` (single token, no copy change — the page's loading skeletons used it). Also fixed a real rendering bug found during verification: a non-numeric URL rendered **`Draw #NaN`** in the header (now falls back to just *Draw*) and its tooltip said *"Invalid round number in the URL."* (now *"This link doesn’t point to a draw."*). `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; probe `/rounds/21` → 200 with *Draw #21* / *Who won draw #21* 1× each and `Round #` / `drand randomness that seeded it` 0×; page chunk `.next/dev/static/chunks/app/rounds/[roundNumber]/page.js` → new strings 1× each, `Independent verification` / `Proof material` / `Round #` / `text-default-400` / `border-default-200` 0×; `/rounds/1`, `/rounds/23`, `/rounds/abc`, `/draws` all 200.

- **`/stats` copy rewritten (2026-10-22, page 4 of the UI-copy pass).** Header `Stats` / "Aggregate metrics … no separate backend" → **`The lottery in numbers`** + a sentence about what is actually counted. Stat tiles `Draws completed / Winners / Total paid out / Biggest prize / Avg prize / draw / Avg eligible / draw` → **`Draws / Winners / Paid out / Biggest prize / Average prize / Average eligible`** with honest captions (`rounds finished` → `completed draws`, `selected total` → `wallets drawn`, `SOL sent` → `SOL actually sent`, `holders` → `wallets per draw`). `Round status breakdown` → **`Draw outcomes`**, plus a plain line explaining that a skipped draw means an empty pool, and a `STATUS_LABEL` map so the raw `completed|skipped|failed` enum never prints. A bar only appears on days when a draw finished." Leaderboard `Top winners` → **`Most won`**, last-win link `#12` → **`Draw 12`**, empty state "Nobody has won yet. The first draw will change that." Empty/error states rewritten (raw fetch/DB error strings only in `title=`), all dead `text-default-*` / `border-default-200` / `bg-default-200` / `danger-200` tokens replaced (`bg-(--border)` for the hairline tile grid).

- **`/stats` treasury section added — 2 wallet cards + tx tables + Buyback & burn (2026-10-22).** New `FinanceSection` block on `src/app/stats/page.tsx`, rendered between the intro/error banners and the stat tiles (with its own `FinanceSkeleton` while `/api/wallets` is in flight). **Left card = Platform wallet**, **right card = Fee wallet** (`grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4 items-start`); each is a `WalletCard` (label + status `Chip`, headline balance via `fmtSolHuman`, full address in a `rounded-md bg-default … font-mono text-xs break-all` strip linking to Solscan with a local `CopyButton` mirroring the admin pattern, then 2 `dl` facts: platform → **Prize pool** (balance − reserve, clamped ≥ 0) / **Reserve**; fee → **Lifetime fees** / **Fee rate** from `feeBps`) and a `TxCard` table beneath it (`ACTIVITY` / `AMOUNT` / `TX`, right-aligned amount coloured `text-success` for inflows, tx cell = `shortAddress(sig, 4, 4)` + `Icon name="external"` → Solscan tx). **Data split:** balances, reserve, fee rate and the cumulative fee ledger are **live** from the new `/api/wallets`; the fee card falls back to `fee.totalEarnedSol` as its headline when no treasury key is configured (`chipLabel` "Not configured", caption says the fees still sit on the platform wallet), and `ledgerAvailable: false` prints **—** instead of a fake 0. **Fee tx table** prefers real rows: `feeTxs` memo in the page derives one row per draw from `platformFeeLamports` + `payoutTx` (the sweep rides in the **same atomic tx** as the payouts, so the payout signature *is* the fee signature — no indexer needed), newest first, chip flips to **From draw ledger**; with no fee-bearing draw it shows `DEMO_FEE_TXS` under a warning **Sample** chip. **Platform tx history has no on-chain index yet**, so it always renders `DEMO_PLATFORM_TXS` + Sample chip + an explicit "indexer isn't wired yet" note — never mixed with real rows. **Buyback & burn** card spans full width underneath: `feeSharePct` explainer + `last run / next` timestamps, a 4-cell hairline grid (`gap-px` on `bg-(--border)`: total buyback SOL, bought back, burned + % of supply, runs), a burned-share progress bar (`bg-default` track, `bg-accent` fill), and a placeholder disclaimer; every figure from `DEMO_BUYBACK` with a required **Sample** chip. A failed wallet snapshot renders one soft `border-danger/30 bg-danger/10` banner with the raw error only in `title=` — it never blanks the draw history (separate `useEffect` from `/api/rounds`). **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint` on `stats/page.tsx` + `api/wallets/route.ts` + `demo-finance.ts` → 0 problems, `GET /api/wallets` live → 200 (`platform.balanceSol` real, `fee.configured: true`, `sweeps: 2`, `ledgerAvailable: true`, `error: null`), `GET /stats` → 200 and the dev chunk contains the new components (SSR shows `FinanceSkeleton` because the fetch is client-side). **Typography correction (follow-up, user feedback "textnya kebesaran"):** HeroUI's `.table__cell` bakes `font-size: var(--text-sm)` + `padding-inline: 1rem / padding-block: 0.75rem` into a layered rule, so the `text-xs` on `<TableContent>` never reached the cells — tx rows rendered 14px/≈48px tall, noticeably heavier than `/draws` and `/eligibility`. Added a `.table-compact` modifier in `src/app/globals.css` (unlayered, same cascade trick as `.table-bordered`): cells **12px / line-height 1.45**, padding **0.75rem × 0.5rem**, outer columns **1.25rem** so the first/last cell line up with the card's `px-5` header instead of bleeding past it; applied to both `TxCard` tables. Wallet headline also dropped to the project stat-tile scale (`text-xl sm:text-2xl … tracking-tight`) so it matches the tiles below. `npx tsc --noEmit` + `npx eslint` → clean; `.table-compact` compiles into `layout.css` (6 rules) and `/stats` stays **200**.


- **`/verify` copy rewritten + `SeedToWinner.tsx` swept (2026-10-22, page 5 of the UI-copy pass).** Header `Verify a draw` → **`Check a draw yourself`**; the old subtitle (BLS-signed, client-side, SHA-256, no backend round-trip) became two plain sentences about drand and the two things the page checks. Selector `Round to verify` → **`Draw to check`**, options `Round #N — date (n winners)` → **`Draw #N — date · n winners`**, focus ring on a real token (`focus:border-accent`), empty state "No draws to check yet." Card heading **`Draw #N`**; raw DB status replaced by a `STATUS_LABEL` map; fields `Beacon round / Completed / Eligible holders / Error` → **`Randomness round / Finished / Eligible wallets / Note`**, and skipped draws show *"The prize pool was empty, so this draw picked no winners."* instead of the engine log line. `Independent verification` → **`Two checks, both in your browser`** (with a one-line explainer), check titles `SHA-256(signature) = randomness` → **`The stored number belongs to its signature`**, `drand.sh publishes the same value` → **`drand published the same number`**, chip labels `Pass/Fail/Idle` → **`Match/Mismatch/Waiting`**. `Proof material` + raw field names → **`The exact values we stored`** (`Random number` / `drand signature`); `drand chain` → **`The beacon behind every draw`**; `How randomness works` → **`Where the random number comes from`**, all four steps rewritten without "512-bit hex", "BLS key", "zero backend trust" (badge colours moved to `bg-accent/10 text-accent`, tick badge to `bg-success/10 text-success`). Error banners now `border-danger/30 bg-danger/10` with the raw fetch/DB string only in `title=`. **`SeedToWinner.tsx`** (shared by `/verify` and `/rounds/[n]`) got the same treatment: `From seed to winner` → **`From a random number to the winners`**, arrow-titles (`drand signature → SHA-256 → the seed`, `Seed → a stream…`, `Eligible wallets → …`, `Fisher–Yates shuffle → the top N win`) → sentences, `Live for round #N` → **`Re-run for draw #N`**, verdicts `Idle` → **`Waiting`**, `✓ Reproduced` → **`✓ Same winners`**, `✓ matches stored rank` → **`✓ same as the saved winner`**, double-hyphen notes replaced with em dashes and "round" → "draw". Full token sweep there: 20 `text-default-*` → `text-muted`/`text-foreground`, 10 `border/ring-default-*` → `border-(--border)`/`ring-(--border)`, `bg-default-100` → `bg-default`, `bg-default-50/40` → `bg-default/40`, amber badges → accent tokens.

- **`/how-it-works` copy rewritten (2026-10-22, page 6 of the UI-copy pass).** The densest page, rewritten around the real mechanism: **PAY** is a Pump.fun token, **creator fees** fund the pool, the pool (minus an ≈0.05 SOL reserve) is paid in SOL, and winners come from a drand number shuffling the holder list. Header `A provably-fair lottery, end to end.` → **`How the lottery works`**, subtitle now names PAY + Pump.fun creator fees + "public randomness service nobody controls"; chips `drand quicknet / Fisher–Yates / On-chain payouts / BLS-signed` → **`Holding is the entry / Random draw / Paid in SOL / Publicly checkable`**. Lifecycle `Step by step, from snapshot to payout` → **`From holder list to payout`**; step titles de-jargoned (`Holder snapshot syncs` → **`The holder list refreshes`**, `The draw fetches a fresh beacon` → **`The draw asks drand for a number`**, `The beacon seeds a shuffle` → **`That number shuffles the list`**, `Winners are paid on-chain` → **`Winners are paid in SOL`**) and the `sha256(signature)` code chip dropped from prose. **Factual fixes:** (1) payouts are **one atomic transaction for all winners** (`engine.distributePayouts`) — copy now says so instead of implying a transfer per winner; (2) the stale "an admin can trigger a manual retry that pays every pending winner" paragraph was **wrong** (the engine deletes orphaned `pending` winner rows on failure and there is no pay-pending retry path) → replaced with *"All payouts for a draw leave in one transaction. If that transaction fails, nothing moves on-chain, the draw is marked failed…"*; (3) the verify card claimed drand signature **BLS verification against the chain public key** — the UI only re-fetches the drand round JSON and compares randomness + signature strings, so the claim is now "confirms the number and its signature are exactly what drand published"; (4) `Where the money goes` never said where the pool comes from → now opens with Pump.fun creator fees minus the reserve. Also: status chips `pending`/`sent` → **`Pending`/`Paid`**, `Verify any draw yourself, in your browser` → **`You can check any draw yourself`**, facts `Chain/Scheme/Beacon period/Relays` → **`Beacon/Signature type/New number/Sources`**, eligibility heading **`Hold PAY. That’s the whole entry.`**, schedule `Holder sync` → **`Holder list`** (and "draw cron is admin-tunable" dropped for "The schedule can be changed"), all 8 FAQ answers rewritten (`minimum Pay token balance` → `minimum PAY`, network answer → "bottom of the sidebar"), CTA "Every finished draw". Every `border-default-200` / `text-default-500` / `bg-default-100` / `divide-default-200` / amber badge in this file replaced with `border-(--border)` / `text-muted` / `bg-default` / `divide-(--border)` / `bg-accent/10 text-accent ring-accent/20`.

- **Draws page copy rewritten (2026-10-21, page 2 of the UI-copy pass).** `src/app/draws/page.tsx` + `src/components/DrawsList.tsx` (~24 items, all approved in a numbered table before editing). **Factual fix:** the header promised *"on-chain verification available in-browser"* — nothing is on-chain; verification is a drand HTTP fetch + browser re-shuffle, so it now reads *"Every draw, from the holder snapshot to the SOL payouts. Winners are picked from eligible holders with drand randomness — a public beacon nobody controls. You can re-run a draw in your browser and get the same wallets."* **Vocabulary unified to "draw"** (Home's term) everywhere it was "round": header, `Round #N` → **`Draw #N`**, *"Round skipped/failed"* → **"Draw skipped/failed"**, *"No draws yet. The first one appears here as soon as it finishes."*, `Eligible` → **`Eligible wallets`**, `Time` → **`Drawn`**, `Total rounds` → **`Draws`** (and its caption `completed draws` was simply wrong — the list also holds skipped + failed), `Total paid out` → **`Paid out`**, `cumulative payouts` → `total in SOL`. **Machine strings hidden:** `StatusChip` rendered the raw DB value with `<Chip.Label>{status}</Chip.Label>` (so users saw lowercase `completed` / `skipped` / `failed` / `sent`) — new `STATUS_LABEL` map + `statusLabel()` now prints **Completed / Skipped / Failed / Paid / Pending / In progress**. Skipped rounds dumped the engine log line (`Prize pool 0 SOL (balance at/below reserve) — round skipped`) straight into the card — new `skipReason()` maps the two known reasons to plain sentences (19 of 21 live rounds hit the "pool empty" branch) and falls back to the raw text. Failed round #3 showed `payout tx 5Dh6… failed on-chain: {"InsufficientFundsForRent":{…}}` — new `failureReason()` prints *"The payout transaction failed on-chain, so this draw didn’t pay out."* with the raw string kept on `title`. **VerifyButton** copy: `✓ drand.sh match` → **`✓ Beacon matches`**, `✗ mismatch` → `✗ Mismatch`, `unreachable` → `drand unreachable`, `✓ reproduced 3/3` → `✓ Winners reproduced 3/3`, `N/A` → `Not available`, `failed` → `Check failed`, and the detail lines lost their `/3/3 … + 93 eligible holders` and `winner(s)` fragments. `open beacon` → **`View on drand`**, `randomness value (seed)` → `Randomness (seed)`, `BLS signature` → `Signature (BLS)`, `tx ↗` → **`payout ↗`**, `No winners` → "No winners in this draw.", `Failed to load draws: {err}` / `DB error:` → `Couldn’t load draws — {err}` / `The draw history couldn’t be read — …`. **Dead tokens purged** (they rendered no border at all): 6× `border-default-200`/`300`/`100`, `bg-default-200`, `text-default-400/500/600/700/800`, `divide-default-200`, 2× `text-primary`, `border-danger-200 bg-danger-50` → `border-(--border)`, `bg-(--border)` (the summary card's `gap-px` hairlines now have a color), `bg-default/40`, `text-muted`/`text-foreground`, `text-accent`, `border-danger/30 bg-danger/10`; the round-stat grid dropped its `sm:-mx-5 divide-x` hack for a plain `gap-x-6`. Internal `/rounds/N` link switched `<a>` → `next/link` (no full reload). Validated: `npx tsc --noEmit` **0 errors**, `GET /draws` **200**, new header strings **1×**, removed strings **0×**, compiled CSS contains `.bg-\(--border\)` / `.ring-\(--border\)`; client-rendered rows still need a browser refresh to eyeball. **Follow-up trim (same day, user request):** the `/draws` intro was cut a second time to a single 15-word line — **"Winners are picked from eligible holders with drand randomness — a public beacon nobody controls."** The old first clause (*"Every draw, from the holder snapshot to the SOL payouts."*) only restated what the `Draws` h4 + the stat tiles already say, and the re-run sentence duplicated Home's *"Anyone can re-run it in the browser"*. **Terminology confirmed with the user:** `Draws` (plural) for the nav/page/list, `draw` (singular) for one event, `Draw #N` capitalised as card heading + table column — matches Home's `Recent draws` / `View all draws` / `<th>Draw</th>`. Still out of alignment: `src/app/rounds/[roundNumber]/page.tsx` renders **`Round #{n}`** + *"Was this wallet eligible for round #{n}?"* — fix when that page enters the copy pass.

- **Home copy rewritten — accurate to the actual mechanism (2026-10-21).** Full pass on `src/app/page.tsx` (~30 strings) against the real flow: **PAY** is a Pump.fun token whose **creator fees** fund the pool, paid in SOL to the **top 3** wallets, picked by **drand** randomness. Key fixes: (1) hero now names the money source — "Hold **PAY**. Win the draw." + "Hold {minRule} and you’re in. Creator fees become the prize pool, split in SOL between **{winnerCount}** wallets picked at random with drand — verifiable by anyone."; the old copy never mentioned creator fees and the number 3 appeared only as a badge. Trimmed twice on user request, with the framing moved to **fee → pool** (creator fees *become* the pool, paid out in SOL, winners random): prize-tile prose is "Funded by creator fees, paid out in SOL to {winnerCount} randomly drawn wallets." (24 → 12 words) and the "How a draw works" paragraph is "We snapshot every wallet holding {minRule}, draw {winnerCount} winners with a drand-seeded shuffle, and pay them in SOL. Anyone can re-run it in the browser and check the result." (the fee→pool clause was dropped here on purpose — creator fees are already named in the hero and the prize tile, so repeating them in the third block read as filler). Wording says "become", never "automatically" — fees are still claimed manually to the platform wallet, so no copy implies an unbuilt auto-claim. (2) `TOKEN_NAME` `"Pay"` → **`"PAY"`** (ticker casing) + new `winnerCount = prize?.winnerCount ?? 3` and `minRule` (`at least 1,000 PAY` / `the minimum balance`) derived once so prose can't drift from the numbers in the chips; `minRule` also kills the old **"— PAY"** first-paint artefact from SSR before `/api/status` lands (chip falls back to "Minimum balance"). (3) Jargon out: "Settling" → **"In progress"**, "Round #N is being settled" → **"Draw #N is in progress"**, "until the next round starts" → **"until the next draw"**, "≥" removed from prose + eligibility chip, "scheduler triggers" → "once it runs", table "ROUND" → **"DRAW"**, "ELIGIBLE" → **"WALLETS"**, "1st place" → **"1st prize"**, `view all` → **"View all draws" / "View all"** with `aria-label`s. (4) Disclaimer rewritten — it no longer claims "live odds"/"playing" (there is no wager, no entry fee, holding is the only requirement): "The animation shows how a draw works. It doesn’t show live odds. PAY is a lottery token, not an investment. Its price is set by the Pump.fun market, prizes are paid only from the creator-fee pool, and you can lose money holding it." (5) All internal `<a>` → `next/link` (`/draws`, `/rounds/[n]`, `/how-it-works`), and the eligible-tile link now goes straight to **`/eligibility`** instead of `/holders` (which is a `next.config.mjs` 308 → saves a redirect hop); new **`arrow-right`** glyph added to `ui.tsx` `Icon` for internal CTAs (external X/Telegram keep `arrow-up-right`). (6) Dead tokens purged in the touched markup: 5× `border-default-200` → `border-(--border)` (cards had no visible border), `text-default-400` → `text-muted`, 2× `bg-default-100` → `bg-default/40` (progress tracks were invisible), `hover:bg-default-50` → `hover:bg-default/40`. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /` → **200**; served HTML has 0× `Settling`, 0× `≥`, 0× `view all`, 0× `transparency reference`. Note: `/holders` and `/live` are **not** orphan pages (earlier assumption wrong) — both are permanent redirects in `next.config.mjs` (`/live`→`/`, `/holders`→`/eligibility`), so they stay out of the copy-review scope.
- **Sidebar: prize-pool pill gone everywhere + new "Information" block + `mainnet` caption removed (2026-10-22).** Three nav cleanups. (1) `AppShell.tsx` (every route except `/`) still rendered the header jackpot pill (`title="Current prize pool — view active round"`, reading `prizePoolSol`) and the `/api/status` `useEffect` that fed it — both deleted, so `AppShell` is now a pure shell: no fetch, `useEffect`/`fmtSolHuman` imports dropped, `<ThemeToggle/>` alone in the `ml-auto` slot. Home was already clean. (2) New **Information** block pinned to the bottom of the sidebar in **both** sidebars (`AppShell.tsx` and the homepage's own copy in `page.tsx`): `mt-auto` wrapper above the collapse row, `text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted` label (hidden when collapsed) and two external rows — **X** and **Telegram** — with `target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"` and an `arrow-up-right` affordance that hides on collapse. This mirrors the otcdesks.cash grouping (Docs / Terms / X), minus docs+terms since those pages don't exist. Data lives in `nav.ts` as `INFORMATION: NavItem[]`; row styling reuses the nav row classes (`px-3 py-2 text-sm text-muted hover:bg-default/40 hover:text-foreground`, `lg:justify-center lg:px-0` when collapsed). (3) The `mainnet` caption above the collapse button is gone in both sidebars: the footer no longer renders `network`, and its wrapper is `hidden ... lg:block` (the collapse button was already `lg`-only) so mobile doesn't show an empty bordered strip. (4) `Icon` gained `x` (filled simple-icons X path on `fill="currentColor"` — the outline set has no brand glyph and a stroked cross reads as a close button) and `telegram` (lucide `Send`). (5) Dead tokens in the touched shell markup swapped for real HeroUI ones: `border-default-200` → `border-(--border)`, `text-default-400` → `text-muted`, `hover:bg-default-100` → `hover:bg-default/40`, `text-default-foreground` → `text-foreground` (confirmed in `.next/dev/static/css/app/layout.css`: those four old classes are absent, the replacements compile). Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `/`, `/draws`, `/stats`, `/verify`, `/eligibility`, `/how-it-works` all **200**, each with 1× `Information`, 1× `x.com/`, 1× `t.me/`, **0×** `Current prize pool`, **0×** the `font-mono text-default-400` mainnet span; served HTML contains the X path (`18.244 2.25`) + `lucide-send`; CSS has `.mt-auto`, `.tracking-wider`, `.uppercase`, `.border-(--border)`, `.hover:bg-default/40`, `.text-[10px]`, `.h-3.5`. **Placeholder URLs:** the two hrefs are `https://x.com/` and `https://t.me/` — swap in the real profiles in `nav.ts` (single source, one line each).

- **Flow cards can now carry brand art + the section footnote is plain text (2026-10-22).** Two follow-ups from the same review. (1) The disclaimer under the flow was first shipped as an amber alert *card*; user: "gausah dibungkus card aja deh" — the box was too heavy for a footnote, so it is back to unboxed text: `flex max-w-2xl gap-2.5` with a 20px solid `bg-warning text-(--warning-foreground)` dot holding a 12px `TriangleAlert`, then **Disclaimer** (`text-sm font-semibold`), one `text-xs text-muted` paragraph ("illustrates the mechanics, not live odds … token price is set by the Pump.fun market, prizes are paid only from the pool funded for each round, and playing can lose you money") and the `Read the full transparency reference →` link (`text-xs`). No border, no background, no `aside`. `TriangleAlert` is the only new import in `src/app/page.tsx`. (2) The three step cards ("Pump.fun trading" / "Pay engine" / "drand beacon") only had lucide icons; `Node` in `HowItWorksFlow.tsx` now takes an optional `image?: string` rendered in the badge slot at `size-6 object-contain` inside the existing `size-8 overflow-hidden rounded-xl` tint, wired through one `ART` constant (`pumpfun` / `pay` / `drand` → `/flow/<name>.png`) in **both** the desktop and mobile layouts. A missing file is not fatal: the `<img>` sets `artBroken` via `onError` and the badge falls back to its lucide icon, so the flow never shows a broken-image glyph and the code is safe to ship before the PNGs exist. Drop the files in `public/flow/` (that folder was previously **empty** — no `public/` at all) with the documented names; `public/flow/README.md` lists them. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /` → **200**, no runtime overlay; served HTML has `src="/flow/pumpfun.png"` / `pay.png` / `drand.png` (2× each = desktop + mobile), 12× `overflow-hidden rounded-xl`, and **0×** `aria-labelledby="flow-disclaimer"`; a probe PNG dropped into `public/flow/` was served `200 image/png` then deleted, and `/flow/pumpfun.png` still 404s as expected until the real asset lands.


- **Flow cards slimmed to one width + Pump.fun pre-roll + winner card re-typed (2026-10-22).** Follow-up on the single-dot flow: the cards were `w-[18%]`, so every label had ~30 px of dead space on its right while the gaps stayed narrow, and the winner card was a different design language (`HeroUI Avatar` + a `text-[10px] font-bold tabular-nums` amber chip) so it read as a different font. Now **all four cards are the same 16.5% column with equal 10.7% gaps** (lefts 1 / 28.2 / 55.3 / 82.5%), internals tightened to `px-3 · gap-2.5 · size-8` badge so the longest label (`drand beacon`, `Pump.fun trading`) still fits at a 1120 px container. Geometry constants moved with them — `SEQ_START_X 175 · PAY_LEFT_X 282 · PAY_RIGHT_X 447 · DRAND_LEFT_X 553 · DRAND_RIGHT_X 718` (still the split point) `· WINNER_X 825`, cards at `top-[164px]`, winners at `64/164/264` with `WINNER_YS [93, 190, 287]`; branch control points `(775-i*3, LINE_Y)` / `(786-i*2, y)`. `WinnerCard` is now just `<Node>` + `<Confetti>` — identical type (`text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight` title, `text-xs` subtitle), identical 62 px height and border; rank lives in the badge slot (`rankBadgeClass`), and the SOL share arrives as the **subtitle** (`Awaiting payout` → `0.50 SOL` in `tabular-nums text-accent`). `rankRingClass` and the `@heroui/react` `Avatar` import are gone. New narrative pre-roll: `PHASES.chargeEnd = .09` — before the dot exists, the first card is `active` and `FeeCharge` runs three emerald pulses + `Collecting...` (same rhythm as `ShuffleDots`/`ScrambleText`), so every card now visibly "works" before handing the particle on. Timeline retuned (`FLOW_CYCLE 6.5 → 8`): charge .00–.09, walk1 .09–.23, shuffle .23–.34 (`SHUFFLE_END` is now `payPauseEnd`, so the Pay card stops shuffling exactly when the dot leaves), walk2 .34–.48, scramble .48–.63, fork .63–.83, reveal .83–1.00. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /` → **200** (no error overlay); served HTML has `M 175 190 L 282 190`, `M 447 190 L 553 190` and `M 718 190 C 775 190 786 93 825 93`; compiled `layout.css` contains `.w-[16.5%]`, `.left-[55.3%]`, `.top-[164px]`, `.gap-2.5`, `.size-8`; a node simulation confirmed the fork lands at x 924 px = winner-card left edge and y 95.4/195/294.6 px = card centres 95/195/295.


- **How-it-works flow now runs on one travelling dot (2026-10-22).** User: "saya tidak melihat dot jalan dari card Pump.fun trading … ke pay engine sampai drand beacon" — the only visible motion was three dash-trails fanning out of `drand beacon` into the winners, and they read as several currents at once. Root cause: `src/components/HowItWorksFlow.tsx` had no traveller at all — every wire drew an `ElectricFlow` trail (`strokeDasharray="9 11"` + SMIL `stroke-dashoffset`), the Pump→Pay and Pay→drand pulses were live for only 18%/12% of the cycle across ~72-unit gaps, and all three winner branches lit simultaneously. Rewritten as a single particle driven by the existing rAF clock (`useFlowProgress` → `p`): `FlowDot` is one 8px accent span positioned in the same 1000x380 space as the wires (percentages, so it stays exactly on its line and stays round — the SVG is stretched with `preserveAspectRatio="none"`, which would squash a `<circle>`). Sequence: dot crosses `Pump.fun → Pay engine` (p .02–.18, smoothstep), is hidden while "inside" the card (the opaque card covers it), comes out and crosses `Pay engine → drand` (.30–.42), disappears into drand (scramble .42–.60), then `forkT` splits it into **one dot per winner** along explicit cubic control points (`winnerBranches` — the same numbers that build the path strings, so no second animation clock). Dot windows derive from `PHASES` (`DOT_WALK_1/2`, `DOT_FORK`), so retiming the narrative is one edit. Also: split point corrected to the real drand right edge (688 → 692), `WINNER_YS` 62/164/265 → 58/159/260 so branch dots land on the card centres, and the SMIL `MobileConnector`/`MobileBranchFlow` became `MobileWire` + three branch dots inside the same `forking` window (the mobile dashes lit all three branches at once — same complaint). Tokens fixed while in the file: `border-default-200/300`, `text-default-400/500`, `bg-default-100`, `ring-default-200` (dead in this theme) → `border-(--border)`, `text-muted`, `bg-default`, `ring-(--border)`, `hover:border-muted/40`; the two card comments that sat above the wrong cards (`drand`/`Pay engine`) were swapped back; a double-encoded `×` in the geometry header repaired to `x`. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /` → **200**; served HTML no longer contains `M 688 190`/`stroke-dashoffset` (3× `M 692 190` instead); compiled `layout.css` contains `.shadow-[0_0_10px_2px_var(--accent)]` → `--tw-shadow: 0 0 10px 2px var(--tw-shadow-color, var(--accent))`.

- **Home "Recent draws" is now a compact table (2026-10-22).** Follow-up to the tile work: the old rows were a flex strip (`#4 · 0.0012 SOL · · 452 eligible · [avatars] · time · ↗`) so nothing lined up column-wise, the `↗` was a dead icon (not a link), and the summary read the ungrammatical `1 draws · 3 winners · 0.0011 SOL paid`. `src/app/page.tsx` now renders a real `<table>`: **`Round | Prize pool | Eligible | Winner 1..N | Tx`**. `winnerCols = min(5, max(3, prize.winnerCount, …winners.length))` — the configured split, but never fewer columns than a round actually paid (legacy 5-winner rounds). Each winner cell is a link to that winner's `payoutTx` (or their address if unpaid) showing a podium rank chip (`rankBadgeClass`) + `shortAddr(addr, 4, 4)`; the `Tx` cell collapses the **distinct** `payoutTx` signatures in the round, because payouts are batched: round #4 paid all three winners in **one** transaction, so showing it as a "1st-place tx" would have been a lie. One distinct tx → short hash link to Solscan (title = `N payouts in this tx · <full sig>`); several → `N txs` link to `/rounds/N`; none → `—`. Header subtitle now matches the "How it works" paragraph typographically (`Typography type="body-sm" color="muted"`): **"Settled rounds, their prize pools, and the wallets they paid."** — the old count strip (`1 draw · 3 winners · 0.0011 SOL paid`) duplicated numbers the table already shows and read "1 draws". The section link is now the same `view all ↗` treatment as the tile footers (`underline` + `hover:text-foreground`, lowercase, no `text-accent`) and is inset `mr-5` so its right edge lines up with the table's `Tx` column. Responsive: `Eligible` appears from `sm`, winner columns from `md` (below `md` the Round cell carries an `N winners` subline), table wrapper is `overflow-x-auto`, numeric cells `whitespace-nowrap`; if a round somehow paid more winners than the 5-column cap, the last column appends a `+N` link to `/rounds/N` rather than dropping them. Dead code removed: `Avatar`/`AvatarGroup` import + `rankRingClass`; a local `explorer(v, kind, net)` mirrors `DrawsList.tsx`. `rounds.dbError` now shows "Draw history is temporarily unavailable" instead of "No draws have run yet". Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /` → **200**; `GET /api/rounds` → 1 completed round (#4, pool 0.00119, 95 eligible, 3 × `status=sent`, one shared `payoutTx`). **Grid + token pass (2026-10-22, user: "jarak … winner 1 2 3 dan tx masih ngawur")**: one padding system now governs the table — outer edges `pl-5`/`pr-5` (20px, which is why `view all`'s `mr-5` lands on the `Tx` column), every interior column `px-3` (12px gutters on *both* sides; the old cells only had `pr-*`, so the gap before `Winner 1` was half the edge gap), header `py-2.5` + body `py-3`, header cells `text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider`, table root `text-xs` so round ids, numbers and hashes share one size, numeric columns right-aligned, text columns left, `<caption className="sr-only">` for screen readers. **Token bug uncovered here**: this build's HeroUI v3 theme has **no numeric `default-*` scale** — `text-default-400`, `border-default-200`, `bg-default-100`, `hover:bg-default-50/60` appear nowhere in the served `layout.css` (checked by fetching `/_next/static/css/app/layout.css`), so every "muted" label was inheriting full `--foreground` and row hairlines only showed up through HeroUI's `border-color: var(--border)` fallback. The section now uses tokens that actually compile: `text-muted` (headers, `#round`, addresses, hashes, `N txs`), `text-muted/40` (empty `—`), `bg-default/40` (row hover, emitted as `color-mix`), `bg-default` (rank 4+ chip fallback), `border-(--border)` (card frame + row/header hairlines — the exact separator colour HeroUI falls back to). The same dead utilities still cover the rest of the homepage and other pages (`text-default-400` is on ~15 elements in `/` alone); left untouched to keep this diff scoped, flagged as a follow-up sweep in `.clinerules`. **Row cap (2026-10-22, user request)**: the homepage shows the **3 newest completed rounds** (`slice(0, 3)`) and deliberately has **no load-more / pagination** — `view all` already leads to `/draws`, so a second navigation control would compete with it. The loading skeleton was reduced from 5 to 3 rows so load-in does not shift the page. The API still returns up to 50 rows (completed/failed/skipped, newest first) and the page filters, so changing the cap is a one-number edit; today only 1 round is `completed` (17 `skipped`, 1 `failed`), so the homepage renders a single row.

Each footer now answers a question only it can answer. **API** (`src/app/api/status/route.ts`) — new additive `prize` object: `winnerCount`, `payoutDistribution`, `effectivePoolSol`, `firstPrizeSol`, `minPrizeSol`, `playable`, computed with the engine's real pipeline (`cap → platform fee → split`, and the same skip rule: `effectivePool > 0 && (min === 0 || effectivePool >= min)`). No secrets exposed, `prizePoolSol` unchanged for other consumers. **`src/app/page.tsx`** — (1) prize tile: chip `Funded` (only tested `pool > 0`, so a pool below `MIN_PRIZE_SOL` looked ready) → `Loading` / `Ready` / `Below minimum` (warning + tooltip with the threshold) / `Awaiting funds`, and the footer's `Last draw · Winners · Paid out` strip → **`Winners 3 · 50% / 30% / 20%`** + **`1st place ≈ x SOL`** (2 cols); (2) next-draw footer: `Draws every hour / in 42m` (local-time, `NaN` for step/fixed crons — `scheduleFromCron` read the *minute* field as hours, so `*/15 * * * *` became "every 15h") → `Draws {cadence}` derived from the actual gap between two `nextCronOccurrence` calls + absolute **`HH:MM UTC`** (date prefix for ≥1-day cadences); helpers `scheduleFromCron`/`nextRunLabel` deleted; (3) holders footer: `412 of 480 holders` (already the big number) → **`Snapshot {timeAgo(lastSyncAt)}`** + `View →`, the fraction moved to a `title` tooltip; subtitle now reads "no wallet clears the threshold yet" at `eligibleCount = 0`; (4) removed the home header's live-jackpot pill (the same pool is the tile's headline ~200px below; `AppShell` keeps its pill for the other routes); (5) number formats unified on `fmtSolHuman` (the Recent-draws header no longer mixes `toFixed(2)`); `drawsCount` now counts completed rounds only. Root cause was mine: the old footer repeated `412 of 480 holders` and thereby labelled the gauge; replacing it with `Snapshot 3m ago` left the bar unlabelled. Fix in `src/app/page.tsx`: the footer under the bar carries the gauge's own number — **`21% of 452 holders`** — replacing `Snapshot 3m ago`, which was low-value noise (sync runs every 5 min, so it read "just now" almost always) and left the bar unlabelled. Snapshot age and refresh cadence stay available in that footer's `title` (**`Snapshot 2m ago · syncs every 5m`**), with the cadence derived the same honest way as the draw cadence (`nextCronOccurrence(holderSyncCron, epoch)` + 1s, then `cadenceLabel` on the gap, memoised on the cron string so it is render-stable and SSR-safe). `ProgressBar.Fill` gained `transition-[width] duration-700 ease-out`, the gauge's only motion: it eases into place when a fresh snapshot arrives instead of snapping. Zero-state reads `No holders tracked yet`; while `status` is null the footer renders `—` and no `title`, so SSR never claims "0% of 0 holders". - **Concurrent-draw race fixed — `Unique constraint failed on (roundNumber)` (2026-10-22).** Production log showed `prisma.lotteryRound.create()` failing with P2002 on `roundNumber` while the UI still showed the round as `skipped`. Root cause was **two schedulers** (an orphaned `next start` node process, parent gone + no listening port but a live Neon Prisma connection, still ticking `node-cron`) racing the **non-atomic** `findFirst(max)` → `create(max+1)` sequence in `runLotteryRound`. Evidence in `LotteryRound`: four hours with **two rounds in the same minute** (#7+#8, #9+#10, #11+#12, #14+#15) — the loser of the race normally dies on the unique constraint, but when its read landed a second after the winner's insert it happily created a *second* round in the same hour (a double-payout hazard once the pool is non-zero). Fix in `src/lib/lottery/engine.ts`: new `claimRoundSlot()` runs the whole allocation inside `prisma.$transaction` guarded by `pg_advisory_xact_lock(ROUND_ALLOC_LOCK_KEY)` — read-max + insert are one critical section, so no number can be handed out twice across **any** number of processes; concurrent callers get a `busy` slot (no round created) instead of throwing. Two extra guards inside the same transaction: (1) rounds stuck in `drawing` for >30 min (`STALE_DRAWING_MS`) are auto-recovered as `failed` so a crashed process can't block future draws, (2) `MIN_ROUND_SPACING_MS` (30s) rejects a duplicate trigger that arrives *after* the first round already finished — a skipped round completes in ~1.5s, so the "still drawing" check alone still let a same-tick duplicate create round N+1 (verified in testing). Busy callers return `status: "skipped"` with a precise `error` message, so `scheduler.ts`, `api/admin/trigger-draw`, and `scripts/runLottery.ts` needed no changes. `src/lib/scheduler.ts` also gained per-process `syncing` / `drawing` overlap flags (a tick that fires while the previous one is still running is skipped, not stacked). Verified by running 3 truly concurrent `runLotteryRound()` calls against the real DB: **exactly 1 round created, 2 ignored, zero P2002** (test round deleted afterwards). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors. **Action needed on the host:** kill the orphan `node.exe` (PID 21024 — needs an elevated shell; `Stop-Process`/`taskkill` from a normal terminal is denied), then `npm run build` + `npm start` so production runs this code. Pool is still **0** (platform wallet balance ≤ `PRIZE_RESERVE_LAMPORTS` 1,000,000), which is why every round is recorded as `skipped` by design — not an error.

- **Skip-on-empty pool + pending-payout system removed (2026-10-21).** Two coupled changes per user request ("skip aja, pending mending hapus aja"). **(1) Engine skip-on-zero** — `runLotteryRound` (`src/lib/lottery/engine.ts`) now skips when `effectivePool === 0n` (platform wallet balance at/below the reserve = no income) OR `effectivePool < minPrizeLamports`, even when `MIN_PRIZE_SOL = 0` (the default). Previously `MIN_PRIZE_SOL = 0` + empty wallet ran the round and stranded winners in `"pending"` forever. Now no drand request, no winner selection, no payout — round recorded `status: "skipped"` with reason `"Prize pool 0 SOL (balance at/below reserve) — round skipped"` (distinct from the below-min reason). **(2) Pending-payout flow removed entirely** — deleted `payPendingWinners()` + `PayPendingResult` from `engine.ts`, deleted `POST /api/admin/pay-pending` route (+ folder), removed `pendingWinnersCount` from `/api/admin/info` (decl + `winner.count({status:"pending"})` query + response field), removed from `AppInfo` type. **Admin UI** (`src/app/admin/page.tsx` `LiveStatusCard`) — removed the "Pending payout" StatTile (status-tiles grid → `lg:grid-cols-4`: Draw status / Total paid / Winners / Network), removed the "pending payouts action" bar (Pending count + "Pay pending winners" button + result message), removed `paying/payMsg/paySev` state + `payPending()` fn, dropped the now-unused `onRefresh` prop (only caller was the button). **Payout-failure cleanup** — since `distributePayouts` is a single atomic tx, a throw means nothing was sent on-chain; the `runLotteryRound` catch now best-effort `deleteMany` the just-created `status:"pending"` winner rows for that round (no manual retry path exists, so orphaned pending rows would be unpayable), then `fail()` marks the round `"failed"`. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors. **Note:** the round-stats tiles row (Completed/Skipped/Failed/Max prize/Min prize) was added earlier this session and remains; the "Skipped" tile now also reflects zero-balance skips, not just below-min. **Manual test pending:** set `MIN_PRIZE_SOL=0`, run with an empty platform wallet → expect `status: "skipped"`, `winners: []`, no pending winner rows created.
- **Max prize cap + minimum prize threshold (2026-10-21).** Two new admin-tunable payout settings, entered as **readable SOL** (not lamports): `MAX_PRIZE_SOL` (max SOL paid per round; `0` = no cap) and `MIN_PRIZE_SOL` (minimum effective prize to run a round; `0` = no minimum). **Config** — `lib/config.ts` `LotteryConfig` gained `maxPrizeLamports` + `minPrizeLamports` (bigint, internal), parsed from the SOL strings via new `parseSolToLamports()` (× 1e9, `Math.round`; unset/invalid/≤0 → `0n`). **Settings** — both keys added to `SETTING_KEYS`, `SETTING_META` (group `payout`, `restartRequired: false`, live-preview descriptions), `SETTING_DEFAULTS` (`"0"`), and `validateSetting` (non-negative number). They render automatically in the admin Settings tab's Payout group. **Engine** (`runLotteryRound`) — after `computePrizePool`, applies the cap: `effectivePool = maxPrize > 0 && pool > maxPrize ? maxPrize : pool` (the excess stays on the platform wallet for future rounds), then if `minPrize > 0 && effectivePool < minPrize` the round is **skipped**: no drand request, no winner selection, no payout — the round row is updated to `status: "skipped"`, `prizePoolLamports: effectivePool`, `eligibleCount`, `error` (human reason, e.g. "Prize pool 0.0200 SOL below minimum 0.1000 SOL — round skipped"), `completedAt`. `LotteryRoundResult.status` widened to `"completed" | "failed" | "skipped"`. All downstream uses switched from `pool` → `effectivePool` (winner sizing, payout guard, stored `prizePoolLamports`, log line). **No migration** — `LotteryRound.status` is `String`, so `"skipped"` works as-is. **Callers** — `scripts/runLottery.ts` now exits `0` unless `failed` (skipped = intentional no-op); `POST /api/admin/trigger-draw` logs skipped at `info` and returns HTTP 200. **UI** — `"skipped"` renders as a neutral gray chip (`color="default"`): `DrawsList.StatusChip` (added `Minus` icon + widened color union), admin `LastDrawBlock` + admin round cards, `/rounds/[n]` header, `/verify` header, `/stats` status breakdown. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors.
