- **Stats page (`/stats`) per-round charts reworked (2026-10-19).** Split the single "Prize pool per round" card into **two side-by-side cards** (`grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4`): (1) **Prize pool per round** (prize SOL), (2) **Holders per round** (eligible count). Charts are now a **30-day daily time-axis** (not one bar per draw): `windowStart = today-midnight - 29d`; `recent = completed.filter(completedAt >= windowStart)`; rounds aggregated into **30 daily buckets** (`byDay` Map keyed `YYYY-MM-DD`; prize summed per day, holders = max eligible that day); `series` = 30 buckets oldest→newest each `{key,label,prizeSol,eligible}` with `label = toLocaleDateString("en-US",{month:"short",day:"numeric"})` e.g. "Oct 1". Bars render only on days with a draw; empty days render `<div className="flex-1" />` spacers so the full 30-day span is visible. **Date axis**: a label row under the bars, one `text-[9px]` label every 6th bucket + the last (today) — replaced the old `#roundNumber` labels. `maxPrize`/`maxHolders` window-scoped; stat-tile "Biggest prize" still all-time `biggestPrize`. `recentCount` gates the peak label (hidden when no draws in window). `Bar` component prop changed `round:number` → **`label:string`** (tooltip now `${label}: ${format(value)}`); `format` prop retained (prize → `fmtSolHuman`, holders → `v.toLocaleString()`). Bar height `h-40` → **`h-28`**; columns `gap-2` → **`gap-px`** (30 thin columns). **Card padding aligned** (user feedback "belum selaras"): both chart cards + loading skeletons + the "Round status breakdown" card `p-6` → **`px-5 py-4`**, internal `gap-4` → `gap-3` — now matches draws/rounds convention page-wide (stats page no longer divergent; verify page still `p-6`). **Chart titles shrunk** (user feedback "keggedean"): `text-base` → **`text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight`** (other section headings like "Round status breakdown" stay `text-base`). Loading skeleton matched to 2-card grid + `px-5 py-4` + `h-4` title skeleton (parity). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors.
- **Round detail page (`/rounds/[n]`) typography aligned (2026-10-19).** Wrapper `pt-12 sm:pt-16 pb-16 gap-8` → `pt-8 sm:pt-10 pb-12 gap-6`; **header redone to match stats/holders/draws** — removed the "Draw" eyebrow, `<h1 text-3xl sm:text-4xl>` → `Typography type="h4"`, description → `Typography type="body-sm" color="muted" mt-1.5`, wrapped in `max-w-3xl` (status chip kept inline beside title). 6 section card headings `text-lg font-bold` → `text-base font-semibold tracking-tight` (Summary, Winners, Independent verification, Eligibility snapshot used, Proof material, drand chain). **Card padding aligned to draws page** (user feedback): all 7 cards (5 data + 2 loading skeletons) `p-6` → `px-5 py-4` — matches `DrawsList` round-card padding (`src/components/DrawsList.tsx:485`); loading↔data parity preserved. Compact `<dl>` `Field`/`Mono` metadata + `CheckRow` left unchanged (different role from big stat tiles). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors. **Winners section unwrapped (user feedback)**: removed the card wrapper + `Winners ({count})` heading + CSV download button — the `table-bordered` HeroUI `Table` now renders as a bare direct child of the page flex column (no card chrome, base indent 12 matching sibling cards). Now `<Table variant="secondary">` (flat root — "No background, padding, or rounding on the root" per `.table-root--secondary`; `variant` is a valid prop via `TableRootProps.variant?: TableVariants["variant"]`) wrapped in `<div className="rounded-lg border border-default-200 overflow-hidden bg-surface">`: `bg-surface` shows through the secondary variant's transparent body cells (white table body, matching the stats card), `overflow-hidden` clips the secondary header's rounded top-corners (`border-start-*-radius: min(32px, var(--radius-2xl))`) to the box's `rounded-lg` (8px), and HeroUI's own internal `.table__scroll-container { overflow-x-auto }` keeps horizontal scroll (no redundant inner scroll div). Loading skeleton wrapper got `bg-surface` added so both boxes match. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors; `/holders` → 200. - **Payout fee recording + manual pending-payout + readable SOL/token formatting (2026-10-18).** Four admin/system changes: (1) **Formatting** — new shared `fmtSolHuman(sol)` + `fmtTokenHuman(raw, decimals)` in `src/lib/format.ts` (trim trailing zeros + group thousands → "1.25 SOL" / "100,000", not "1.2500 SOL" / "100,000.0000"); replaced every `n.toFixed(4)+" SOL"` and local `fmtSol`/`fmtToken` across admin/live/stats/verify/winners/home/DrawsList/holders, and swapped `formatTokenAmount`→`fmtTokenHuman` in `/api/status` + `/api/holders` + `/api/eligibility` so balances read human at the source. (2) **Fee + tx recorded on the round** — `LotteryRound` gained `payoutTx String?` + `payoutFeeLamports BigInt?` (schema pushed to Neon, client regen'd via `db push`); `distributePayouts()` now returns `{ signature, feeLamports }`, deriving the fee from the payer balance delta (before − after − transferred), and `runLotteryRound` writes both onto the round at completion. (3) **Manual "Pay pending winners"** — new `payPendingWinners()` in `engine.ts` pays every `status:"pending"` winner across all rounds (each round in its own atomic tx, oldest-first, records `payoutTx`+`payoutFeeLamports` per round; all-or-nothing per round, skips with a reason if pool < owed); new `POST /api/admin/pay-pending` (isAdmin-guarded, logs via `logEvent`); admin `LiveStatusCard` shows the pending-winner count + a "Pay pending winners" button whose result message reports paid/total SOL/fee (or skipped/error) and triggers `onRefresh={refreshAll}`. (4) **Draw-running indicator** — `/api/admin/info` now returns `pendingWinnersCount` + `drawingRoundNumber`; `LiveStatusCard` renders a "● draw #N running…" chip when a round is in `"drawing"` status (the engine already sets that at round create). `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors. **Dev server was stopped to free the Prisma engine DLL lock for `db push` — restart `npm run dev` to view the changes.**
- **Platform revenue cut (PLATFORM_FEE_BPS) — 2026-10-18.** New admin-tunable `PLATFORM_FEE_BPS` (basis points 0–10000, default 0 = no cut; group "payout", applies to the next draw, no restart) in `settings.ts` (`SETTING_KEYS`/`SETTING_META`/`SETTING_DEFAULTS`/`validateSetting`) + `config.ts` (`platformFeeBps`, clamped 0–10000, override→env→0). `LotteryRound.platformFeeLamports BigInt?` records the cut taken per round (schema pushed to Neon, client regen'd). `computeWinnerAmounts(pool, winners, platformFeeBps)` now deducts `fee = pool*bps/10000` FIRST, splits the remaining `distributable` across winners (remainder still to rank 1), and returns `{ amounts, platformFeeLamports }` — the fee is NEVER transferred; it stays on the platform wallet (the funder) because winners are paid out of `distributable`, not `pool`. `runLotteryRound` records `platformFeeLamports` at finalize; `payPendingWinners` derives it per round from `prizePoolLamports − Σ winner.amountLamports` (so legacy pending rounds drawn before this feature record 0). `verify.ts` `reproduceWinners` only re-derives selection (rank+address), not amounts → provably-fair verify unaffected. `/api/rounds` projects `platformFeeLamports` (BigInt→string); `/api/admin/info` returns effective `platformFeeBps` + aggregated `totalPlatformFeeLamports` (sum across rounds). Admin: round card grid → `sm:grid-cols-5` + 5th "Platform fee" StatTile (SOL, 6 dp; hint is treasury-aware — "swept to treasury" when `info.treasuryWallet` set + fee>0, else "kept on platform wallet" for the legacy no-treasury path, else "no cut"); LiveStatusCard grid → `lg:grid-cols-5` + "Platform fee" StatTile showing effective % + "collected X SOL → treasury" hint (the `→ treasury` suffix only when a treasury is configured). **Note:** `payoutFeeLamports` (existing) = network tx fee paid BY the platform wallet TO validators; `platformFeeLamports` (new) = revenue cut KEPT ON the platform wallet — distinct. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors; `db push` synced Neon.

- **Payout tx on-chain failure detection (2026-10-18).** Fixed latent bug in `distributePayouts` (`engine.ts:246-275`): `confirmTransaction(sig, "confirmed")` only verifies block INCLUSION, not success — a tx that lands but FAILS (e.g. "Insufficient Funds For Rent" when sweeping a fee to an unfunded treasury) was still "confirmed", so the DB marked winners "sent" even though they were never paid. Now after confirmation, the code fetches the full tx via `connection.getTransaction(sig, {maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0})` with a 3×400ms retry loop (RPC indexing can lag behind confirmation), then checks `meta.err` — if non-null, throws `payout tx … failed on-chain: <err>`. The throw propagates to both callers' catch blocks: `runLotteryRound`'s `fail()` marks the round "failed" + winners stay "pending"; `payPendingWinners` returns partial results (earlier rounds paid) + `error`, failed round's winners stay "pending" for retry. JSDoc updated to document the throw contract. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors. **Also clarified (no code change):** the 0.0019→0.0018 "total paid out" gap is the 5% platform fee (96,621 lamports swept to treasury), NOT the `PRIZE_RESERVE_LAMPORTS` (0.001 SOL) — the reserve is already deducted BEFORE the prize pool is computed (`engine.ts:137` `pool = balance − reserve`), so the displayed pool is already net of reserve.

- **DrawsList pagination + drand info redesign (2026-10-18).** Two `/draws` page UX improvements in `DrawsList.tsx`: (1) **Load-more pagination** — added `visibleCount` state (default 5) + `visibleRounds = data.rounds.slice(0, visibleCount)`; the rounds list now renders only the first 5 rounds, with a HeroUI `<Button variant="outline">` "Load more (N remaining)" button below the list that increments `visibleCount` by 5 on press. The API (`/api/rounds`) still returns up to 50 rounds — this is purely a client-side display slice, no API change. (2) **Drand info section redesign** — replaced the bare "drand chain metadata" grid at the bottom of the draws page with a rich educational card titled "How randomness works": a header ("No one — not even us — can tamper with the draw"), followed by a 4-step `<ol>` walking a layperson through the provably-fair chain of trust — (1) drand.sh beacon = public BLS-signed randomness nobody controls, (2) beacon value becomes the seed stored permanently on each round, (3) SHA-256(seed) → Fisher–Yates shuffle of eligible holders → winners in rank order, (4) press Verify to re-run the whole thing in-browser via Web Crypto and compare rank-for-rank. Step badges use neutral `bg-default-100` rings (steps 1–3) + `bg-amber-100` gold ring (step 4 ✓). Chain parameters (name, scheme, period, hash, public key) moved into a bordered footer card with inline-code styling (`rounded-md bg-default-100 px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs break-all`) and descriptive labels. All tokens HeroUI-theme-aligned. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors.

- **[2026-10-18 session] DrawsList.tsx round-card UI refinement** — accent-themed randomness/beacon card (`bg-accent/10 border-accent/25`); `StatusChip` helper using HeroUI v3 slot API (`<Chip><Icon /><Chip.Label/></Chip>` with lucide `CircleCheck`/`CircleX`/`Clock`); `HashRow` helper (copy-to-clipboard `Copy`→`Check` 1.5s revert, `bg-surface` mono code blocks, single-line `truncate` + `title` tooltip for full hash, `—` fallback when empty); Verify button `variant="primary"` (HeroUI primary = `var(--accent)`/`var(--accent-foreground)`, accent-themed with working hover via `--accent-hover` from theme preset — no className override needed). Header reorg: `StatusChip` moved right via `className="ml-auto"`, `timeAgo` span removed from header. Stat grid: "Avg prize" cell → "Time" stat showing `timeAgo(completedAt ?? startedAt)` with `fmtTime` tooltip (avg prize dropped as redundant). **Removed redundant per-check spinners** in beacon-check + derive-check result rows — render conditions tightened from `!== "idle"` to `!== "idle" && !== "checking"`, so during verification the rows don't render at all (no empty gaps) and appear *progressively* (Chip + detail) as each check resolves. The single loading indicator now lives **inside** the Verify button: `{busy && <Spinner size="sm" color="current" />}` where `busy = beaconState === "checking" || deriveState === "checking"`. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npx eslint src/components/DrawsList.tsx` → 0 errors.
- **[2026-10-18 session] HeroUI v3 Spinner/Button gotchas (IMPORTANT for future verify/draws UI work)** — (1) HeroUI v3 `Button`'s `isPending` prop does **NOT** auto-render a spinner; it only disables the button, sets `[data-pending]`, and announces to screen reader. The spinner must be a **conditional child**: `{busy && <Spinner .../>}`. (2) `Button` has **no** `spinner` prop — `ButtonProps` only adds `className` + `isPending` on top of react-aria-components `Button`. Verified in `node_modules/@heroui/react/dist/components/button/button.d.ts` + `react-aria-components/dist/types/src/Button.d.ts`. (3) Default `Spinner` color is `"accent"` (blue) → **invisible on a blue/accent primary button**; must use `color="current"` → `color: inherit` → matches the button's foreground (white on primary). (4) `fmtTime` is a **file-local** helper (~line 52 of `DrawsList.tsx`), NOT imported from `@/lib/format` (which exports `shortAddress`, `explorerUrl`). (5) `Spinner` usage in DrawsList.tsx is now a single occurrence: line ~273 inside the Verify button (import on line 5 stays valid).

- **UI-copy pass — page 3 of 8: `/eligibility` rewritten (2026-10-22).** Applied the approved 25-item table across `src/app/eligibility/page.tsx` + `src/components/HoldersBrowser.tsx`. (1) **Header** — subtitle is now *"Hold at least the minimum PAY and you're in every draw automatically. Check your wallet below, or browse the full holder list."* (`≥` gone, "Pay" → **PAY**, "holders snapshot" → "holder list"). (2) **Form** — placeholder shortened to `Paste a Solana wallet address` (the 44-char demo address made the input look pre-filled), submit label `Check` → **`Check wallet`**, and a new `text-xs text-muted` caption under the input surfaces the read-from-last-snapshot delay: *"The holder list refreshes every few minutes, so a recent buy may not show up yet."* (3) **Errors** — the API's `{error:"Invalid Solana address format."}` (route.ts L42) is mapped to *"That doesn't look like a Solana wallet address — check for missing or extra characters."*, other non-OK responses become *"Couldn't check that wallet (status)"*, and a failed fetch/JSON parse becomes *"Couldn't reach the server — check your connection and try again."* instead of leaking `err.message`. (4) **Three result states** — eligible copy no longer promises money (*"Entered into every draw automatically — sit back and wait for a payout"* → *"Your balance clears the minimum, so you're in the pool for the next draw."*, accurate while the pool is 0 SOL and draws are `skipped`); short state reads *"You need <x> more PAY to qualify."*; the never-seen state is no longer an operator instruction (*"…sync holders and re-check"* → heading **"Not in the holder list"** + *"This wallet hasn't held PAY yet. The list refreshes every few minutes — check again after your first buy."*), and its chip reads **"Not in list"** (`"Not found"` before). (5) **PAY casing** — static labels use the literal `PAY`; dynamic ones use `const token = data.tokenName.toUpperCase()` (the API fallback was `"Pay"`, now `"PAY"` in `src/app/api/eligibility/route.ts` L29). Same pass in `layout.tsx` metadata (`"PAY Token Lottery"` / *"A verifiable holder lottery for the PAY token…"*); the `AppShell` wordmark stays `Pay` — brand mark, matching Home's own sidebar and the `Pay` breadcrumb — only the ticker is uppercased in copy. (6) **Dead-token cleanup (they rendered at `--foreground` weight, so balance and minimum looked identical)** — `text-default-foreground`, `text-default-400`, `text-default-500`, `border-default-200`, `bg-default-100`-as-track, `border-success-200`/`text-success-700`, `border-danger-200`/`bg-danger-50/50` all replaced with `text-foreground`/`text-muted`/`border-(--border)`/`bg-default/40`/`border-success/30`+`text-success`/`border-danger/30`+`bg-danger/10`. The form's `focus-within:border-default-400` (no such token → no focus ring) became `focus-within:border-accent`. (7) **Detail rows** — "Balance snapshot" + "Last holder sync" were two nearly identical timestamps (same batch); now a single **"Holder list"** row (`timeAgo(lastSyncAt)`) plus **"Balance from"** only when `capturedAt` exists and differs. `dbError` line reads *"The holder list couldn't be read — …"*. (8) **`HoldersBrowser` labels** — stat tiles `Total holders`→**Wallets** / `Threshold`→**Minimum** / `Eligibility rate`→**Eligible share**, captions `unique wallets`→"in the latest list", `min to qualify`→"to qualify", `of holders qualify`→"of all holders", `last sync`→"last refreshed"; skeleton labels updated to match (`Wallets`, `Minimum`, `Eligible share`) so the loading→loaded swap doesn't reflow text; table chip `Below` → **`Below min`**; empty state *"No wallets in the holder list yet."*; `bg-default-200` separator trick fixed to `border-(--border) bg-(--border)`. **Verified:** `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**; `GET /eligibility` → **200**, no error overlay; SSR HTML has 1× each of `Hold at least the minimum PAY`, `Paste a Solana wallet address`, `Check wallet`, `The holder list refreshes every few minutes`, `>Wallets<`, `>Eligible share<`, and **0×** `≥`, `Pay balance`, `Min eligible`, `Total holders`, `unique wallets`, `min to qualify`, `Eligibility rate`, `Wallet address, e.g.`, `full holders snapshot`; the data-driven strings (stat captions, `Below min`, empty state, result banners) can't be probed server-side (client component) so they were confirmed in the compiled dev chunks under `.next/dev/static/chunks` (`Below min`, `No wallets in the holder list yet`, `last refreshed`, `Toward the minimum`, `Not in the holder list`, `of all holders`); `.next/dev/static/css/app/layout.css` now emits `.bg-\(--border\)`, `.focus-within\:border-accent`, `.hover\:bg-default\/40`, `.bg-default\/40`, `.border-success\/30`, `.text-success`; `/api/eligibility?address=<eligible wallet>` returns `tokenName:"PAY"` with `capturedAt === lastSyncAt` → the single-row layout is what ships.

- **Payout broadcast hardened against silent drops (2026-10-22, after draws #36/#37).** Both manual draws succeeded but only on the retry: total round time was **43,5 s / 43,7 s** and the tx landed at second ~42 — the first attempt burned its whole blockhash window without ever landing. Measured on mainnet via `getBlockHeight` polling: **149 blocks = 31,1 s** (~0,21 s/block), i.e. the window is half what the old "~60 s" assumption implied, which is why the old `confirmTransaction` failure looked like a plain "block height exceeded" after ~30 s. Diagnosis (all verified against the live chain): (a) each winner was credited **exactly once** with the exact expected lamports, so nothing double-paid; (b) `getTransaction` for both `payoutTx` shows `err=null, fee=5000`; (c) the failing first send is not a funds/tx-shape problem — a standalone script sending the **same 3-recipient 313 B payout shape** (1 lamport each to round #37's winners) confirmed in **2,1 s**, and three consecutive 1-lamport self-transfers landed in **0,9–2,7 s**; (d) a priority-fee variant (50k µLamports, 10k CU) actually failed to land once, so a priority fee is **not** the fix and was not added. Fix in `distributePayouts` (`src/lib/lottery/engine.ts`): `confirmTransaction` is replaced by new `broadcastUntilLanded()` — it polls `getSignatureStatuses` every **500 ms**, checks `getBlockHeight("confirmed")` every **1 s** for window expiry, and **re-broadcasts the identical signed bytes** every **1 500 ms** (`sendRawTransaction(..., {skipPreflight:true, maxRetries:0})`, errors only logged) until the tx lands. Resending the same bytes reuses the signature, so a duplicate is impossible — this is the zero-risk remedy for "RPC accepted the bytes but no leader included them". `maxRetries: 5` stays on the initial send; `PAYOUT_SEND_ATTEMPTS = 2` outer retry (fresh blockhash) is unchanged and still gated by the presence check (`fetchLandedTx` → `getSignatureStatuses(searchTransactionHistory:true)`) before any resend. Settling a landed tx is now a single local `settle()` closure (verifies `meta.err`, derives the network fee from the payer balance delta) used by both the landed and the late-landed paths. New logs: `payout tx … landed after Xs (attempt n/2)`, `… was not included within its blockhash window (Xs, N re-broadcasts)`, a warn when `sendRawTransaction` itself takes >1 s, and `payout re-broadcast N … failed: …`. Validated with a mirror of the loop against the real RPC: real tx → `true` in 1,3 s; unknown signature → `false` at 32,6 s after 21 re-broadcasts. `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npm run build` → clean (build id `YM3Bcc0AfE3LaxzLbCyax`), and `.next/server/chunks/*.js` contains the new strings.
- **`/treasury` wallet addresses are no longer clickable (2026-10-22).** `WalletCard` (Platform + Fee wallet) renders the address as a plain selectable `<span>` (`select-all break-all font-mono text-xs`); the Solscan `<a>` now wraps **only** `<Icon name="arrow-up-right" />`, pushed to the right edge (`flex-1` + `shrink-0 -mr-1.5`, 20×20 hit area) with `title`, `aria-label`, `hover:text-accent` and a focus ring, so a mis-click on the address text no longer leaves the page. `PolicyCard`'s "Fee destination" row stays an inline `shortAddress` link. Confirmed in the built client chunk (`.next/static/chunks/app/treasury/page-*.js` → `select-all break-all`).

- **Homepage mobile pass (2026-10-22).** `src/app/page.tsx`: content gutter `px-8` → **`px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10`** (32 px gutters ate 64 px of a 360 px screen) and the header bar `px-6` → `px-4 sm:px-6`; hamburger 36 → **40 px**; breadcrumb wrapped in `min-w-0 flex-1`; drawer brand `min-w-0 truncate`. The mobile drawer is now a real panel: in-panel **X** (`lg:hidden`, 32 px), `Escape` closes it, `document.body` scroll is locked while open and restored on close/unmount (`useEffect` on `mobileOpen`), nav gets `overscroll-contain`. Hero: `p-5` → `p-4 sm:p-6`, section `pt-8 pb-6` → `pt-5 pb-5 sm:pt-10 sm:pb-6`, both CTAs stack full-width below `sm` (`flex-col` → `sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap`, `justify-center`). Status strip is `sm:grid-cols-2` with the pool tile `sm:col-span-2` (2×2 on tablet, 3-up at `lg`) instead of one stretched column; the three tiles `p-5` → `p-4 sm:p-6`. `RecentDraws.tsx`: pool column `w-32` → `w-28 sm:w-44`, winner address `min-w-0 truncate`, amount `shrink-0`. Small links (`View all`, `View all draws`, `View on drand`) got `-my-1 py-1` (16 → 24 px hit area, no layout shift); `DrandBeacon.HashRow` copy button `p-1` → `p-1.5` (21 → 25 px) with `pr-9` → `pr-10`. **Measured** with headless Chrome + CDP at 320/390/1024/1440: `document.scrollWidth === innerWidth` (no horizontal overflow), zero clipped text leaves with a homepage draw panel expanded, drawer opens at `x=0` with `body overflow: hidden` and closes back to `x=-240`. `tsc` 0 errors, `eslint src` 0 errors. **Known and deliberately not changed:** the absolutely-positioned desktop flow in `HowItWorksFlow.tsx` (rendered from `lg` = 1024 px) truncates its node titles between 1024–1400 px (`cw 46` at 1024, `cw 88` at 1280 vs ~110 px needed) — it only fits once the content column is ~1080 px. Raising its breakpoint (e.g. `min-[1400px]`) would let smaller laptops use the stacked flow.
- **`/stats` rebuilt: DB-side aggregates + one tabbed chart card (2026-10-23).** Every number on the page used to be derived from `/api/rounds`, which is capped at `take: 50` and filtered to completed/failed/skipped - with the hourly cron that is ~2 days of history, so "Draws", "Paid out", "Biggest prize" and the 30-day series silently stopped growing. New `src/app/api/stats/route.ts` (public, `force-dynamic`, no winners payload) returns: all-time `lotteryRound.aggregate` totals (count/sum/max/avg + `payoutFeeLamports`), `groupBy` status counts, `winner.groupBy` per wallet (unique winners, repeat wins, top-8 leaderboard + last round), a narrow 30-day timeline (status/pool/fee/eligible only, bucketed in **UTC** so server labels never drift from the browser), and per-day eligible history from `HolderSnapshot` via one raw SQL query that uses the day's MOST COMPLETE batch (a partial 5-row batch after a 444-row one would otherwise read as "95 -> 4 eligible" overnight; threshold from `effectiveMinEligibleBigint()`; returns null on failure so the page falls back to draw-time peaks). `src/app/stats/page.tsx` was rewritten around it: 4 tiles (Winners and Average prize dropped as arithmetic duplicates of the others; platform fees surface in the chart legend / stacked bar instead of a fifth tile, which left a gray hole in the tile frame at tablet width), **one** full-width chart card with 3 tabs (Prize paid / Eligible wallets / Outcomes) instead of two half-width cards with separate axes, a hover **tooltip card** with mono values and a draw-count chip instead of the old `title` attribute, an always-visible readout row of pills above the plot (so values are legible before the pointer touches a bar; removed again 2026-10-23 — see the next bullet), horizontal rules + baseline, a 4px outcome strip under the X axis (green completed / gray skipped / red failed / hairline = no draw; removed again 2026-10-23 — the red cells read as a stray red line), and the Most-won table with an "N unique wallets have won - M repeat wins" footer. **Two bugs found while verifying in a real browser:** (1) the y-scale used `Math.max(1, ...)` - a 1 SOL floor - so sub-SOL pools (all of ours, < 0.01 SOL) rendered as 3px stubs in an empty plot; it now scales to the window peak; (2) percentages inside the chart resolved against a wrapper that also contained the strip + axis, so hover dots/line points sat ~10% low; the plot now has its own fixed `h-44` box. The eligible area fill is suppressed below 5 points (with 3 snapshot days it degenerated into a tall sliver at the right edge) and short series get dots instead. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` -> 0 errors, `npx eslint src/app/stats/page.tsx src/app/api/stats/route.ts` -> 0 problems, plus headless-Chrome CDP runs (bar heights 25/176/23px after the scale fix; tooltips render on all 3 tabs; an empty day says "No draw finished" instead of "0.0000 SOL"). `npm run build` was NOT re-run - the dev server holds `.next/`.
- **`/stats` chart de-cluttered, eligible tab is now a filled area (2026-10-23).** Follow-up on the rebuild above. Removed the always-visible pill readout above the plot (the tooltip already carries date, draws and per-metric values — the row only pushed the plot down), dropped the `At draw time · peak/avg` row from the eligible tooltip (now Eligible + Holders only; a day with no snapshot says `no snapshot`), and deleted the 4px outcome strip under the X axis — its red cells were days with a failed draw and read as a stray red line under the chart (failures/skips stay visible in the Outcomes tab, the legend and each tooltip). Eligible wallets is a real **area** chart now: the accent gradient fill renders for every series (it was gated at ≥5 points, so the 3-day snapshot history showed a bare line + dots), with the polyline and 6px dots marking the actual snapshot points; the y-scale moved to a 0-based `y = 100 − v/max · 92` so the fill closes exactly on the baseline instead of 4% below it. Tooltip card narrowed to `w-44` and positioned from the hovered cell's `offsetLeft` (clamped to half-card + 8px), replacing the fixed 18–82% band that overflowed a 269px plot at 375px. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → **0 errors**, `npx eslint src/app/stats/page.tsx` → **0 problems**, CDP audit → **0** strip cells, **1** area path (`x 91.7–98.3`, `y 8→100`), tooltips `inside` the plot on all three tabs including empty days, eligible tooltip = Eligible/Holders only.

- **Read-path latency pass — in-process TTL cache + boot warm-up (2026-10-24).** Measured on the local production build (same machine, real Neon us-east-2 + Helius): a **single DB round-trip is ~240 ms**, the **first pooled Neon connection costs ~2.0–2.2 s**, and warm parallel queries settle at ~476 ms — so every extra query in a handler was felt directly. Before: `/api/status` **1.84 s warm**, `/api/stats` **2.31 s**, `/api/admin/info` **1.29 s**, `/api/admin/holders` **1.16 s**, `/api/admin/creator-fees` **0.75 s**, `/api/rounds` **0.46 s**, `/api/rounds/[n]` **0.95 s**, `/api/eligibility` **1.43 s**. After: every one of them answers in **2–10 ms** on a cache hit, first hit pays once. (1) New **`src/lib/cache.ts`** — `cached(key, ttl|{ttlMs,staleMs}, load)` with stale-while-revalidate + in-flight dedupe (`clearCache(prefix?)` for mutations). Applied to `status` (10 s), `rounds` (15 s), `stats` (30 s), `holders?page` (30 s), `eligibility?address` (30 s), `rounds/[n]` (30 s, immutable once drawn), `admin/info` (5 s), `admin/holders` (30 s), `admin/creator-fees` (20 s / 40 s stale — hides the pumpdev round-trip). `clearCache()` is called by trigger-draw, trigger-sync, creator-fee claim + auto toggle, exclusions add/remove and reset-data, so an admin action is never shadowed. (2) `/api/admin/info`'s **5 sequential Prisma queries → one `Promise.all`**. (3) `src/instrumentation.ts` now **pre-warms 4 Prisma connections** at boot (`[db] pool warm (≈3.8 s)`) instead of letting the first page load pay the TLS/auth handshake. (4) New **`src/lib/warm.ts`** self-fetches `/api/status`, `/api/rounds`, `/api/stats` 1.5 s after boot (production only, fire-and-forget, 4 retries) so the caches are already populated for the first visitor — boots log `[warm] /api/status 200 in 5.5 s`, `[warm] /api/rounds 200 in 973 ms`, `[warm] /api/stats 200 in 4.8 s`. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npm run build` clean, timed `curl` before/after plus a 12 s-gap re-request (still 4.5 ms → stale served while refreshing), `/`, `/draws`, `/admin`, `/api/rounds/1`, `/api/eligibility?address=…` all 200, no errors in the server log.

- **Mobile gutter united across pages (2026-10-25).** `/draws` (and 7 other AppShell pages) sat on `px-8` = **32 px** left/right on a 375 px screen while the home dashboard used `px-4` = **16 px**, so switching tabs visibly changed the column width — and the shared header was a third value (`px-6` = 24 px), not even aligned with its own content. Fix: every AppShell page container `mx-auto max-w-7xl px-8 lg:px-10` → **`px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10`** (draws, eligibility, holders, live, how-it-works, stats, treasury, verify, rounds/[n]) and the `AppShell` header row `px-6` → **`px-4 sm:px-6`**, so header and content share one gutter scale — the exact scale home already used (`px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-10`). Measured in headless Chrome (CDP `Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride`, computed `paddingLeft/Right` + card width): at **375 px** and **414 px** → **16 px** gutter, card **343 px / 382 px**; at **768 px** → **24 px**, card **705 px**; header padding identical on every route. So `/`, `/draws`, `/stats`, `/treasury` now report the same numbers. Desktop (`lg:px-10` = 40 px) untouched. Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` → 0 errors, `npm run build` clean, all 10 routes 200 (/holders and /live are intentional 308s to /eligibility and / — `next.config`), zero errors in the server log.

## How to resume (first steps on a new machine)
> Full copy/move checklist — robocopy command, every env key, safety warnings — is in **`HANDOFF.md`**. Short version:
1. Copy the source **without** `node_modules` (**±1.28 GB**, 76k files) and `.next` (**±522 MB**); the rest is **116 files / ±1.54 MB** (measured 24 Oct 2026). `npm ci` re-installs the exact locked versions (Node ≥ **20.9**; this machine runs 24.19 / npm 11.17).
2. `npm run db:generate`  (Prisma client — skip it and every query dies with "@prisma/client did not initialize yet")
3. Ensure `.env` exists — **SECRETS**: Solana RPC URL, `PLATFORM_WALLET_SECRET`, `PLATFORM_TREASURY_SECRET`, Neon `DATABASE_URL`, `ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `ADMIN_SESSION_SECRET`. Never commit, never paste into chat. The DB is **remote Neon**, so the data travels with the URL, not with the folder.
4. `npx tsc --noEmit` → expect **0** → `npm run dev` → http://localhost:3000
5. **Don't** run `db:push` / `db:migrate` against the existing Neon DB (it holds real rounds + holders; the additive `CreatorFeeClaim` table is already applied), and don't run `lottery:run` / `airdrop` / `holders:sync` unless you mean it — `LOTTERY_CRON_ENABLED=false` on purpose.
6. **Sesi terakhir (24 Okt 2026) ditutup bersih:** kartu **Creator Fees** di `/admin` → App Info sudah dirombak (strip 4 biaya + `Claim history` sebagai tabel, `getRecentClaims(8)`) dan lolos `tsc` 0 error, `eslint` 0 masalah, `npm run build` ✓ 12.7s, plus audit headless Chrome pada build produksi (6 baris, 420px tanpa overflow). Tidak ada pekerjaan yang menggantung. Yang perlu dicek pertama di laptop baru: `npx tsc --noEmit` → `npm run dev` → buka `/admin` (login) dan pastikan kartu Creator Fees tampil dengan tabel, bukan blok flex lama.
7. **Sesi 25 Okt 2026 juga ditutup bersih:** seluruh kerja landing page strip 3 tile sudah masuk ke `### ✅ Done` di atas (urutan tile, caption `text-xs` tanpa mono, `ProgressBar size="lg"` + row `h-10`, chip `≈ X SOL` pakai body font + `Tooltip` HeroUI). `npx tsc --noEmit` **0**, `GET /` **200**, audit CDP 1440 / 390 bersih (**253 / 236**), file `.tmp-cdp*.js` sudah dihapus dan Chrome headless dimatikan. Yang menggantung hanya hal opsional di `### ⏳ Pending / next` (footer `… eligible` → sans, tooltip serupa di teks cadence) — **keduanya sudah ditutup 26 Okt**, lihat bullet teratas di `### ✅ Done`.
8. **Sesi 26 Okt 2026 ditutup dengan `npx tsc --noEmit` 0 error di setiap langkah** (browser pass hanya dipakai pada dua perubahan strip; sisanya sengaja "tanpa verif browser" atas permintaan). ## Notes for the AI resuming this
- **All user-facing times are UTC — use the helpers, never bare `toLocale*`.** `fmtDateTimeUtc()` /
  `fmtDateUtc()` in `src/lib/format.ts` pin `timeZone: "UTC"` and append the `UTC` label; a
  `toLocaleString()` without `timeZone` silently shows the visitor's clock (that class of bug made
  `/treasury` read 15:32 WIB next to an 08:32 UTC API value). The **UI** is now TZ-independent, but
  **scheduling is not**: `node-cron` reads the server's TZ, so a VPS must be UTC (`timedatectl`) or the
  countdown will disagree with when draws actually fire.
- Always run `npx tsc --noEmit` after edits; fix all errors before declaring done.
- Respect HeroUI theme tokens; avoid raw dark Tailwind colors that break the light/dark toggle.
- Before touching the verify/draws UI, read `DrawsList.tsx`, `nav.ts`, `lib/lottery/verify.ts`.
- Use absolute paths in tool calls (the working dir on the new machine is different — this file was written on `C:\Users\gosdo\Desktop\AI\Pay`; **never** hard-code an old path such as `D:\Cline\Pay`).
- **Buyback data is a file, not a DB table.** `data/buyback.json` is the source of truth (read via `@/lib/buyback-store`, served by `/api/buyback`, edited in Admin → Buyback). It is not seeded by Prisma and not in `.env` — check it in with the repo. Never add a `buyback` model without migrating this file first.
- **Never delete/rebuild `.next` while a dev server is running (learned 2026-10-24).** Removing `.next` or running `npm run build` next to a live `next dev` corrupts that dev server's cache: `⨯ Error: Manifest file is empty`, `⨯ SyntaxError: Unexpected end of JSON input` on API routes, and the page does a **full reload every few seconds**. Nothing in the source is wrong — stop and restart `npm run dev`. If you need a compiled check during UI work, don't fight the dev server: `npx next start -p 3100` (leave :3000 alone, `next start` reads the existing `.next` build) and point the browser check at **:3100**.
- **UI verification recipe that actually works here (used 2026-10-24).** Headless Chrome + CDP: `chrome --headless=new --remote-debugging-port=9222 --user-data-dir=.tmp-chrome-profile`, read the real DOM over `Runtime.evaluate`, and stub admin endpoints with `Fetch.enable` + `Fetch.fulfillRequest` for `/api/admin/creator-fees` **and** `/api/admin/me` (that one intermittently 500s in dev, which bounces `/admin` to the login form — the usual cause of "card not found"). Authenticate with `POST /api/admin/login` from Node and push the `pay_admin_session` cookie via `Network.setCookie`. Always redirect output to a log file and `Get-Content` it; long `npx`/CDP commands piped straight to stdout often come back empty in this shell.
- **Admin page needs a real session.** `/admin` renders the login form when `/api/admin/me` fails, so any DOM assertion on the Creator Fees card must set the session cookie first — a 200 on `/admin` proves nothing.
- **Headless CDP needs focus emulation before testing hover/focus (learned 2026-10-25).** React Aria (`Tooltip`, `Menu`, `Popover`, any `useHover`/`useFocus` path) stays closed in a headless page because `document.hasFocus()` is `false`. Send `Emulation.setFocusEmulationEnabled { enabled: true }` right after connecting, then real `Input.dispatchMouseEvent` moves open tooltips normally; also check `matchMedia('(hover: hover)')` (true once focus emulation is on). Synthetic `dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerover'…))` from `Runtime.evaluate` is **not** enough — it silently does nothing, which looks exactly like a broken tooltip. Measure the tooltip itself via `document.querySelector('[role="tooltip"]')` + `getComputedStyle` (bg, font, radius, width).
- **`Tooltip.Trigger` is a `div`, so give it `flex` (learned 2026-10-25).** Hovering a `Chip` inside a bare `<Tooltip.Trigger>` block wrapper adds the wrapper's line-box to the header row: the prize-split tile grew **253 → 256** desktop / **236 → 240** mobile. `className="flex shrink-0"` makes the wrapper exactly as tall as the chip and the tile height returns to the measured baseline. Keep `role="presentation" tabIndex={-1}` when the trigger has no action (pattern already used in `RecentDraws.tsx`).
